Closed-loop evaluation and comprehensive ablations on nuPlan show that DFP achieves competitive performance while producing continuous, stable, and controllable motion plans in complex driving scenarios.

</details>


### [76] [A Spiking Neural Architecture for Coordinating Arm and Locomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11034)
*Lea Steffen,Kathryn Simone,Graeme Damberger,Travis DeWolf,Hudson Ly,Chris Eliasmith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个集成脉冲神经网络（SNN）控制器，结合神经工程框架（NEF）和语义指针架构（SPA），通过基底节模型协调仿人机器人双臂控制与双足运动，在Nengo和Isaac Sim联合仿真中验证了目标抓取、数字绘制、路径跟踪及行走与手臂控制切换等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN电机控制系统将双足运动和手臂控制分开处理，缺乏对两者集成控制的研究。

Method: 采用NEF和SPA构建脉冲架构，利用基底节模型实现高层行动选择，协调基于力的手臂控制与双足运动控制。

Result: 在Nengo与Isaac Sim联合仿真中成功实现了目标抓取、连续数字绘制、路径跟踪行走以及通过基底节去抑制机制实现行走与手臂控制之间的切换。

Conclusion: 这是首个在全身仿人平台上集成双足运动与手臂控制的全脉冲控制器，为未来部署于低功耗神经形态硬件奠定基础。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) coupled with neuromorphic hardware offer energy-efficient solutions for humanoid robot control. However, existing SNN-based motor control systems address bipedal locomotion and arm control in isolation, leaving integrated control of both unaddressed. We present a spiking architecture that coordinates force-based arm control and bipedal locomotion in a simulated humanoid, using the Neural Engineering Framework (NEF) and Semantic Pointer Architecture (SPA). High-level action selection between locomotor and arm control is mediated by a biologically grounded spiking basal ganglia model. We validate the system through co-simulation of Nengo, for the neural control, and Isaac Sim, demonstrating successful target reaching, continuous digit drawing, path-following locomotion, and finally, switching between walking and arm control via basal ganglia disinhibition. To our knowledge, this is the first integrated spiking controller to combine bipedal locomotion and arm control on a full-scale humanoid platform. The full spike-based implementation enables future deployment on low-power neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [77] [Generation of Diverse and Functional Robot Designs using Superquadrics Parametrisation and Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11037)
*Leni Le Goff,Simon Smith,Emma Hart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于超二次曲面（SQs）的机器人形态表示方法，结合质量多样性算法MAP-Elites，在机器人生成设计中实现了更高的设计多样性与功能性，优于传统进化算法和CPPN表示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化算法在机器人生成设计中容易过早收敛到少量次优设计，无法维持种群多样性以发现功能各异的机器人形态。

Method: 采用超二次曲面（SQs）作为机器人身体的数学表示，SQs具有可解释、紧凑、计算高效的特点；将该表示与质量多样性算法（MAP-Elites）相结合以促进形态多样性。

Result: 在两种测试环境中，SQs结合MAP-Elites算法取得了最高的质量多样性分数，最大化了生成机器人的设计多样性和功能多样性。

Conclusion: 紧凑且可解释的几何表示有助于探索复杂设计空间，将SQs与显式多样性机制结合能够提升所生成设计的质量和数量。

Abstract: Generative design of robots requires navigating a vast search-space, encompassing physical configurations and behavioural parameters. Evolutionary Algorithms (EAs) have shown promising results, but often converge prematurely to a small set of sub-optimal designs. Most EAs fail to maintain sufficient diversity in the population that would allow the discovery of distinct functional robots. To counter premature convergence, we introduce a superquadrics-based representation (SQs) for robot bodies. SQs are interpretable, compact and computationally efficient mathematical representations of 3D geometrical shapes that can be tuned to specific design-spaces. To encourage morphological diversity, we combine this representation with a quality-diversity (QD) algorithm (MAP-Elites). We compare SQs and Compositional Pattern Producing Networks representations as generators of morphologies, combining them with standard EAs and MAP-Elites. In two test environments, we find that using SQs to generate morphology in conjunction with the MAP-Elites algorithm reaches the highest QD-score across both environments, maximising diversity of design and functionality of generated robots. The findings highlight the benefits of using a compact and interpretable geometric representation for exploring a complex design-space and suggest that combining SQs with an explicit diversity mechanism increases the quality and number of designs generated.

</details>


### [78] [A Distributed Multi-UGV Exploration Framework With Loop-Aware Planning and Descriptor-Aided Localization in Resource-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11088)
*Zhiwei Li,Haiou Liu,Xijun Zhao,Ji Li,Yingze Wang,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多无人地面车辆（UGV）在未知、GPS受限、带宽有限环境下的完全分布式协同探索框架，通过描述子辅助的UGV间闭环检测与闭环感知的分层规划，实现自主定位与高效探索。实验表明，该框架在闭环识别、通信开销和探索效率方面均显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多UGV在无先验地图、GPS受限、带宽有限环境中协同探索时，因定位漂移导致地图不一致和重复覆盖的问题。

Method: (1) 轻量级LiDAR全局描述子（含距离图像预对齐）实现跨UGV鲁棒地点识别；(2) 不确定性感知的跨UGV闭环选择模块，对候选闭环评分并保留高实用性闭环作为规划锚点；(3) 闭环感知的分层规划框架，结合全局任务分配与局部路径优化；(4) 闭环验证维护全局一致轨迹与稀疏拓扑表示。

Result: 闭环模块AR@1达89.9%，AR@1%达95.5%；分布式优化降低了绝对轨迹误差；系统大幅减少双向通信量；整体框架相比mTSP基线减少探索时间15%、行驶距离14%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的描述子辅助闭环检测与闭环感知规划相结合的分布式框架，有效提升了多UGV在退化环境中的协同探索鲁棒性与效率，为无GPS、低带宽场景下的自主探索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robust and efficient cooperative exploration with multiple unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) in unknown, GPSdenied, and bandwidth-limited environments without prior maps remains challenging, as localization drift degrades map consistency and induces redundant coverage. This paper presents a fully distributed exploration framework that couples descriptoraided inter-UGV loop closure with loop-aware hierarchical planning while enabling autonomous localization and exploration. We develop a lightweight LiDAR global descriptor with range-image prealignment to enable robust cross-UGV place recognition under large yaw and lateral variations, and use verified loop closures to maintain globally consistent trajectories and a sparse topological representation. We further introduce an uncertainty-aware crossUGV loop-closure selection module that scores candidate loop closures under pose uncertainty and retains high-utility loop closures as planning anchors for global task allocation and local route refinement. Simulations and real-UGV experiments show that the loop-closure module achieves AR@1/AR@1% of 89.9%/95.5%, distributed optimization reduces absolute trajectory error, the system substantially reduces two-way communication volume, and the overall framework reduces exploration time and travel distance by 15% and 14%, respectively, compared with an mTSP baseline.

</details>


### [79] [RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092)
*Yichao Zhong,Yidan Lu,Yuhang Lu,Tianyang Tang,Haoguang Mai,Yixuan Pan,Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhongyu Li,Peng Lu,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboNaldo，一个三阶段运动引导课程强化学习框架，使仿人机器人能够完成高冲击力的足球射门动作。该方法从单人踢球参考动作出发，逐步优化射门表现，在仿真和真实场景中均显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动跟踪的强化学习方法虽能提供全身稳定性，但固定参考难以适应变化的球位置和击球时机；而纯任务奖励驱动的方法则难以从零开始探索有效的踢球动作。因此需要一种既能利用运动先验又能灵活适应不同场景的方法。

Method: RoboNaldo采用三阶段课程学习框架：第一阶段学习稳定的全身踢球先验（使用单人踢球参考）；第二阶段将踢球动作适应到固定球位于随机位置的任意球场景；第三阶段通过运动指令与踢球触发接口扩展到移动球射门。训练时高级启发式规划器控制该接口，推理时可由其他高级控制器驱动同一低级策略。

Result: 仿真中任意球射门误差降低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。在真实Unitree G1机器人上，3米距离任意球和移动球射门平均误差分别为0.73米和0.86米，触球后球速达13.10 m/s，达到职业球员开放比赛射门速度的59-71%。

Conclusion: RoboNaldo通过运动引导的课程强化学习，有效解决了仿人机器人高冲击力足球射门中稳定性与适应性的权衡问题，实现了从仿真到真实世界的成功迁移，并取得了显著优于基线方法的表现。

Abstract: Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: $\href{https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}{\text{opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo}}$.

</details>


### [80] [EM-Fall: Embodied mmWave Sensing for Day-and-Night Fall Detection on Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11109)
*Yanshuo Lu,Yuxuan Hu,Shenghai Yuan,Xinyu Zhou,Kuangji Zuo,Bofan Lyu,XiChen Yuan,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EM-Fall是一个部署在移动人形机器人上的具身化跌倒检测框架，通过集成毫米波感知与机器人移动性，实现主动视角调整和跨房间目标追踪，在复杂住宅环境中保持了鲁棒的跌倒检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跌倒检测系统依赖可穿戴设备或固定传感器，存在用户依从性低、空间覆盖有限、遮挡和光照不足下降级等问题，亟需一种更灵活、鲁棒的跌倒检测方案。

Method: 将毫米波感知部署在移动人形机器人上，实现主动调整感知视角；设计以人为中心的感知流水线结合轻量级时序建模，捕捉跌倒事件前后的运动演化过程，并处理宠物运动和多径干扰。

Result: 在8个真实室内环境、4名参与者上评估，具身移动感知范式提高了监测连续性，在多样化环境条件下保持了鲁棒的跌倒检测性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为家庭环境中机器人辅助的安全监测提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Falls are one of the leading causes of injury and hospitalization among elderly individuals, making reliable fall awareness an essential capability for safety monitoring in residential environments. However, existing fall detection systems often rely on wearable devices or fixed sensing installations, which may suffer from low user compliance, limited spatial coverage, or degraded performance under occlusion and poor lighting conditions. In this work, we propose \textbf{EM-Fall}, an embodied fall detection framework deployed on a mobile humanoid robot. The system integrates millimeter-wave (mmWave) sensing with robotic mobility, allowing the robot to actively adjust its sensing viewpoint and maintain target observability across rooms and under occlusion. To address interference in complex residential environments, including pet motion and multipath artifacts, we design a human-centered perception pipeline combined with lightweight temporal modeling to capture motion evolution before, during, and after fall events. We evaluate the proposed system across eight real indoor environments with four participants and construct an in-home mmWave fall detection dataset. Experimental results show that the embodied mobile sensing paradigm improves monitoring continuity and maintains robust fall detection performance under diverse environmental conditions. The proposed framework provides a practical solution for robot-assisted safety monitoring in home environments.

</details>


### [81] [JOIN: Anchor-Grasp-Conditioned Joining via Opposition, Inference, and Navigation for Bimanual Assistive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11151)
*Drake Moore,Matt Cheng,Xiang Zhi Tan,Taşkın Padır*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对轮椅单臂系统无法完成双手操作任务的问题，本文提出异构按需双手协作系统JOIN：轮椅固定锚臂+可召唤移动补臂；通过VLM+几何工具的三阶段分解（规划-驱动-抓取），在20次尝试中成功19次，优于基线方法的14次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮椅辅助平台多为单臂系统，无法完成开罐、倒水、端托盘、备餐等大量日常双手任务；而直接为轮椅加装第二条臂不切实际（功耗、成本、占用转移空间）。因此需要一种异构、按需的双手协作方案。

Method: 提出JOIN系统，包含：(i) 以锚臂已抓取为条件，补臂自主选择站位和抓取点的"双手连接"问题，分解为规划-驱动-抓取三阶段；(ii) 使用VLM提供任务级语义知识，结合标准几何工具实现决策；(iii) 提出轮椅参考系下的对抗评分（opposition score）和任务条件方向可操作度（task-conditioned directional manipulability）。

Result: 在Kinova Gen3（锚臂）与Hello Robot Stretch 3（补臂）上评估同对象和异对象双手任务：JOIN完成19/20尝试，SOTA方法仅14/20，且所需操作员校正显著更少。

Conclusion: VLM结合几何工具可有效解决异构移动双臂的按需协作问题，为轮椅辅助系统扩展双手操作能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Assistive mobility and manipulation platforms have received increasing attention as a means of restoring independence to individuals with disabilities. While effective for many basic activities of daily living (ADLs), a significant percentage of everyday tasks such as opening a jar, pouring a liquid, lifting a tray, or basic meal preparation, is fundamentally bimanual and remains out of reach for any single-arm system. Adding a second arm to a wheelchair is impractical, due to the additional power draw, cost, and the loss of space required for transfers and mobility. We instead propose a heterogeneous, on-demand bimanual system, in which a wheelchair-mounted anchor arm is joined when needed by a summoned mobile manipulator that serves as a complement arm. The central technical problem, which we call bimanual joining, is conditional: the anchor has already committed to a grasp, and the complement arm must choose where to stand and what to grasp to complete the task. We formulate bimanual joining as a three-phase decomposition (plan, drive, grasp) and show that a vision-language model (VLM), coupled with standard geometric tools, provides task-level knowledge sufficient to solve a representative class of bimanual ADLs. Our system JOIN, contributes (i) a wheelchair-referenced opposition score, and (ii) task-conditioned directional manipulability. We evaluate JOIN on a Kinova Gen3 anchor and a Hello Robot Stretch~3 complement on representative same-object and different-object tasks. JOIN accomplished more attempts (19/20) than state-of-the-art methods (14/20) and required markedly less correction by the operator.

</details>


### [82] [TacForeSight: Force-Guided Tactile World Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11184)
*Yujie Zang,Yuhang Zheng,Xian Nie,Yupeng Zheng,Shuai Tian,Songen Gu,Chen Gao,Zining Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TacForeSight，一个轻量级的力条件触觉前向感知框架，通过TacForceWM触觉世界模型预测短时域触觉潜在动态，并结合预测性触觉条件策略实现实时操作中的主动接触推理，在五个机器人任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法虽融合触觉或力反馈提升接触感知控制，但缺乏对全局力觉与局部触觉在时空上不对称角色的建模。

Method: 提出TacForeSight框架，包含TacForceWM（以高频腕部力/力矩信号为条件，预测双指触觉潜在动态的世界模型）和预测性触觉条件策略（利用预测的潜在变量作为接触先验，通过交叉注意力建模当前到未来的触觉演化，并采用触觉引导门控模块自适应融合视觉-触觉特征）。

Result: 在五个代表性任务和三种过程扰动设置下的真实机器人实验中，TacForeSight持续优于现有基线，尤其在动态接触扰动场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过在紧凑潜在空间内进行预测，TacForeSight实现了适用于高频操控控制的主动接触推理和高效实时推理。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to continuously perceive and regulate evolving physical interactions under dynamic contact transitions or complex surface geometries. Recent imitation learning methods improve contact-aware control by incorporating tactile or force feedback, but they rarely model the asymmetric spatiotemporal roles of global force and local tactile sensing. To address this, we propose TacForeSight, a lightweight force-conditioned tactile foresight framework for real-time manipulation. The core component is TacForceWM, a tactile world model that predicts short-horizon tactile latent dynamics from dual-finger tactile observations conditioned on high-frequency wrist force and torque signals. Another key component, the Predictive Tactile-Conditioned Policy, leverages the predicted latents as anticipatory contact priors, models the current-to-future tactile evolution via cross-attention, and adaptively fuses visuo-tactile features through a tactile-guided gating module. By forecasting purely within a compact latent space, TacForeSight enables proactive contact reasoning with efficient real-time inference suitable for high-frequency manipulation control. Real-robot experiments on five representative tasks and three in-process perturbation settings show that TacForeSight consistently outperforms existing baselines, particularly under dynamic contact disturbances. All models and datasets will be made publicly available on the project website at https://tacforesight.github.io/ProjectPage.

</details>

### [83] [An algorithm for dynamical quantum optimal transport with applications to quantum chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10075)
*Genevieve Dusson,Virginie Ehrlacher,Etienne Obermeyer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对量子最优传输（QOT）中的Benamou-Brenier动力学形式化距离族进行了数值研究，提出了内点正则化方法计算正半定矩阵间的测地线，并展示了其在量子化学中的近似应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子最优传输作为经典最优传输在密度矩阵空间上的推广，已有多种数学形式化方法，但对其数值性质（特别是基于Benamou-Brenier动力学形式化的距离族）的系统性数值研究尚不充分，亟需探索其在实际计算中的表现及在量子化学等领域的应用价值。

Method: 提出了一种内点正则化方法用于计算正半定矩阵之间的测地线；将结果通过积分核和密度进行可视化；通过参数调优使动力学QOT逼近量子化学中的特定问题。

Result: 动力学QOT在适当参数调优下能够较好地近似量子化学中的某些问题；研究了所提距离的数值性质以及随矩阵规模增大时对象的收敛性。

Conclusion: 动力学量子最优传输距离族具有良好的数值性质，在量子化学领域具有应用潜力，且随着系统规模增大表现出收敛性。

Abstract: Quantum optimal transport (QOT) is a rapidly developing field. Among the many formulations of this adaptation of classical optimal transport (OT) to spaces of density matrices, we numerically study a family of distances based on a dynamical formulation inspired by the Benamou-Brenier OT formulation. We introduce an interior-point regularized method to compute geodesics between positive semidefinite matrices and visualize the results in terms of integral kernels and densities, inspired by quantum chemistry applications. We show that dynamical QOT may provide a good approximation to certain problems in quantum chemistry with appropriate parameter tuning. We also study the numerical properties of the distances at hand, and the convergence of the objects when the size of the matrices increases.

</details>


### [84] [Branch and Price for Railway Crew Scheduling: Benchmark Instances and Computational Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10081)
*Bart van Rossum*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个先进的铁路乘务调度分支定价算法，并创建了包含75个基准实例的公开库（任务数450-3016），实验表明平均最优性缺口低于1%，其中原始启发式算法贡献最大，而分支和简化成本固定对缩小差距贡献有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路乘务调度是运营商面临的关键规划问题，虽然列生成是主流求解方法，但精确的分支定价方法研究不足，且缺乏公开的基准测试实例。

Method: 开发了先进的分支定价算法，系统研究了分支规则、原始启发式和简化成本固定等组件；通过模拟完整铁路规划流程生成了75个基准实例，并经荷兰铁路数据验证。

Result: 所有规模实例的平均最优性缺口远低于1%；原始启发式是缩小差距的主要驱动力，而分支和简化成本固定贡献很小。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持了基于列生成的原始启发式算法的流行性，同时表明改进对偶边界仍是铁路乘务调度精确方法面临的主要挑战。

Abstract: Railway crew scheduling consists of assigning a set of tasks to crew members in the form of feasible duties so as to minimise costs, and is one of the key planning problems faced by railway operators. While column generation is the dominant solution method, literature on exact branch-and-price approaches is scarce. Moreover, few publicly available benchmark instances exist. We present a state-of-the-art branch-and-price algorithm and conduct a systematic computational study of its components, including branching rules, primal heuristics, and reduced cost fixing techniques. To this end, we introduce a novel library of 75 benchmark instances, ranging in size from 450 to 3,016 tasks. The instances are generated by mimicking the full railway planning process, validated against data from Netherlands Railways, and publicly available with best known solutions and a solution checker. We report average optimality gaps well below 1% across all instance sizes, driven primarily by the strong performance of primal heuristics, whereas branching and reduced cost fixing contribute little to closing the gap. Our findings provide support for the popularity of column generation-based primal heuristics, and show that improving the dual bound remains the main challenge for exact methods in railway crew scheduling.

</details>


### [85] [The Dual Averaging Power-Prox Method with Application to Heavy-Tail Incremental Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10110)
*Yuan Gao,Jeremy Rack,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限和复合优化在增量梯度访问和重尾梯度噪声下的收敛性，提出了增量梯度的对偶平均幂逼近方法（Dual Averaging Power-Prox），并首次建立了该设置下的收敛分析，证明了该方法相较于传统SGD具有更优的渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGD理论通常假设独立同分布采样和轻尾梯度噪声，而实际机器学习训练中常采用固定循环的增量梯度访问（如epoch-wise遍历）且梯度噪声常呈现重尾分布（q阶矩有界，q∈(1,2]），缺乏对此类实际设置的理论理解。

Method: 提出了增量梯度下的对偶平均幂逼近方法（Dual Averaging Power-Prox），该方法结合了对偶平均和幂逼近技术，适用于固定循环的增量梯度访问模式。

Result: 首次建立了增量梯度访问和重尾梯度噪声设置下的收敛分析，并证明所提方法在渐近收敛率上优于采用有放回采样的传统SGD方法。

Conclusion: 本文填补了增量梯度与重尾噪声这一实际重要设置的理论空白，所提出的Dual Averaging Power-Prox方法不仅在理论上可行，而且比传统SGD具有更好的渐近性能。

Abstract: We study finite-sum composite optimization under two departures from classical stochastic gradient descent theory that are central in practice: incremental gradient access and heavy-tailed gradient noise. Specifically, we consider fixed cyclic passes over component gradients and assume that, at the optimum, component gradients have a bounded $q$-th centralized moment for some $q\in(1,2]$. This setting is much closer to modern ML training practice than the assumptions used in classical SGD theory, yet its theoretical understanding remains limited. We propose a Dual Averaging Power-Prox method for incremental gradients and establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first convergence analysis in this regime. We further show that our method achieves a better asymptotic convergence rate than the corresponding SGD method with i.i.d. (with-replacement) sampling.

</details>


### [86] [Bidirectional SDDP with dimension-free complexity for solving strongly convex stochastic dynamic programming equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10161)
*Vincent Guigues,Pablo Barros*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了双向随机对偶动态规划（BSDDP）算法在强凸多阶段随机优化问题中的复杂度，建立了与状态空间维度无关的显式期望迭代次数上界，并在中等阶段数和大状态维度场景下优于传统SDDP算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDDP算法的复杂度分析未充分利用强凸性带来的加速优势，且复杂度上界依赖于状态空间维度，限制了算法在高维问题中的应用效率。

Method: 在标准正则性假设下（无折扣因子），利用强凸性常数和Lipschitz常数推导BSDDP算法达到ε-最优策略所需的期望迭代次数显式上界。

Result: 得到的复杂度上界与状态空间维度无关，仅依赖于阶段代价函数的强凸性常数和Lipschitz常数；与Lan (2020)的结果相比，在阶段数适中且状态维度大的场景下复杂度更优；且复杂度随强凸性增强而降低。

Conclusion: BSDDP算法在强凸多阶段随机优化中具有维度无关的复杂度优势，强凸性越强算法效率越高，特别适用于中等阶段数、高维状态空间的结构化问题。

Abstract: We analyze the complexity of Bidirectional Stochastic Dual Dynamic Programming (BSDDP) algorithm applied to multistage stochastic optimization problems with strongly convex cost functions. Under standard regularity assumptions and without discount factor, we establish an explicit complexity bound on the expected number of iterations needed to obtain an epsilon-optimal policy. Our bound depends on the strong convexity constants and Lipschitz constants of the stagewise cost functions but is independent of the dimensionality of the state space. This improves upon the complexity of SDDP for convex problems from (Lan, 2020), particularly when the number of stages is moderate and the state dimension is large. The analysis further reveals that the complexity decreases as the strong convexity of the cost functions increases, highlighting the algorithm efficiency in structured problem instances.

</details>


### [87] [Submodular Optimization with Applications to Decision and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10192)
*Shamak Dutta,Bahman Gharesifard,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了子模优化在系统与控制领域中的理论、算法与应用，涵盖从基本性质（曲率、子模率）到各类约束族（拟阵、背包、p-系统），再到单调与非单调最大化问题的近似算法及最新比率与下界。应用领域包括传感器调度、多智能体协调、鲁棒优化、领导者-跟随者系统、分布式优化、博弈论、资源分配、社交网络及信息路径规划。特别指出部分控制论目标函数（如可控性Gramian的对数行列式和秩、卡尔曼滤波信息矩阵的对数行列式）具有子模性，而稳态卡尔曼误差协方差和逆Gramian平均控制能量则不具备。最后展望了开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模集函数因其边际收益递减特性，为决策与控制中的子集选择问题提供了统一的组合优化框架。尽管精确最大化是NP难的，但子模函数的结构特性使得简单的贪心算法能实现常数因子近似保证，这对于大规模系统与控制问题具有重要的理论与实际意义。本文旨在系统梳理该领域的研究进展，特别是面向控制系统的应用。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法，系统回顾了子模优化的基础理论（边际收益递减、曲率、子模率等）、约束族（拟阵、背包、p-系统）、主要近似算法（贪心及其随机化变体）及其近似比与紧界，并分类总结了在系统与控制多个子领域中的典型应用。

Result: 明确了可控性Gramian的对数行列式和秩、卡尔曼滤波信息矩阵的对数行列式是子模函数，而稳态卡尔曼滤波误差协方差和逆Gramian的平均控制能量既不满足子模性也不满足超模性。同时总结了各类约束下单调与非单调子模最大化的最新近似比与不可近似性结果。

Conclusion: 子模优化为系统与控制中的组合选择问题提供了理论上有保证且实际可实现的贪心算法框架；部分经典控制目标具有子模性可直接应用现有算法，而另一些目标则不具备，需要开发新的分析工具；跨领域的开放性问题值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Submodular set functions, characterized by the diminishing-returns property, provide a unifying combinatorial framework for many subset-selection problems in decision and control. Although exact maximization is NP-hard in general, the structural properties of submodular functions enable simple greedy algorithms that achieve constant-factor approximation guarantees for monotone objectives, with randomized greedy-based variants extending such guarantees to the non-monotone case. This survey reviews the theory, algorithms, and applications of submodular optimization with a focus on systems and control. We cover the structural properties of submodular functions, including curvature and the submodularity ratio, the constraint families that arise in practice (matroids, knapsack, and $p$-systems), and the main approximation algorithms for monotone and non-monotone submodular maximization, with up-to-date approximation ratios and hardness results. We then survey applications across sensor scheduling, multi-agent coordination, robust submodular optimization, leader-follower systems, distributed submodular optimization, game theory, system theory, resource allocation, social networks, and informative path planning. The survey emphasizes practically implementable greedy-based algorithms and instance-dependent refinements via curvature and the submodularity ratio. We close with observations on canonical control-theoretic objectives: certain functionals are submodular (the log-determinant and rank of the controllability Gramian, and the log-determinant of the Kalman filter information matrix), whereas closely related objectives fail to be sub- or supermodular (the steady-state Kalman filter error covariance, and the average control energy obtained from the inverse Gramian). We also highlight the cross-cutting open directions that follow.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DDP复杂度结果依赖于状态向量的维度，且随维度呈指数增长。本文旨在建立与维度无关的复杂度界，突破维度诅咒的限制。

Method: 首先为无约束强凸问题设计了灵活的DDP框架（FDDP），通过平滑和惩罚技术将原线性约束问题转化为无约束强凸问题，然后应用FDDP求解。FDDP借鉴束方法的思想，通过通用过程更新代价函数的低价近似，包含经典多切DDP和新的两切DDP两种变体。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了一种维度无关的DDP复杂度理论，提出的两切DDP在保证理论性能的同时显著降低了内存开销，为大规模凸优化问题的求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper studies the complexity of a dual dynamic programming (DDP) method for solving a class of convex optimization problems with linear coupling constraints. Existing complexity results based on DDP depend on the dimensions of the state vectors and, in particular, grow exponentially with dimension. The goal of this paper is to establish a complexity bound that is independent of the dimension. Our approach first studies an unconstrained strongly convex problem and develops a flexible framework for DDP, called FDDP, for solving the associated dynamic programming equations, and establishes an iteration-complexity bound for it that is independent of the dimension. A dimension-independent complexity bound for the original linearly constrained problem is then obtained by applying FDDP to a corresponding unconstrained strongly convex problem obtained via smoothing and penalization. Inspired by the literature on bundle methods, FDDP updates lower approximations of cost-to-go functions through a generic procedure that includes both the classical multi-cut DDP and a new two-cut DDP variant as special cases. The two-cut variant maintains only two affine cuts per stage at each iteration, making it more memory-efficient while retaining the same theoretical guarantees. Finally, numerical experiments illustrate the practical behavior of multi-cut and two-cut DDP, including their dependence on problem parameters and their performance relative to a direct quadratic constraint reformulation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman建模在非线性系统中广受欢迎，但存在输入变量时，即使是在无限维空间中也无法得到完全线性时不变模型，这使得基于Koopman的控制 largely 仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 将非线性离散时间系统分别将状态变量和输入变量提升到各自的再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中，证明其可精确表示为双线性动力学。采用线性-径向乘积核定义RKHS，使函数由分量函数与Sobolev函数的乘积张成。镇定问题被表述为关于输入空间上状态依赖条件概率测度的无限维优化问题，通过离散化方案求解。

Result: 满足适当光滑性和正则性条件的非线性离散时间系统可精确表示为双线性形式；基于核平方和形式的Lyapunov函数可构造反馈律实现镇定。

Conclusion: 该方法为Koopman框架下的非线性控制提供了新的理论路径，通过双线性化避免了传统Koopman线性化在输入存在时的根本性困难。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of Koopman modeling for nonlinear systems, in the presence of input variables, the evident nonexistence of a fully linear time-invariant model even in infinite dimensions makes Koopman-based control largely an open problem to date. Focusing on discrete-time systems in this paper, which eschews from using operator semigroup and infinitesimal generator notions, it is proven that nonlinear systems, if satisfying appropriate smoothness and regularity conditions, can be expressed exactly as bilinear dynamics, when the state variables and input variables are separately lifted into their reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs). To account for the knowledge of an equilibrium point at the origin, the RKHS is defined by a linear--radial product kernel, and hence the functions belonging to this RKHS are spanned by the multiplications of component functions and Sobolev functions. The stabilization problem, namely the determination of a feedback law that causes a Lyapunov function (expressed as a kernel sum-of-squares form) to decrease, is then posed as an infinite-dimensional optimization problem over state-dependent conditional probability measures over the input space, solved via a discretization scheme.

</details>

Result: 得到了在一般网络拓扑下，子模性与超模性成立的充要条件，这些条件仅取决于攻击位置。利用额外网络信息（如详细参数和特殊拓扑）可建立更宽松的条件。在串并联网络中，为最短路径和最大流干扰问题推导了充要条件。对于含防御者二元决策的复杂场景，识别出保持子模性或恢复超模性的条件。数值实验表明，利用这些性质可带来数量级的计算加速。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地揭示了网络干扰问题中防御者目标函数的子模/超模性质及其条件，为高效求解大规模双层网络干扰问题提供了理论依据和实用方法，通过生成有效不等式可显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: We study a bilevel network interdiction problem, with an attacker interdicting (attacking) certain components of a network and a defender optimizing operations over the ensuing network. We study when the defender's optimal objective is submodular or supermodular with respect to the attacker's interdiction decisions, for optimizing the bilevel integer program more efficiently. We first consider the min-cost flow (MinCF) interdiction problem and derive necessary and sufficient conditions for the supermodularity or submodularity to hold under three types of attacks, respectively on supplies/demands, flow capacities, and cost coefficients. We extend to other variants, including capacitated facility location, maximum flow (MaxF), and shortest path (SP) interdiction. The conditions hold under general network topologies and parameter settings, and depend solely on the locations of the attacks. We further incorporate additional network information (e.g., detailed parameters and special topologies) to establish less restrictive conditions. We also derive necessary and sufficient conditions for supermodularity or submodularity in SP and MaxF interdiction in series-parallel networks. Furthermore, we explore more challenging interdiction problems where the defender may make additional binary decisions (e.g., repairing or reinforcing the network) and identify conditions that preserve submodularity or recover supermodularity. Via extensive numerical studies with diverse types of attacks, we demonstrate an order-of-magnitude computational speedup achieved by exploiting these properties and generating valid inequalities, for solving network interdiction at scale.

</details>

Method: 提出一个SGD风格的优化框架，采用两种批量梯度构造：理想逐批梯度G和缓存的替代梯度H（用于全数据项计算昂贵的场景）。在样本可分离情形下退化为标准小批量SGD，并建立了统一的局部收敛理论。

Conclusion: 所提框架统一了可分离与不可分离目标的优化理论，为使用缓存梯度近似实现高效训练提供了理论保障，并量化了关键超参数对收敛性能的影响。

Abstract: We study non-separable objectives in which the loss depend on dataset-level quantities. We introduce an SGD-style framework that employs two batch-gradient constructs: the ideal per-batch gradient `$G$' and a cached surrogate `$H$' for cases where full-data terms are expensive.
  Notably, in the sample-wise separable case, our method reduces to standard mini-batch SGD. Our main contribution is a unified local convergence theory: under mild smoothness and Jacobian-boundedness assumptions,
  we prove local linear convergence under local strong convexity and local $O(1/k)$ sublinear convergence under local convexity for both `$G$'-driven and `$H$'-driven updates.
  Crucially, these guarantees hold for fixed step sizes within explicitly characterized ranges; we provide explicit bounds showing how cache staleness, surrogate approximation error, batch size, and step size influence the convergence constants and allowable step-size ranges.

Abstract: This paper develops a mean-field theory for a simplified single-layer causal multi-head self-attention model trained by cross-entropy minimization. Each attention head is treated as a particle in parameter space, and the empirical law of the heads is used as the large-head state variable. In the infinite-head limit, the averaged attention logits define a risk functional on probability measures, whose first variation generates a nonlinear Wasserstein gradient-flow equation. Unlike classical mean-field analyses of shallow networks that often focus on square-loss regression, the present model contains the softmax residual from the cross-entropy objective and the query-key-value structure of masked self-attention. We prove a static finite-head approximation bound for the optimal risk, characterize global minimizers through a variational support condition, and establish a quantitative finite-time propagation-of-chaos estimate comparing finite-head stochastic gradient descent with the limiting PDE. We then study the long-time behavior of the PDE: energy dissipation, convergence to the stationary set under compactness, convergence to a single stationary measure under topological or Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz assumptions, and explicit convergence rates under gradient-domination conditions. Finally, we prove local exponential stability under a Wasserstein strong-monotonicity condition and give verifiable stability and instability criteria for Dirac stationary measures. The results provide a rigorous baseline mean-field framework for attention-head training and clarify the additional compactness, landscape, and curvature assumptions needed to pass from stationarity to convergence and stability.

</details>

Abstract: We consider a finite-horizon, zero-sum stochastic differential game in which one player controls a path-dependent stochastic system, while the opponent is given the opportunity to terminate the game prematurely. We introduce a control randomization formulation, which allows us to establish that the upper and lower value functions coincide.
  Our approach also yields a representation of the common game value in terms of a nonlinear Snell envelope, where the underlying stopped process is given by the unique maximal solution of a backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with constrained jumps.

Abstract: We propose a new optimization method, the Nyström-enhanced relaxed scalar auxiliary variable method (N-RSAV), which incorporates curvature information into the RSAV framework to accelerate convergence while preserving an unconditional modified energy dissipation law. Existing RSAV-based methods rely solely on first-order information and often suffer from slow convergence, particularly for ill-conditioned problems such as those arising in physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). To address this limitation, we design the linear operator in the RSAV scheme using approximate Hessian information obtained from a randomized low-rank Nyström approximation. To preserve the dissipation structure, we enforce positive semidefiniteness through eigenvalue truncation. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive strategy that reuses the approximate Hessian based on the deviation between the original and modified energies, significantly reducing computational cost. We also provide a convergence analysis of the RSAV scheme with a general positive semidefinite operator under the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition and establish corresponding convergence guarantees for N-RSAV under the PL condition and an additional convexity assumption. Numerical experiments on ill-conditioned problems with effectively low-rank structure, including convex quadratic problems and training of PINNs, demonstrate that the proposed methods achieve substantially faster convergence than conventional RSAV-based approaches.

</details>


### [96] [Short-Range IAM Operations to Overcome Access Gaps Around Bologna Airport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10552)
*Jean-Claude Lebègue,Xuhao Gui,Daniel Delahaye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究创新航空出行（IAM）作为博洛尼亚马可尼机场（BLQ）的枢纽喂给与疏散服务，利用时间扩展网络模型和两阶段优化方法（A*路径规划+动态规划调度），模拟50架电动垂直起降飞行器在09:00-17:00时段内的运营效果，结果表明IAM在多条航线上具有显著的旅行时间优势（如BLQ-Il Ciocco仅需46分钟vs火车237-294分钟），并通过敏感性分析揭示了技术参数和旅客留存率对覆盖范围与可达性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统固定翼航班效率低下、地面交通耗时过长的短程机场间连通性问题，探索利用IAM（电动垂直起降飞行器）作为枢纽机场的喂给和疏散服务，以增强区域可达性而不改变现有航班时刻表。

Method: 采用交错滚动窗口模型从BLQ航班时刻表生成需求；构建IAM-only时间扩展网络（机场作为垂直起降场）；两阶段求解：先用A*算法进行旅客路径规划以揭示边级需求压力，再用动态规划进行机队调度以安排有限IAM机队。

Result: IAM在多条航线上具有显著旅行时间优势（如BLQ-Il Ciocco: 46分钟 vs 火车237-294分钟）；少数线路（如BLQ-佛罗伦萨）火车仍有竞争力；敏感性分析表明飞行速度和旅客留存率会显著影响覆盖范围、时刻可行性和枢纽可达性。

Conclusion: IAM有潜力在无需改变现有航班时刻表的前提下增强BLQ的区域覆盖能力，技术参数和旅客接受度是决定可行性及效益的关键因素。

Abstract: We study Innovative Air Mobility (IAM) as a dedicated hub feeder and dispersal service for Bologna Guglielmo Marconi Airport (BLQ), addressing short-range airport-to-airport connectivity where scheduled fixed-wing services are inefficient and surface access is time-consuming. Demand is generated directly from BLQ's flight timetable via a staggered rolling-window model: arriving passengers become IAM-eligible after a 20-minute buffer (a baseline 70% ''staying'' share is considered and distributed across nearby airports using a gravity model), while feeder demand for departures is timestamped backward by IAM travel time plus a 2-hour pre-flight buffer. We build an IAM-only time-expanded network (airports as vertiports) and solve two sequential subproblems: passenger routing with A* to reveal edge-level demand pressure, followed by fleet dispatch with dynamic programming to schedule a finite IAM fleet. Using OAG schedules for 5 May 2024, we simulate 09:00-17:00 with a 50-vehicle fleet (10 seats, 30 m/s) and 5-minute windows. Results show large travel-time advantages versus rail for many links (e.g., BLQ-Il Ciocco: 46 min IAM vs. 237-294 min by train), while a few pairs remain competitive for rail (e.g., BLQ-Florence). Sensitivity to IAM speed and staying share quantifies how technology and adoption shape feasible coverage, timing feasibility, and hub accessibility, demonstrating the potential of IAM to strengthen BLQ's regional reach without altering airline schedules.

</details>


### [97] [Direct Data-Driven Approximate Optimal Control of Nonlinear Input-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10810)
*Benita Nortmann,Thulasi Mylvaganam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过将未知非线性动力学嵌入扩展状态空间，利用纯数据依赖的代数条件来刻画最优控制问题的解，从而无需显式辨识系统模型即可直接基于数据设计具有局部稳定性和性能保证的动态状态反馈控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知非线性、输入仿射系统，现有方法通常需要先辨识系统动力学模型再进行控制器设计，而辨识过程本身可能引入误差且计算成本高。本文旨在消除对显式系统辨识的依赖，直接从数据出发构建具有理论保证的最优控制器。

Method: 提出一种数据驱动的系统表示方法，将未知动力学嵌入到扩展状态空间中；利用纯数据依赖的代数条件来刻画非线性最优控制问题的（近似）解；基于上述代数条件设计动态状态反馈控制器。

Result: 所设计的控制器能够为未知非线性输入仿射系统提供局部稳定性和性能保证，且整个过程无需显式辨识系统动力学。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将数据驱动控制与非线性最优控制理论结合，在避免系统辨识的前提下实现了具有稳定性和性能保障的控制器设计，为处理未知非线性系统提供了一种高效且理论完备的框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we combine a data-driven system representation with a framework to systematically construct (approximate) solutions to nonlinear optimal control problems. By immersing the unknown dynamics into an extended state space, solutions are characterised via purely data-dependent algebraic conditions. This allows us to design dynamic state-feedback controllers with local stability and performance guarantees for unknown nonlinear, input-affine systems directly using data, without explicitly identifying the dynamics.

</details>


### [98] [A Stochastic Maximum Principle for Partially Observed Jump-Diffusion Systems with Counting-Process Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10844)
*Jie Xiong,Ying Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推导了部分观测跳扩散系统在多元计数过程观测下的随机最大值原理，并通过参考概率测度转换和条件哈密顿量驻点条件得到最优性必要条件，应用于线性二次金融执行问题，数值实验表明粒子滤波实现优于高斯和跳盲近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机最大值原理针对部分观测跳扩散系统，其观测过程多为连续过程或单变量计数过程，缺乏对多元计数过程（强度依赖于状态）的通用理论框架，且实际金融执行问题中状态不可直接观测，需要理论指导。

Method: 引入参考概率测度将观测过程转化为单位泊松过程，将部分观测控制问题重新表述为条件分布上的完全观测控制问题，推导条件哈密顿量驻点条件作为必要最优性原理；应用于线性二次金融执行问题，通过匹配系数得到由潜在状态条件均值驱动的闭式Riccati反馈律，并采用粒子滤波进行数值实现。

Result: 推导出部分观测跳扩散系统在多元计数过程观测下的随机最大值原理；得到闭式Riccati反馈律；数值实验表明粒子滤波实现能够捕捉非对称跳跃和非线性计数观测机制引起的非高斯滤波结构，在滤波精度上优于高斯近似和跳盲近似，闭环性能接近全信息理想情况。

Conclusion: 建立了一个通用且可计算的部分观测跳扩散控制理论框架，在线性二次金融执行问题中验证了其有效性，表明捕获非高斯滤波结构对性能提升至关重要。

Abstract: This paper derives a stochastic maximum principle (SMP) for partially observed jump-diffusion systems whose observations are multivariate counting processes with state-dependent intensities. By introducing a reference probability measure under which the observation processes become unit Poisson processes, the partially observed control problem is rewritten as a fully observed control one on the conditional distribution, leading to a conditional Hamiltonian stationarity condition as a necessary optimality principle. We apply the framework to a linear-quadratic financial execution problem. Matching coefficients yields a closed-form Riccati feedback law driven by the conditional mean of the latent state. Numerical experiments with a particle-filter implementation demonstrate that the resulting controller captures the non-Gaussian filtering structure arising from both asymmetric jumps and the nonlinear counting-process observation mechanism, improves filtering accuracy over Gaussian and jump-blind approximations, and achieves closed-loop performance close to the full-information oracle.

</details>


### [99] [Dynamic inverse problems: Single-loop online algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10951)
*Jyrki Jauhiainen,Yassine Nabou,Tuomo Valkonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无限时域动态逆问题，提出了一种高效在线方法，通过单循环估计（单步迭代求解PDE及其伴随PDE）替代精确梯度，在满足可和误差与光滑性条件下使标准遗憾界成立，并在动态电阻抗断层成像（EIT）数值实验中验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态逆问题（如EIT）中，快速流场监测要求在每个时刻快速求解PDE约束反问题，但传统方法在数据到达前无法完成PDE求解，亟需一种无需完整求解PDE的在线计算方法。

Method: 采用在线优化框架，外层为标准在线优化方法；内层用单循环估计——在每个时间索引只取单步（或少数几步）迭代求解器，同时逼近正问题PDE和伴随PDE的解，以此构造对数据保真项梯度的近似，且该近似满足可和误差和标准光滑性假设。

Result: 理论层面：证明了单循环梯度估计的标准光滑性及可和误差性质，使得标准遗憾界证明得以沿用。实验层面：在动态EIT数值实验中验证了理论预测，展示了该方法在PDE约束动态逆问题实时求解中的潜力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的单循环在线方法有效解决了快速动态PDE约束逆问题中计算时间与数据到达速度不匹配的难题，在保证理论收敛性的同时实现了实时求解能力。

Abstract: We study efficient online methods for dynamic inverse problems with infinite time horizon. We concentrate, in particular, on problems whose forward model arises from a PDE. Our motivating application is flow monitoring with Electrical Impedance Tomography (EIT). The idea of such online methods is to take single steps of of standard optimisation algorithms, on each time index; each data frame. A predictor, based on problem dynamics, is used to transfer iterates one from time index to the next one. If we monitor a fast flow with a correspondingly fast measurement modality, such as EIT, basic methods are unable to solve the PDE before new data arrives. Our idea, then, is to not solve it, and instead, on each iteration, each time index, take single or few steps of standard iterative solvers towards the solution of both the PDE and an adjoint PDE. This is what ``single loop'' refers to. To the overall problem, we apply standard online optimisation methods, at the outside developed for exact gradients $\nabla E_k(x^k)$ of the iteration-dependent data fidelity $E_k$ that incorporates the PDE. We replace the gradient by a single-loop estimate $\tilde\grad E_k(x^k)$ that satisfies standard smoothness properties with summable errors. This allows standard regret proofs to go through. Our numerical experiments on dynamic EIT validate the theoretical predictions and highlight the potential of the proposed approach for the real-time solution of PDE-constrained dynamic inverse problems.

</details>


### [100] [Continuous Time Identification of Linear Systems: Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10963)
*Fatima J. Ghadieh,Mireille E. Broucke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非神经网络的神经形态模型识别框架，采用连续时间自适应观测器，通过过参数化模型和离散算法逐步学习模型阶次与参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种遵循神经形态计算原则但不依赖神经网络作为数学基础的模型识别框架，解决未知系统阶次与观测器阶次不匹配的核心技术障碍。

Method: 采用连续时间自适应观测器，引入过参数化模型处理过建模情况，并理论扩展到欠建模情况；设计离散算法协调递进实验以增量学习模型阶次，同时使用标准参数自适应律学习参数。

Result: 提出的过参数化模型在过建模情况下提供了输入-输出等价的合适参数化方法，离散算法能够逐步学习模型阶次。

Conclusion: 该框架为非神经网络路径的神经形态计算提供了可行的模型识别方法，通过过参数化与增量学习策略解决了阶次失配问题。

Abstract: We consider a problem to develop a framework for model identification adhering to the tenets of neuromorphic computation, without resorting to neural networks as the mathematical substrate. In particular, all computations take place in continuous time. We are naturally led to adaptive observers, where the main technical obstacle is the possible mismatch between the unknown plant order and the observer order. The key concept that informs the proposed framework is an overparameterized model, an input-output equivalent model that provides a suitable parameterization in the overmodeled case, with theoretical extensions also addressing the undermodeled case. A discrete algorithm orchestrates successive experiments to incrementally learn the model order, while a standard parameter adaptation law learns the parameters.

</details>


### [101] [Dominance and symmetry-breaking rules for the Graph Burning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10966)
*Nice Prado,Rafael Colares*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过研究图燃烧问题与支配集问题的相似性，提出了一种新的整数线性规划公式，并应用支配规则和对称性破缺技术来减少搜索空间，同时引入目标函数扰动和剪枝规则，从而加速问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图燃烧问题（GBP）是NP难的组合优化问题，现有整数线性规划方法在大规模图上收敛困难，因此需要减少搜索空间以提高求解性能。

Method: (1) 研究GBP与支配集问题的相似性；(2) 基于此提出新的整数线性规划公式；(3) 应用支配规则和对称性破缺技术缩减搜索空间；(4) 引入目标函数扰动及相应的剪枝规则进一步加速求解。

Result: 所提出的方法通过减少搜索空间显著加快了求解速度，相比现有方法在更大规模的图上表现更优。

Conclusion: 利用GBP与支配集问题的结构相似性，结合对称性破缺、目标函数扰动等技巧，可以有效提升GBP整数规划求解的可扩展性。

Abstract: The Graph Burning Problem (GBP) is a NP-Hard combinatorial optimization problem that models the propagation of influence or contagion in a network. The propagation is represented through the metaphor of a fire spreading through the vertices of a graph. A burning process takes place in a series of discrete time-steps. At each time step, the burning process is characterized by a propagation (where burned nodes spread the fire to their neighbors), and an ignition (where one additional unburned node is chosen to become burned). The minimum number of steps required to burn all vertices of a graph defines its burning number. Literature provides integer linear programming formulations to solve the problem, but with no surprise, such approaches struggle to converge as the graph size increases. Therefore, reducing the search space explored by these formulations becomes a key point to improve performances. In this work, we study the similarities of the Graph Burning Problem with the well-known Dominating Set Problem. We propose a new formulation based on this study and apply dominance rules and symmetry-breaking techniques to reduce the search space and consequently speed up resolution time. We also introduce a perturbation of the proposed objective function, as well as a pruning rule for the perturbed model in order to further accelerate its resolution.

</details>


### [102] [The Fuel Optimizer: A Data-Driven Numerical Framework for Formulation of Aviation Turbine Fuel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11099)
*Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Jacobo Porteiro,Dario Lopez-Pintor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Fuel Optimizer的逆向设计框架，用于可持续航空燃料的配方优化。该框架从用户定义的效益函数出发，通过代理模型和遗传算法，在满足燃料性质标准（包括密封膨胀）和组成限制的条件下，优化化学组分以实现氮氧化物与CO排放的权衡突破，并最小化起飞-着陆循环中的污染物排放。案例研究表明，优化后的燃料配方在所有效益函数上均优于训练数据库，并通过反应器模拟进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够从目标出发逆向设计可持续航空燃料配方的框架，以同时优化污染物排放（突破NOx与CO的权衡）并满足燃料性质标准，从而加速可持续航空燃料的研发。

Method: 1) 构建符合选定性质标准的燃料混合物数据库；2) 在巡航条件下通过反应器模型模拟以获得污染物排放数据；3) 开发以燃料组成为输入、排放为输出的代理模型以降低计算成本；4) 使用遗传算法根据两个效益函数优化燃料配方；5) 约束条件包括燃料性质标准（含密封膨胀）和组成限制。

Result: 优化后的燃料候选配方在所有效益函数上均优于训练数据库中的燃料；最优候选配方通过了反应器模拟的验证。

Conclusion: Fuel Optimizer框架能够有效逆向设计可持续航空燃料，在满足性质标准的前提下实现污染物排放的优化，为可持续航空燃料的理性设计提供了新方法。

Abstract: The Fuel Optimizer is an inverse design framework for sustainable aviation fuels that starts from a user-defined merit function and identifies the optimal combination of chemical species or hydrocarbon families that optimize a combination of targets. As a case study, a database of fuel blends meeting selected property standards was simulated in a reactor model to obtain pollutant emissions at cruise conditions. A surrogate model was developed to reduce the computational cost of evaluating candidate blends, taking fuel composition as input and predicting emissions as output. A genetic algorithm was used to optimize fuel formulation according to two merit functions designed to break the nitrogen oxides - CO trade-off, and minimize pollutant emissions over the landing-and-take-off cycle. Constraints included selected property standards (including seal swelling) and composition limits. Fuel candidates from the framework outperformed the training database across all merit functions, and the optimal candidates were validated through reactor simulations.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Smooth manifolds homotopy equivalent to products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10239)
*Sagnik Biswas*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文对同伦等价于三种球面乘积流形 $S^{4k-1}\times S^{4k}$, $S^{4k}\times S^{4k}$, $S^{4k}\times S^{4k+1}$ 的光滑闭定向流形进行了几乎微分同胚分类，并通过显式流形族实现了手术精确序列中法不变映射的像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对特定维数球面乘积的同伦等价流形进行几乎微分同胚分类，并显式构造实现法不变映射像的流形族。

Method: 运用光滑手术理论，将法不变映射的像的刻画转化为对三类球面乘积流形的具体构造：$S^{4k}$上的球面丛；pinch映射与Milnor圆盘丛的plumbing；Novikov球面丛与同伦球面的连通和。

Result: 给出了三种球面乘积流形的几乎微分同胚分类，并分别用显式流形族实现了法不变映射的像。

Conclusion: 完成了特定球面乘积同伦等价类的几乎微分同胚分类，并为手术理论中法不变映射的像提供了具体几何实现。

Abstract: We classify, up to almost diffeomorphism, the smooth closed oriented manifolds homotopy equivalent to each of the sphere products $S^{4k-1}\times S^{4k}$, $S^{4k}\times S^{4k}$, and $S^{4k}\times S^{4k+1}$. In each case we realize the image of the normal-invariant map in the smooth surgery exact sequence by explicit families of manifolds: sphere bundles over $S^{4k}$; pinch maps and Milnor plumbings of disk bundles; and Novikov sphere bundles together with connected sums of homotopy spheres.

</details>


### [104] [A characterization of sheaves among six functor formalisms on $\mathrm{LCH}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10453)
*Ulrich Bunke,Marco Volpe*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了稳定可表现对称幺半∞-范畴C上的六函子形式Shv(-,C)在局部紧Hausdorff空间上是唯一的，满足自然性质列表，并推出任何连续六函子形式D等价于Shv(-,D(pt))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在导出代数几何和范畴化中，六函子形式是核心工具。本文旨在为Shv(-,C)提供唯一性刻画，从而统一理解各种六函子形式。

Method: 利用稳定可表现对称幺半∞-范畴理论，在局部紧Hausdorff空间上列出自然公理，通过范畴论手段证明唯一性，并推导等价性。

Result: 证明了Shv(-,C)是满足给定自然性质的唯一六函子形式；进而任何连续六函子形式D都等价于Shv(-,D(pt))。

Conclusion: 为六函子形式提供了唯一性刻画，揭示了所有连续六函子形式本质上由点上的范畴决定，简化了理论框架。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{C}$ be any stable presentably symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category. In this paper, we characterize $\mathrm{Shv}(-,\mathcal{C})$ on locally compact Hausdorff spaces as the unique six functor formalism satisfying a list of very natural properties. As a consequence, we deduce that every continuous six functor formalism $D$ in the sense of Zhu is equivalent to $\mathrm{Shv}(-, D(\mathrm{pt}))$.

</details>


### [105] [Old and new structures on Ran spaces: Length structures, completeness, and conicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10952)
*Sylvain Douteau,Marie Labeye*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了度量空间M上的Ran空间拓扑，构建了介于Hausdorff拓扑和最终拓扑之间的加权拓扑族，证明了最终拓扑作为极限的完备一致性结构，并分析了Riemannian流形上加权拓扑的锥形分层性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中Ran空间存在两种不同拓扑（Hausdorff拓扑和最终拓扑），但缺乏对二者之间连续过渡结构的研究，且最终拓扑的均匀性和分层性质尚未得到充分刻画。

Method: 通过构造加权拓扑族来插值Hausdorff与最终拓扑，在空间范畴中取极限恢复最终拓扑，利用一致性结构证明完备性，并在Riemannian流形背景下分析锥形分层性质。

Result: 加权拓扑成功插值了Hausdorff和最终拓扑；最终拓扑被恢复为极限并具有完备的一致性结构；在Riemannian流形上加权拓扑是锥形分层的，而最终拓扑仅在弱意义下成立。

Conclusion: 加权拓扑为Ran空间提供了一种精细的分层结构，完善了最终拓扑的一致性理论和分层性质，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study topologies on Ran spaces. In the literature, two distinct topologies frequently appear: the Hausdorff topology, and a finer one constructed as a colimit, that we call the final topology. In this work, given a metric space $M$, we construct new metric topologies on $\mathrm{Ran}(M)$, called weighted topologies. They interpolate between the Hausdorff and final topologies, the later being recovered as a limit in the category of spaces. This structure equips the final topology with a uniformity, which we show to be complete. Finally we study the Ran spaces as stratified spaces. We show that, whenever $M$ is a Riemannian manifold, the weighted topologies are conically stratified, while the final topology is only so in a weak sense.

</details>

### [106] [Exceptional Points as Manifestations of Analyticity Breakdown in the 't Hooft Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10141)
*Kejun Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用1+1D大N_c QCD的't Hooft模型严格研究了因果响应函数（介子两点函数）解析性的破坏。通过PT对称变形引入虚化学势类比，在临界点γ_c处最低两个介子合并为异常点（EP），解析地证明了平方根指数ν=1/2，并给出了时域上的线性增长（Jordan secular law）与指数增长信号。该阈值与禁闭耦合，是禁闭规范理论中首个解析可控的EP解析性破坏实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在禁闭规范理论中，因果响应函数的解析性破坏机制尚不明确。本文旨在利用可严格求解的't Hooft模型，通过非厄米PT对称变形，研究介子两点函数在异常点（EP）处的解析性破坏，并揭示其时域特征与禁闭的关联。

Method: 对't Hooft模型中的光锥介子算符施加PT对称虚势变形iγ(x-1/2)，将预解式重写为Jacobi连分式形式，解析求解EP阈值γ_c；通过2×2 Jordan块固定临界指数ν=1/2，结合N=1999的有限尺寸标度验证；分析时域传播子范数的渐进行为。

Result: 在二极点水平得到γ_c=2πg²N_c，深度五收敛至7.966g²N_c的解析表达式（非数值拟合）。临界指数ν=1/2由Jordan形式确定。时域上：γ<γ_c时传播子范数有界，γ=γ_c时线性增长（Jordan secular law），γ>γ_c时指数增长。阈值与禁闭耦合正比（γ_c∝g²N_c），并表现为均匀EP级联。另一非互易变形产生精确指数非厄米趋肤效应。

Conclusion: 该工作是禁闭规范理论中首个解析可控的异常点解析性破坏实例，证明了非厄米PT对称变形可作为研究QCD解析结构的新工具，并在光子学与拓扑电子学中具有可观测的类比实现。

Abstract: We use the exactly-solvable t Hooft model of 1+1D large-N_c QCD as a rigorous laboratory for the breakdown of analyticity of a causal response function, the meson two-point function. A PT-symmetric deformation i gamma(x-1/2) of the light-cone meson operator, the analogue of an imaginary chemical potential, drives the lowest two mesons to an exceptional point (EP) at gamma_c. Recasting the resolvent as a Jacobi continued fraction yields gamma_c in closed form: 2 pi g^2 N_c at the two-pole level, converging to 7.966 g^2 N_c by depth five -- an analytic, not numerical, threshold. The square-root exponent nu=1/2 is fixed by the 2x2 Jordan form and confirmed by finite-size scaling to N=1999. The breakdown has an unambiguous time-domain signature: the propagator norm is bounded for gamma < gamma_c, grows linearly at gamma_c (the Jordan secular law), and exponentially beyond -- observable, since the deformed operator is a non-Hermitian Wannier-Stark ladder, in photonic and topolectrical analogues. The threshold is locked to confinement, gamma_c propto g^2 N_c, and recurs as a uniform EP cascade; a second, non-reciprocal deformation yields an exactly-exponential non-Hermitian skin effect. This is the first analytically-controlled instance of exceptional-point analyticity breakdown in a confining gauge theory.

</details>


### [107] [Contact Tulczyjew Geometry for Continuous and Discrete Dissipative Dynamics on Skew Algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10515)
*Leonardo Colombo,Manuel de León*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在斜代数胚上建立了接触Tulczyjew形式体系，用于描述耗散动力学。通过引入Euler向量场贡献，将标准Tulczyjew态射扩展为接触版本，从而局部得到Euler-Lagrange-Herglotz方程和接触Hamilton方程。同时发展了离散对应，通过离散接触生成对象构造接触Tulczyjew关系，在正则切丛情形恢复标准接触变分积分器，在奇异或斜代数胚情形保持为隐式离散关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Tulczyjew形式体系从辛几何/李代数胚推广到接触几何/斜代数胚，以统一处理耗散动力学系统，并建立其连续和离散框架。

Method: (1) 在斜代数胚的Tulczyjew态射基础上，通过添加Euler向量场贡献得到接触扩展，在线丛平凡化下给出局部表示；(2) 通过接触生成对象构造隐式耗散动力学，导出Euler-Lagrange-Herglotz方程；(3) 在超正则情形通过Legendre变换得到接触Hamilton方程；(4) 离散化：建立离散容许关系，定义离散接触Tulczyjew关系，通过匹配接触动量得到离散Herglotz极值曲线。

Result: 建立了斜代数胚上的接触Tulczyjew形式体系，包括连续和离散两个层面。连续情形得到Euler-Lagrange-Herglotz方程和接触Hamilton方程；离散情形恢复标准接触变分积分器，并在奇异/斜代数胚情形保持为隐式离散关系，具有法丛解释。

Conclusion: 本文为耗散动力学的几何框架提供了统一且内在的接触描述，其离散构造为变分积分器在耗散系统中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a contact Tulczyjew formalism for dissipative dynamics on skew algebroids. Starting from the Tulczyjew morphism of an skew algebroid, we identify its contact extension in a local line-bundle trivialization. The local representative is obtained by adding to the ordinary Tulczyjew morphism the Euler vector field contribution on $E^*$. This gives an intrinsic explanation of the contact term appearing in the local contact Tulczyjew morphism.
  For a contact generating object, the construction produces an implicit dissipative dynamics on the contact phase side. In local coordinates, the matching condition gives the Euler-Lagrange-Herglotz equations on the skew algebroid. In the hyperregular case, the corresponding contact Hamiltonian equations are recovered by Legendre transformation.
  We also develop the discrete counterpart of the construction. After fixing a discrete admissibility relation, a discrete contact generating object defines a discrete contact Tulczyjew relation on the contact phase space. Discrete Herglotz extremals are obtained by matching consecutive contact momenta, with the usual conormal interpretation in the constrained case. In the regular tangent-bundle case, this recovers standard contact variational integrators, while in the singular or skew algebroid setting the same construction remains meaningful as an implicit discrete relation rather than an a priori update map.

</details>


### [108] [Weighted least action principle for Maxwell equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10745)
*Jacob Rubinstein,Gershon Wolansky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个升级版的变分原理，基于在两个平面上测量波强度，为任意介质中麦克斯韦方程的几何光学极限提供了完整的菲涅尔射线束，并利用菲涅尔射线与相位法线之间的互易性，从两次强度测量中确定电磁波的相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统费马原理仅确定单条光线路径，但单条光线并非可测量的物理对象，因此需要基于可测量量（强度）的变分原理来描述完整的光束。

Method: 基于在两个平面上测量波的强度，推导了适用于任意介质中麦克斯韦方程几何光学极限的升级版变分原理，提供完整的菲涅尔射线束。

Result: 获得了连接第一和第二平面上关联点的完整菲涅尔射线束。

Conclusion: 利用菲涅尔射线与相位法线的互易性，可通过两次强度测量确定电磁波的相位。

Abstract: The Fermat principle of least time determines the path of a single ray of light given its initial and final positions and the medium electromagnetic properties. However, a single ray is not a measurable physical object. We derive here an upgraded variational principle for the geometric optics limit of Maxwell equations in an arbitrary medium, based on measuring the intensity of the wave on two planes. The principle provides the complete Fresnel rays bundle connecting associated points at the first and second planes. One of the applications of the present theory is to use the reciprocity between Fresnel rays and phase normals to determine the phase of an electromagnetic wave from two intensity measurements.

</details>


### [109] [Nonlinear Anisotropic Visco-Anelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10801)
*Souhayl Sadik,Arash Yavari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的非线性几何理论，将粘弹性和非弹性响应纳入同一热力学框架。通过将变形梯度乘法分解为弹性、粘性和非弹性畸变，推广了Bilby-Kröner-Lee分解，并利用材料度量、结构张量等几何工具构建了完整的本构方程、平衡律、热力学势和动力学方程，并通过各向同性和横观各向同性示例加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性粘弹性与粘塑性理论中，中间构型的定义存在歧义，且粘弹性与弹性应变驱动的非弹性效应缺乏统一的几何表述。作者旨在建立一套自洽的几何框架，清晰界定弹性、粘性和非弹性畸变的几何结构，消除现有理论中的模糊性。

Method: 采用乘法分解（推广Bilby-Kröner-Lee分解）将总变形梯度分解为弹性、粘性和非弹性部分；引入材料度量描述固体的自然构型演化；利用结构张量处理一般各向异性；从热力学第一、第二定律出发推导本构方程和演化方程，无需依赖观察者不变性假设。

Result: 建立了统一的粘弹-非弹性几何理论框架；通过各向同性和横观各向同性特例验证了框架的有效性；给出了适用于不可压缩各向异性固体（含分布本征应变及残余应力）的通用变形闭合或半闭合解示例。

Conclusion: 该几何框架成功统一了非线性粘弹性与弹性应变驱动的非弹性效应，明确了中间构型的几何意义，解决了现有粘弹性和粘塑性理论中关于中间构型的歧义，为耦合时间相关与残余应力效应的材料建模提供了自洽的理论基础。

Abstract: We formulate a nonlinear geometric theory of visco-anelasticity that unifies viscoelastic and anelastic responses within a single thermodynamic framework. At each material point, the total deformation gradient is multiplicatively decomposed into elastic, viscous, and anelastic distortions, thereby generalizing the Bilby-Kröner-Lee decomposition to visco-anelasticity. The theory explicitly incorporates the material metric, which encodes the evolving natural configuration of the solid, the transformed structural tensors, and provides a consistent formulation of the constitutive equations, the balance laws, the thermodynamic potentials, and the kinetic equations. The first and second laws of thermodynamics are systematically applied to derive the constitutive and evolution equations without invoking observer invariance. Anisotropy is treated in full generality through structural tensors. As illustrative examples, we specialize the general framework to isotropic and transversely isotropic visco-anelastic solids. Two examples within the class of universal deformations, which admit closed or partially closed form solutions, show how the proposed framework can be used to model the coupled viscous and anelastic response of incompressible anisotropic solids with distributed eigenstrains and the associated residual stresses. This geometric framework unifies nonlinear viscoelasticity and anelasticity by coupling time-dependent and eigenstrain-driven effects within a single, fully consistent geometric formulation. In particular, the proposed framework clarifies the geometric structure of the elastic, viscous, and anelastic distortions and resolves ambiguities associated with intermediate configurations in existing formulations of nonlinear viscoelasticity and viscoplasticity.

</details>


### [110] [The Yang-Baxter Equation for the Chiral Potts Model and Integrable Parafermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11146)
*Zhao Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从手征Potts模型的星-三角和星-星关系出发，构造了一种由三个谱参数参数化的新型Yang-Baxter方程（YBE），该构造扩展了最近可解边模型和顶点模型的统一框架，将Onsager的星-三角关系从边模型YBE的替代形式提升为其基本组成要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征Potts模型作为Ising模型的Z_N对称推广，其玻尔兹曼权重依赖于描述高亏格曲线的两个变量（N>2时），且边模型的量子哈密顿量同时包含最近邻相互作用和局域势项，导致R-算子需要额外的谱参数。现有框架未能充分统一这些结构。

Method: 从手征Potts模型的星-三角关系和星-星关系出发，构造了由三个谱参数参数化的新型Yang-Baxter方程，将Onsager的星-三角关系从边模型YBE的替代形式重新解释为其基本组成要素。

Result: 成功构造了具有三个谱参数的新型YBE，扩展了可解边模型和顶点模型的统一框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将星-三角关系提升为YBE的基本组成部分，为理解手征Potts模型等高亏格曲线上的可解模型提供了新的代数结构视角。

Abstract: A new type of Yang-Baxter equation (YBE) for $R$-matrices parameterized by three spectral parameters is constructed from the star-triangle and star-star relations for the chiral Potts model. As the $Z_N$ symmetric generalization to the Ising model, its Boltzmann weights are known to depend on two variables describing a curve with genus larger than one for $N>2$, except for the self-dual point corresponding to the Fateev-Zamolodchikov chain. This combined with the fact that the quantum Hamiltonians of edge models like Ising contain both nearest neighbor interaction and onsite potential terms results in the extra spectral parameter of the $R$-operator. My construction extends the recent unification of solvable edge and vertex models which recasts Onsager's star-triangle relation from a mere alternative form of the YBE for edge models to its underlying ingredient.

</details>

### [111] [Modelling the Dynamics of Middle-Aged Pulsar Wind Nebulae in the Reverberation Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09914)
*Yuri Batini,Niccolò Bucciantini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过一维和二维模拟系统研究了中年脉冲星风云（PWNe）在回响阶段的动力学演化，发现尽管系统存在多样性，但最终都会趋向与Sedov解一致的弛豫状态，且一维模型在全局动力学预测上是稳健的，而多维效应可使视大小增加最高50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星风云是甚高能伽马射线波段的重要辐射源，预测其长期演化对于即将运行的高能天文台（如ASTRI和CTA）至关重要。中年PWNe可能是银河系TeV辐射的主要贡献者，但现有方法的鲁棒性及多维效应对演化的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 采用标准分布生成合成PWN-SNR样本，定义感兴趣区域；利用半解析框架描述早期演化，使用一维拉格朗日代码追踪回响阶段；对典型系统进行优化的二维模拟以研究不稳定性增长和长时标混合效应。

Result: 感兴趣区域内晚期演化呈现多样性，但所有系统最终收敛到与Sedov解一致的弛豫状态；不稳定性增长依赖于初始扰动，但不显著改变全局动力学；二维模型中有效体积演化与一维预测一致，但视大小可增加高达50%。

Conclusion: 首次详细研究了中年PWNe在回响阶段的多维演化，证实了一维模型在全局动力学预测上的稳健性，表明当前预测是可信的。

Abstract: Pulsar Wind Nebulae (PWNe) are among the most important sources emitting in the very-high-energy gamma-ray band. Predicting their long-term evolution is crucial for forthcoming high-energy observatories like ASTRI and CTA. In this work, We investigate the dynamical evolution of middle-aged PWNe - probably the major contributors to the Galactic TeV emission - and test the robustness of current approaches. To understand the diversity of these systems, we derive the Pulsar and Supernova Remnant (SNR) parameters governing PWN evolution. SNR evolution is set by supernova kinetic energy, ejecta mass, and ambient density, while pulsar energy injection powers the PWN expansion. Adopting standard distributions, we generate a synthetic PWN-SNR population and define a region of interest encompassing the majority of these objects. We use a semi-analytical framework for the early evolution and a 1D Lagrangian code to track their interaction with parent SNRs (the reverberation phase). Within our region of interest, we find large diversity in the late-stage evolution. Despite this, all systems converge toward a relaxed state consistent with the Sedov solution. To address 1D limitations, we perform 2D simulations - optimized to reduce computational cost while preserving physical accuracy - to study instability growth and long-term mixing. We find that instability growth depends on initial perturbations but does not significantly alter global dynamics. While effective volume evolution agrees with 1D predictions, multidimensional effects can increase the apparent size by up to 50%. For the first time, we investigated in detail the multidimensional evolution of middle-aged PWNe in the reverberation phase. By characterizing the late-time evolution across the population, our results confirm the robustness of 1D models, demonstrating that current predictions remain trustworthy.

</details>


### [112] [The Curious Case of PHL 1811: Heavy Obscuration Versus Intrinsic X-ray Weakness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09981)
*B. Luo,Xiaolei Chen,Jian Huang,W. N. Brandt,Qingling Ni*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对窄线I型类星体PHL 1811在2001-2024年间的X射线数据分析，确认其长期处于X射线弱态（weakness factor ~23-179），但2024年爱因斯坦探针（EP）首次探测到其X射线正常态（weakness factor ~0.63）并伴随耀发。研究识别出三个支持重遮蔽的关键观测特征：5 keV以上的硬X射线过剩、平坦光谱形状、以及X射线态转换时缺乏光学/红外对应变化。部分覆盖遮蔽模型能很好地再现多历元光谱，观测到的陡峭光谱主要来自内禀连续谱的少量泄露/散射成分，变率由泄露比例和柱密度的变化驱动。结果表明PHL 1811受超爱丁顿吸积驱动的辐射驱动吸积盘风遮蔽，将其纳入统一的超爱丁顿吸积AGN遮蔽框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示PHL 1811长期X射线弱态的本质原因，检验其是否由遮蔽效应而非内禀X射线弱导致，并将其与超爱丁顿吸积AGN群体统一。

Method: 利用2001-2024年间的档案X射线观测数据（包括EP和Swift后耀发数据），进行光谱拟合和变率分析，识别硬X射线过剩、光谱形状和光变特征，采用部分覆盖遮蔽模型进行多历元光谱拟合。

Result: 确认PHL 1811在2024年前所有历元均呈X射线弱态（f_weak ~23-179）；2024年EP首次探测到X射线正常态（f_weak ~0.63）并伴随耀发；识别出三个支持重遮蔽的观测特征；部分覆盖遮蔽模型成功拟合所有多历元光谱；变率由泄露比例和柱密度变化驱动。

Conclusion: PHL 1811的X射线弱态是由遮蔽效应而非内禀性质导致，其遮蔽体很可能是超爱丁顿吸积驱动的辐射驱动吸积盘风，该源应被纳入统一的超爱丁顿吸积AGN遮蔽框架中。

Abstract: We present a systematic X-ray analysis of the narrow-line Type 1 quasar PHL 1811, which has long been regarded as the prototype of intrinsically X-ray weak quasars. A critical breakthrough came with the first detection of a bright X-ray flare from this source by the Einstein Probe (EP) in 2024. We utilize archival X-ray observations spanning 2001-2024, including the post-flare EP and Swift data. We confirm that PHL 1811 shows X-ray weakness factors $f_{\rm weak} \approx 23$-179 across all epochs before 2024. The 2024 EP flare marks the first detection of an X-ray nominal state with $f_{\rm weak} \approx 0.63$, followed by a rapid flux decline. We identify three key observational signatures that strongly support heavy obscuration: (1) a significant hard X-ray excess above $\approx5$ keV in the 2015 XMM-Newton spectrum; (2) relatively flat spectral shapes in two Swift observations; and (3) transitions between X-ray nominal and multiple X-ray weak states without corresponding optical/infrared variability, consistent with expectations from obscuration by a clumpy dust-free absorber. Fitting with a partial-covering obscuration model reproduces all multi-epoch spectra well. The observed steep spectra are dominated by a small leaked/scattered fraction of the intrinsic continuum, and variability is driven by changes in the leakage fraction and column density. Our results strongly favor the scenario where PHL 1811 is obscured by a radiatively driven accretion-disk wind from super-Eddington accretion, unifying PHL 1811 with the broader population of super-Eddington accreting AGNs under a single obscuration framework.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激波突破是超新星爆发的首个信号，产生紫外和X射线爆发，但这类事件在观测上极为罕见。作者利用爱因斯坦探针卫星的快速响应能力，对近邻的快速X射线暂现源EP260321a进行多波段观测，旨在揭示其物理起源并探索超新星与伽马暴/快速X射线暂现源之间的联系。

Method: 采用多波段观测策略，包括爱因斯坦探针卫星的X射线探测、光学/近红外后随观测（光谱+测光）以及钱德拉X射线天文台的深度后随观测。通过分析X射线光谱（热成分kT=160 eV）、光变曲线、超新星光谱演化及膨胀速度，并与已知模型（伽马暴余辉、激波突破模型）进行对比约束。

Result: 1) X射线辐射为热辐射（kT=160 eV），峰值光度2.2×10^44 erg/s，符合激波突破起源。2) 光学对应体为宽谱线Ic型超新星，性质与GRB相关的超新星一致。3) 钱德拉未探测到X射线余辉，排除了成功相对论性喷流的典型余辉。4) 若存在相对论性喷流，则其洛伦兹因子Γ₀<30、动能E_kin<10^49 erg。5) 提出mildly relativistic的扼杀喷流/弱外流模型。

Conclusion: EP260321a是一个由激波突破主导的快速X射线暂现源，伴随一颗典型的GRB相关Ic型超新星，但缺乏成功的相对论性喷流。该事件连接了SN 2008D（低X射线光度）和低光度GRB之间的观测空白，表明恒星坍缩时产生的喷流/外流参数存在比以往认知更广泛的多样性。这一发现对理解大质量恒星死亡时的喷流产生机制和超新星-伽马暴关联具有重要启示。

Abstract: The explosion of a star is first marked by the shock wave breaking out of the stellar surface, producing a burst of ultraviolet and X-ray radiation. These events are observationally rare, despite likely accompanying the majority of supernovae. Here, we report on our multi-wavelength observing campaign of the closest Einstein Probe fast X-ray transient EP260321a at $z=0.0344$. The thermal ($kT=160$ eV) X-ray emission with peak luminosity $2.2\times10^{44}$ erg s$^{-1}$ points to a shock breakout origin. We demonstrate that EP260321a is accompanied by a broad-lined Type Ic supernova, SN 2026gzf. The supernova properties, including its spectral evolution, lightcurve evolution, and expansion velocities, are all typical of the energetic stripped-envelope supernovae associated with gamma-ray bursts. However, deep X-ray upper limits obtained with the \textit{Chandra X-ray Observatory} do not detect an X-ray afterglow, and instead exclude the afterglow of known gamma-ray bursts or fast X-ray transients. If the stellar explosion launched a successful relativistic jet, we require that it had both a low Lorentz factor $Γ_0$\,$<$\,$30$ and a kinetic energy $E_\textrm{kin}$\,$<$\,$10^{49}$ erg for a stellar wind density of $A_*$\,$\gtrsim$\,$1$. We propose that EP260321a originated from a mildly relativistic, weak outflow that was choked by the progenitor star. This scenario is capable of naturally explaining its low X-ray luminosity and lack of prompt gamma-ray emission. EP260321a bridges the gap between SN 2008D and low-luminosity GRBs, suggesting a greater diversity in the physical parameters of stripped stars as they undergo terminal collapse.

Abstract: A long-standing question in the death of massive stars is the role of relativistic jets. While many gamma-ray bursts and some fast X-ray transients seem to be associated with broad-lined type Ic supernovae, the opposite is not true. The lack of observable jet emission in those Ic-BL SNe can be explained by invoking off-axis jets, choked jets that inject all their energy into the stellar envelope, baryon-loaded jets for which the prompt high-energy emission is strongly suppressed, or non-jetted SNe. The lack of exact explosion time in the majority of SNe presents an obstacle to distinguish between these scenarios. Here we report the properties of SN 2026gzf associated with the X-ray thermal Einstein Probe shock-breakout EP260321a at z=0.0343. The absence of compelling shocked cocoon and radio emission up to 54 days, combined with initial expansion velocities of ~30,000 km/s and a circumstellar shell of ~0.07 M$_\odot$, favour a scenario for SN 2026gzf in which a jet was choked in the circumstellar shell. Our high-spatial resolution images of the SN environment show that the progenitor was located between two highly star-forming regions with a metallicity lower than any previously known Ic-BL SN. As the first case of a Ic-BL SN associated with high-energy prompt emission without the signature of a jet, SN 2026gzf provides a unique perspective to understand the successful launch of relativistic jets during the deaths of massive stars.

Abstract: When a massive star explodes as a supernova, crucial information about its immediate environment is lost within hours. Here we report rapid optical observations from Lulin Observatory of the broad-lined Type Ic supernova SN 2026gzf, beginning 1.25 hours after Einstein Probe detected the X-ray transient EP260321a. Our data led to the discovery of the optical counterpart and showed a luminous blue first-day excess that cannot be reproduced by standard radioactive models. We find that interaction between the ejecta and $\approx 0.02$ M$_{\odot}$ of circumstellar material accounts for the early excess. Archival Panoramic Survey Telescope and Rapid Response System (Pan-STARRS) images show variability at the explosion site over the previous $\sim 12$ years, with the source brightening by a factor of $\sim 1.5$ in the final $\sim 3$ years before explosion, providing rare evidence for pre-explosion activity in a stripped-envelope progenitor system. The precursor brightening suggests enhanced eruptive mass loss during late-stage oxygen burning before core collapse, while an additional silicon-burning episode shortly before explosion may have created the compact nearby material responsible for the X-ray shock-breakout signal. SN 2026gzf therefore offers the first view of how a stripped progenitor modifies its immediate environment shortly before death, linking long-term precursor variability, circumstellar interaction and the explosion itself.

TL;DR: EP260321a是爱因斯坦探针（EP）发现的最邻近（z=0.0343）的快速X射线暂现源（FXT），也是首个被确认为X射线"激波突破"（SBO）发射的EP-FXT。其对应的Ic型超新星SN 2026gzf是首个具备明确X射线SBO信号的Ic-BL超新星。多波段观测排除了高能星周喷流可能性，光学数据将SN 2026gzf置于Ic-BL超新星典型性质范围内，而最佳光变曲线拟合来自星周物质相互作用与$^{56}$Ni放射性衰变的联合模型。基于ZTF巡天速率推断，EP探测SBO的预期速率（4.4-16/年）与当前实际探测率在90%置信水平上不一致，暗示大部分Ic-BL超新星的X射线SBO信号比EP260321a更暗弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EP260321a作为EP发现的最邻近FXT且首次提供与X射线激波突破（SBO）预期强匹配的事件，其多波段后续观测对于理解Ic-BL超新星的早期X射线发射机制、激波突破物理以及此类事件的宇宙探测率具有重要意义。

Method: 对EP260321a/SN 2026gzf进行多波段（射电、光学）后随观测；利用射电数据约束喷流动能和各向同性等效能量；利用光学数据拟合五个不同的物理光变模型（CSM相互作用、$^{56}$Ni衰变等）；基于ZTF Bright Transient Survey中Ic-BL超新星发生率推断EP探测SBO的预期速率并与实际探测率比较。

Result: 射电观测（5.8-54.5天）排除了各向同性等效动能$E_K > 10^{49}$ erg的星周喷流（星周密度$n > 10^{-2}~{\rm cm}^{-3}$）。SN 2026gzf的光学性质全部落在Ic-BL超新星90%置信区间内。最佳光变曲线拟合来自CSM相互作用与$^{56}$Ni放射性衰变的联合模型。推断EP探测SBO预期速率为4.4-16/年，与当前实际探测率在90%置信水平上不一致。

Conclusion: SN 2026gzf是首个具有明确X射线SBO的Ic-BL超新星；联合CSM相互作用和$^{56}$Ni衰变模型最佳描述其光变；大多数Ic-BL超新星产生的X射线SBO信号可能比EP260321a更暗弱，这解释了预期速率与实际探测率之间的差异。

Abstract: In March 2026, the Einstein Probe (EP) discovered its most nearby (z = 0.0343) Fast X-ray Transient (FXT), EP260321a, the first EP FXT to provide a strong match to expectations for X-ray ''shock breakout'' (SBO) emission. Here, we present our multi-wavelength follow-up campaign of EP260321a and its broad-line Type Ic (Ic-BL) supernova (SN) counterpart, SN 2026gzf, the first Type Ic-BL SN with a definitive X-ray SBO. We show that our radio follow-up extending over 5.8 - 54.5 days post-FXT rules out an on-axis jet counterpart of isotropic-equivalent kinetic energy $E_{K} > 10^{49}$ erg for circumburst densities $n > 10^{-2}~{\rm cm}^{-3}$ and constrains radio synchrotron emission from the fastest-moving SN ejecta. In addition, we derive the properties of SN 2026gzf and its host galaxy from our well-sampled optical data and compare them with those of optically discovered Type Ic-BL SNe, finding that SN2026gzf is well within the 90% confidence interval across all properties. We further fit SN 2026gzf's light curve with five different physical models, and determine that combined emission from both interaction with circumstellar material (CSM) and $^{56}$Ni radioactive decay provides the best fit with plausible model parameters. Finally, using the rate of Ic-BL SNe from the ZTF Bright Transient Survey and assuming all Type Ic-BL SNe produce EP260321a-like FXTs, we infer an expected rate of EP-detected SBOs of 4.4 - 16 year$^{-1}$. This is inconsistent at the 90% confidence level with current EP detection rates, potentially indicating that most Type Ic-BL SNe produce less luminous X-ray SBO signals compared to EP 260321a.

</details>


### [117] [Limits on primordial black holes from the extragalactic gamma-ray background; current status and future projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10013)
*Ilias Cholis,Iason Krommydas,John Carlini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用Fermi、EGRET和COMPTEL观测的河外伽马射线背景（0.5 MeV-1 TeV），结合多类天体物理源（耀变体、星暴星系、射电星系）和超高能宇宙射线的贡献，以及原初黑洞霍金辐射（包括直接辐射、强子化、末态辐射、正负电子对湮灭等过程），对质量在10¹⁴-10¹⁷ g范围内的原初黑洞丰度给出了目前最严格的限制，超越了其他间接暗物质探测手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞（PBHs）作为早期宇宙密度扰动塌缩的产物，若质量约为10¹⁴ g，其霍金辐射寿命与宇宙年龄相当，可能对观测到的MeV-GeV伽马射线通量有显著贡献。通过精确计算PBHs的伽马射线辐射谱，并利用多波段观测数据构建可靠的河外伽马射线背景（EGRB）模型，可以对其丰度施加迄今最严格的限制。

Method: (1) 使用Fermi-LAT、EGRET和COMPTEL在0.5 MeV-1 TeV能段的EGRB观测数据；(2) 构建包含耀变体、星暴星系、射电星系以及超高能宇宙射线与红外背景相互作用产生的伽马射线的EGRB模型，并利用Fermi点源目录及X射线、可见光、红外、射电等多波段观测数据约束各类源的贡献；(3) 考虑PBHs霍金辐射的完整伽马射线发射过程：直接辐射、强子化与不稳定粒子衰变产生的伽马射线、末态辐射（FSR）以及星际介质中正负电子对湮灭产生的伽马射线；(4) 分别研究单色质量分布和扩展质量分布的PBHs，通过比较理论预测与观测数据导出丰度上限。

Result: 由于末态辐射和正电子湮灭增强了PBHs伽马射线谱的低能部分，EGRB观测能够在10¹⁴-10¹⁷ g质量范围内给出比所有其他间接暗物质探针更严格的PBHs丰度限制，成为该质量区间内最强有力的约束手段。

Conclusion: EGRB观测是限制10¹⁴-10¹⁷ g质量范围原初黑洞丰度的最有效途径，对PBHs作为暗物质候选者的参数空间施加了重要的约束。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs), possibly formed from the collapse of early universe perturbations, will evaporate via Hawking radiation with a lifetime comparable to the age of the universe, if their mass is $O(10^{14})$ g. Such black holes can contribute to the observed gamma-ray fluxes in the MeV and GeV range. Using the observed extragalactic gamma-ray background (EGRB) from the \textit{Fermi} Large Area Telescope, the \textit{EGRET}, and the \textit{COMPTEL} telescopes that cover gamma-ray energies from 0.5 MeV to 1 TeV, we evaluate limits on the abundance of PBHs with masses of $10^{14}$ to $10^{17}$ g. We study both monochromatic and extended mass distributions of PBHs. To model the EGRB spectrum, we calculate the contribution from extragalactic sources including blazars, star-forming galaxies and radio galaxies and also account for ultra-high-energy cosmic rays that produce gamma rays when interacting with the infrared background. Our EGRB modeling uses information from the \textit{Fermi} gamma-ray point sources catalog, from observations at X-rays, the visible spectrum, the infrared and radio waves, and also accounts for modeling uncertainties and variations on the properties within each class of these sources. Moreover, we use recent work on the modeling of the PBHs' gamma-ray emission, that includes the direct Hawking radiation, gamma rays produced in the hadronization and decay of unstable particles, final state radiation and gamma rays from pair annihilations in the interstellar medium. As the contribution of final state radiation and the annihilation of positrons enhances the low-energy part of the produced gamma-ray spectra from PBHs, we find that the EGRB observations can set the tightest limits on their abundance among all indirect dark matter probes, within the mass range of interest.[abridged]

</details>
