Sahu,Aditya Pawan Saikia,Jeremy Sanders,Liming Song,Olga Spiridonova,Ning-Chen Sun,Shengli Sun,Xiaojin Sun,Yuyin Tan,Aishwarya Linesh Thakur,Samaporn Tinyanont,Valery Vlasyuk,A. V. Volnova,Ailing Wang,Hong Wu,Qianrui Wu,Haitao Xu,Zelin Xu,Changbin Xue,Yi-Han Iris Yin,I. A. Zaznobin,Jia-Sen Zhang,Shuang-Nan Zhang,Songbo Zhang,Yu Zhang,Zipei Zhu,Zecheng Zou,Bing Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了快速X射线瞬变源EP260321a的发现，其后伴随一颗宽线Ic型超新星（SN Ic-BL）出现。其X射线发射为软谱且可用黑体辐射完美建模，被确认为真正的激波突破（SBO）事件。观测到的长持续时间和高总能量输出表明激波是从约300倍太阳半径的周围壳层突破，而非恒星表面，这为恒星核心坍缩前一个月内发生的剧烈质量抛射提供了直接证据。实时探测SBO可精确定位恒星核心坍缩时刻，有利于搜寻中微子和引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量恒星死亡过程中的激波突破事件（SBO）携带爆炸能量学、前身星性质及其周围环境的关键信息，但由于其持续时间极短且历史上缺乏灵敏的宽视场监测仪器，仅有两次SBO事件在X射线波段被探测到，且其发射谱受复杂物理因素影响偏离简单热辐射形式。因此，寻找并分析真正的SBO事件对于理解大质量恒星如何终结生命至关重要。

Method: 利用灵敏的宽视场X射线监测发现EP260321a快速X射线瞬变源，随后进行光学/紫外等多波段后随观测，识别出其关联的SN Ic-BL超新星。对X射线数据进行能谱分析（黑体辐射拟合）和光变曲线建模，推断激波突破的物理参数（半径、能量、持续时间），并结合超新星性质约束前身星特征。

Result: EP260321a的X射线辐射为软X射线且可被黑体辐射完美建模，确认为真正的激波突破事件。其持续时间异常长、总能量输出大，表明激波并非从恒星表面突破，而是从约300倍太阳半径的周围壳层突破。该壳层是前身星（Wolf-Rayet星，氢氦包层被剥离）在核坍缩前约一个月内剧烈抛射物质形成的。

Conclusion: 这项发现提供了大质量恒星在核心坍缩前存在剧烈预爆活动的直接观测证据（即一个月内发生突然的质量抛射）。实时探测SBO能精确定位恒星核心坍缩时刻，为高效搜寻伴随的中微子和引力波信号创造了条件，结合及时的多波段观测，有望揭示大质量恒星如何结束生命这一未解之谜。

Abstract: Massive stars die as energetic supernova explosions, but the physical processes during and before such explosions are poorly studied observationally. The first electromagnetic signals from core-collapse events are predicted to be a flash of soft X-ray and ultraviolet (UV) light, produced as a result of a shock wave breaking out of the star and its surrounding medium. Such shock breakout (SBO) events often carry essential information about the explosion energetics, the progenitor star, and its immediate environment. However, they are difficult to catch because of their very short durations and a historical lack of sensitive wide-field monitors. Only two SBO events have been detected so far in X-rays, but their emission spectra are modified from the simple thermal form by complicated physical factors, however. Here we report the discovery of a fast X-ray transient, EP260321a, followed by a broad-lined Type Ic supernova (SN Ic-BL) emerging days later, suggesting its progenitor as a Wolf-Rayet star with its hydrogen and helium envelopes stripped. Its X-ray emission is soft and best modeled by blackbody radiation, making it a bona fide SBO. The observed long duration and large total energy output of the X-ray event jointly indicate a shock breaking out from a surrounding shell at a radius of about 300 solar radii, rather than from the progenitor star's surface. This provides direct evidence of abrupt mass ejection within a month prior to core collapse, suggesting intense pre-explosion activity for a massive star. The real-time detection of SBOs yields precise timing of stellar core-collapse, allowing for efficient searches for associated neutrinos and potential gravitational-wave signals. These, together with timely multi-wavelength observations, may uncover how massive stars end their lives.

</details>


### [119] [Refined anti-proton and anti-deuteron fluxes from weak-scale Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10017)
*Marco Cirelli,Mattia Di Mauro,Arpan Kar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于更新后的银河系传播模型（SLIM/BIG/QUAINT方案下的MIN/MED/MAX参数集）和CosmiXs提供的改进能谱，计算了弱相互作用暗物质粒子（质量从几GeV到100 TeV）在银河系湮灭或衰变产生的反质子和反氘核宇宙线通量。研究采用了半解析方法处理传播效应，考虑了NFW、Einasto和Burkert暗物质晕分布，并纳入了基于ALICE数据的最新非弹性截面模型。结果显示，新传播模型下的通量在MIN-MAX变化范围内比旧模型（如PPPC4DMID）更稳健，并讨论了这在提升暗物质信号发现潜力方面的影响。所有星际通量结果以表格形式（动能0.1 GeV-100 TeV）公开于CosmiXsPPPC的GitHub仓库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗物质间接探测研究中使用的宇宙线反物质通量计算（如PPPC4DMID）基于旧版传播模型，其不确定性较大。需要基于更新后的传播参数、暗物质晕轮廓和实验截面数据，提供更精确、稳健的反质子和反氘核通量，以提升暗物质信号的发现潜力。

Method: 采用半解析方法处理银河系传播，包括空间扩散、能量损失、对流和扩散再加速；考虑MIN/MED/MAX三组传播参数（SLIM/BIG/QUAINT方案）；使用NFW、Einasto、Burkert三种暗物质密度轮廓；基于CosmiXs提供改进的反质子和反氘核产生能谱，并纳入基于ALICE数据的最新非弹性截面模型。

Result: 新传播模型下的宇宙线通量在MIN-MAX变化范围内的稳健性显著优于旧版PPPC4DMID结果；提供了动能0.1 GeV-100 TeV范围内暗物质诱导的星际通量表格式数据，并发布于CosmiXsPPPC的GitHub仓库。

Conclusion: 更新后的传播模型和输入参数显著降低了暗物质诱导反物质通量的理论不确定性，有助于提升当前和未来宇宙线观测对暗物质信号的发现潜力。所有计算结果已公开，可直接用于暗物质间接搜索研究。

Abstract: We provide the cosmic-ray (CR) fluxes of antiprotons and antideuterons produced by the Galactic annihilation or decay of weak-scale dark matter (DM) particles of masses in the range from a few GeV to 100 TeV. We estimate these fluxes based on the updated models for the propagation of charged particles in the Galaxy and using the improved $\bar{p}$ and $\bar{d}$ spectra provided by $\texttt{CosmiXs}$. For the updated propagation models we consider the MIN/MED/MAX sets under the new SLIM/BIG/QUAINT schemes. We treat the Galactic propagation in a semi-analytic way including different possible effects such as spatial diffusion, energy-losses, convection and diffusive reacceleration. For the DM distribution in the Galactic halo we consider NFW, Einasto and Burkert profiles with the most updated parameters. Moreover, we also incorporate the latest models for the inelastic cross-sections of $\bar{p}$ and $\bar{d}$ based on ALICE data. We validate our calculations with those available in the literature or those obtained from other publicly available numerical packages. We compare the CR fluxes obtained in this work with those provided previously by $\texttt{PPPC4DMID}$ which were based on old propagation scenarios. We find that the CR fluxes obtained here with the new propagation models are much more robust (compared to the older ones) under the variation MIN - MAX. We also discuss the impact of this in the improvement of the discovery potential of a possible DM signal in the light of the present and upcoming CR observations. We provide all our results for the DM-induced interstellar CR fluxes in a tabulated format (for the kinetic energy range 0.1 GeV - 100 TeV) in the $\texttt{GitHub}$ repository of the newly created $\href{https://github.com/CosmiXsPPPC}{\texttt{CosmiXsPPPC}}$ project. The results are ready to be used for studies related to DM indirect searches.

</details>


### [120] [Internal constitution of the outer crust of non-accreted neutron stars and magnetars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10118)
*Juliette Servais,Nicolas Chamel*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文开发了magcrust程序，能够快速计算磁星外壳层在完整天体物理相关磁场强度范围（10^13-10^16 G）内的组成，计算速度相比传统方法提升10^3-10^7倍，且精度保持在百分之几以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星外壳层组成对其天体物理表现（如巨耀斑喷出物中的r过程核合成模拟）至关重要。然而传统方法计算成本高，现有迭代方法要么限于无磁场物质，要么限于强量子化磁场，无法高效处理观测磁星的中间磁场强度范围。

Method: 通过求解界面处近似平衡条件计算相邻壳层间的跃迁，利用爱因斯坦广义相对论方程的近似解估算核素丰度和壳层深度。程序覆盖17个核质量表及10^13-10^16 G范围内1300个磁场强度点。

Result: 与详细数值计算对比，跃迁性质的相对偏差保持在百分之几以内，壳层组成得到良好再现。计算时间相比传统方法降低10^3-10^7倍。

Conclusion: magcrust为完整天体物理相关磁场强度范围内磁星外壳层分层研究提供了鲁棒且高效的工具，其计算速度使其适用于系统性计算，包括敏感性分析、不确定性量化和系综研究。

Abstract: Context. Determining the internal constitution of the outer crust of magnetars is important for interpreting several of their astrophysical manifestations. In particular, the crustal composition is a key input for simulations of r-process nucleosynthesis in giant flare ejecta. However, traditional methods are computationally expensive, limiting their use in large-scale studies. Although faster iterative approaches exist, they are restricted to unmagnetized matter and strongly quantizing magnetic fields, leaving the intermediate field strengths characteristic of observed magnetars without an efficient treatment.
  Aims. We developed the program magcrust to extend these existing iterative approaches, enabling the rapid computation of the outer-crust composition of cold, non-accreted magnetars over the full range of the magnetic-field strengths inferred for these objects.
  Methods. Transitions between adjacent crustal layers are computed by solving approximate equilibrium conditions at the interface. Nuclear abundances and layer depths are estimated from approximate solutions of Einstein's equations of general relativity.
  Results. The performance and accuracy of the program were assessed against detailed numerical calculations. Relative deviations from exact transition properties remain within a few percent, and crustal compositions are well reproduced across 17 nuclear mass tables and 1300 magnetic-field strengths from 1E13 to 1E16 G. Computation times are reduced by factors of 1E3-1E7 compared to traditional approaches.
  Conclusions. This program provides a robust and efficient tool for determining the stratification of magnetars' outer crust over the full range of astrophysically relevant magnetic-field strengths. Its computational speed makes it well suited to systematic calculations, including sensitivity analyses, uncertainty quantification, and ensemble studies.

</details>


### [121] [Radio precursors of monster shocks: a mechanism for fast radio bursts from SGR 1935+2154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10189)
*Andrei M. Beloborodov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出活动磁星中的千赫兹扰动在约10^8 cm半径处演变为强辐射激波，产生X射线和半相干射电前驱体。该前驱体与激波前方磁层等离子体相互作用并自调节其发射，自洽频率落在GHz波段，在激波膨胀至约10^9 cm时发射达到峰值。产生的GHz暴持续亚毫秒，能量约10^34 erg。该暴传播至光圆柱时可能被磁层吸收，只有在局部等离子体密度比典型活跃磁星低约30倍时才能逃逸。激波X射线比射电波延迟毫秒尺度。SGR 1935+2154中约10^38 erg能量的千赫兹扰动产生的X射线和射电暴与观测活动类似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释活动磁星中千赫兹扰动产生的射电暴（尤其是快速射电暴）的物理机制，特别是射电前驱体的自洽频率、光度及其与磁层等离子体的相互作用，以及这类射电暴能否逃逸至远距离观测者的问题。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，研究活动磁星中千赫兹扰动演变为强辐射激波的过程，分析激波产生的半相干射电前驱体与前方磁层等离子体的相互作用及其自调节机制，推导前驱体的自洽频率和光度，并评估射电暴传播至光圆柱时的吸收与逃逸条件。

Result: 1) 射电前驱体频率在GHz波段，在激波膨胀至约10^9 cm时发射峰值；2) GHz暴持续亚毫秒，能量约10^34 erg；3) 射电暴逃逸需光圆柱处等离子体密度比典型活跃磁星低约30倍；4) 激波X射线比射电波延迟毫秒尺度；5) SGR 1935+2154的活动可用约10^38 erg能量的千赫兹扰动解释。

Conclusion: 活动磁星中的千赫兹扰动可通过激波过程产生类似快速射电暴的GHz射电暴和伴随X射线暴，但射电暴的逃逸需较幸运的磁层密度条件，这解释了为何此类事件较为罕见。该模型自洽地解释了SGR 1935+2154的观测特征。

Abstract: Kilohertz perturbations in active magnetars evolve into monster radiative shocks at radii $r\sim 10^8$ cm. The shock generates X-rays and a semi-coherent radio precursor, which strongly interacts with the magnetospheric plasma ahead of the shock. We show that this interaction self-regulates the precursor emission and find its self-consistent frequency and luminosity. The precursor frequency falls in the GHz band and its production peaks when the shock expands to $r\approx 10^9$ cm. The resulting GHz burst has a sub-millisecond duration and energy ${\cal E}_{\rm FRB}\approx 10^{34}{\cal E}_{38}^{0.2}$ erg where ${\cal E}$ is the energy of the primary magnetosonic disturbance that launched the shock. As the GHz burst propagates to the light cylinder $R_{\rm LC}\sim 10^{10}$ cm, it faces a threat of being absorbed by the magnetosphere. The burst escapes if the local plasma density at $R_{\rm LC}$ is $\sim 30$ times lower than typically expected for active magnetars, so distant observers need some luck to see the radio burst. The shock X-rays follow the radio waves with a millisecond delay. Shocks from kilohertz disturbances with energies ${\cal E}\sim 10^{38}$ erg generate X-ray and radio bursts similar to the activity detected in SGR 1935+2154.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用HAWC天文台7.5年的M87观测数据，通过光子-轴子混合机制间接搜索轴子暗物质候选者，未发现信号，在质量范围约10^{-8}至10^{-6}eV、耦合常数高于5×10^{-12}GeV^{-1}的参数空间设置了竞争性排除限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子类粒子（ALP）是重要的暗物质候选者，光子-ALP混合机制为利用甚高能伽马射线观测约束其质量和耦合常数提供了独特渠道。

Method: 利用HAWC天文台7.5年的观测数据，以M87为源，通过分析光子-ALP混合对河外伽马射线谱的衰减改变效应进行间接搜索。

Result: 未发现光子-ALP转换信号，在质量约10^{-8}至10^{-6}eV、耦合常数高于5×10^{-12}GeV^{-1}的参数空间设置了排除区域，与之前结果一致。

Conclusion: 该分析提供了对ALP参数空间的竞争性约束，补充了其他伽马射线天文台的先前限制。

Abstract: In this work, we perform an indirect search for axion-like particles (ALPs) through their hypothesized mixing with photons in the presence of magnetic fields. ALPs are a well-motivated dark-matter candidate class, and the photon-ALP conversion mechanism provides a unique channel to constrain their mass and coupling constant using very-high-energy gamma-ray observations. The photon-ALP mixing could alter the observed gamma-ray spectrum from extragalactic sources by effectively reducing the apparent attenuation due to extragalactic-background-light absorption. We analyze 7.5 years of data from the High Altitude Water Cherenkov (HAWC) Observatory, targeting the nearby radio galaxy M87. This source is located within the Virgo cluster and is an ideal environment for photon-ALP conversion due to its low redshift and the large-scale, strongly magnetized medium of the cluster. We find no evidence for a photon-ALP conversion signal and, consequently, set constraints on the ALP mass and photon-ALP coupling constant with emission from M87 which are consistent with previous results. Our analysis places competitive constraints on the ALP parameter space, defining an exclusion region in the mass range of approximately $10^{-8}$ to $10^{-6}$ eV for coupling constants above $5\times10^{-12}$ GeV$^{-1}$, complementing previous constraints from other gamma-ray observatories.

</details>


### [123] [Gamma-Ray Emission from the Crab Pulsar: A 17-Year Fermi-LAT Reanalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10519)
*Liancheng Zhou,Yun-Lu Gong,Jun Fang,Li Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于17年费米LAT数据（2008-2025）对蟹状星云脉冲星进行重分析，利用Jodrell Bank射电星历表对齐相位，在100 MeV-300 GeV能段获得脉冲轮廓，发现P1贡献随能量下降而P2和Bridge贡献上升（P2在>10 GeV超过P1），并在100 MeV-1 TeV的off-pulse区间确认了同步辐射和逆康普顿分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用更长时间跨度（17年）的费米LAT数据对蟹状星云脉冲星进行重分析，以更精确地研究其高能脉冲辐射特性，特别是不同相位成分（P1、P2、Bridge）随能量的演化行为。

Method: 使用Jodrell Bank射电星历表按月分配脉冲相位并对齐相位零点；在100 MeV-300 GeV能段提取脉冲轮廓；在100 MeV-30 GeV能段进行14个相位分箱的相位分辨似然分析；并在100 MeV-1 TeV进行off-pulse分析。

Result: 脉冲发射在10-20 GeV和20-30 GeV显著（H检验32.36σ和11.59σ），但在30-300 GeV不显著；P1的固定窗口分数流量随能量稳定下降，而P2和Bridge增加，P2在>10 GeV超过P1；off-pulse区间确认了同步辐射和逆康普顿分量。

Conclusion: 长达17年的数据分析揭示了蟹状星云脉冲星不同相位成分随能量的系统演化趋势，证明了P2在较高能段的主导地位，并验证了off-pulse区间辐射的物理起源。

Abstract: We present a reanalysis of 17 years of Fermi Large Area Telescope (LAT) observations of the Crab pulsar obtained between 2008 August and 2025 August. Using monthly Jodrell Bank radio ephemerides, we assigned pulse phases to the LAT events and aligned the phase zero across the full data set. From this phase-aligned data set, we derived pulse profiles over 100 MeV to 300 GeV. The pulsed emission remains clearly detectable in the 10 to 20 GeV and 20 to 30 GeV bands, with H-test significances of 32.36 sigma and 11.59 sigma, respectively, but is not significantly detected in the 30 to 300 GeV band. Phase-resolved likelihood analysis was performed over 100 MeV to 30 GeV using 14 phase bins with comparable pulsed statistics. The fixed-window fractional fluxes show that the contribution of Peak 1 (P1) decreases steadily with energy, while those of Peak 2 (P2) and the Bridge increase, with P2 exceeding P1 above 10 GeV. Finally, the same phase-assignment framework also enables an off-pulse analysis from 100 MeV to 1 TeV, confirming the synchrotron and inverse-Compton components that dominate the emission in the selected off-pulse interval.

</details>


### [124] [Circular polarization effects induced by photon-axion mixing in astrophysical environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10527)
*Meng Wang,Nan Ding,Qiusheng Gu,Yunyong Tang,Rui Xue*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究光子-轴子混合在外部磁场中诱导的圆偏振信号，在单域近似下推导了圆偏振度P_C的解析表达式，对比了四种天体物理环境中的能谱特征，并以耀变体S4 0954+65为例，利用光学圆偏振上限将轴子-光子耦合常数约束到g_{aγγ} ≤ 5×10^{-12} GeV^{-1}（m_a ~ 10^{-16}–10^{-10} eV），证明圆偏振可作为轴子搜索的互补探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子和类轴子粒子是暗物质及超出标准模型新物理的重要候选者。相比于光谱和线性偏振方法，圆偏振受天体物理背景污染更低，可作为独立的轴子探测手段，但目前缺乏系统的理论框架和天体物理应用研究。

Method: 在手征基框架下研究光子-轴子混合，采用单域近似解析求解演化方程，推导圆偏振度P_C的表达式（涵盖共振、强耦合和弱耦合区域）。在单域磁场模型中比较四种天体物理环境（AGN喷流、星系团内介质、星系际介质、银河磁场）的能量依赖圆偏振，并以耀变体S4 0954+65为例，考虑随机磁畴中相位积累对圆偏振的影响。

Result: X射线至MeV波段是最灵敏的观测窗口。利用S4 0954+65的光学圆偏振上限P_C < 0.184%，将g_{aγγ}约束在≤ 5×10^{-12} GeV^{-1}（m_a ~ 10^{-16}–10^{-10} eV），在m_a ~ 10^{-14} eV处约束最强。

Conclusion: 圆偏振可作为互补的轴子探针，为轴子搜索提供独立于光谱和线性偏振的新途径。

Abstract: Axions and axion-like particles (ALPs) are compelling candidates for dark matter and new physics beyond the Standard Model. Photon-axion mixing in external magnetic fields modifies the photon energy spectrum and linear polarization state, and also induces circular polarization signals. Compared to spectral and linear polarization methods, circular polarization benefits from lower astrophysical background contamination, providing an independent probe for axion searches. In this work, we study the circular polarization induced by photon-axion mixing within the chiral basis framework. By analytically solving the evolution equations under the single-domain approximation, we derive an expression for the circular polarization degree P_C, applicable in the resonant, strong coupling, and weak coupling regimes. Within single-domain magnetic field models, we compare the energy-dependent circular polarization in four astrophysical environments (AGN jets, intracluster medium, intergalactic medium, and Galactic magnetic fields). We find that the X-ray to MeV band represents the most sensitive observational window. Using the blazar S4 0954+65 as a case study, phase accumulation in random magnetic domains causes the circular polarization degree to fluctuate with redshift and exhibit pronounced energy structures. Using the optical circular polarization upper limit P_C < 0.184% from this source, we constrain g_{aγγ} <= 5 x 10^{-12} GeV^{-1} for m_a ~ 10^{-16}--10^{-10} eV, with the strongest constraint near m_a ~ 10^{-14} eV. These results establish circular polarization as a complementary axion probe.

</details>


### [125] [Updating the PATH framework with FRB host galaxy models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10538)
*C. W. James,N. Loudas,M. Woodland,B. C. Andersen,J. X. Prochaska,J. L. Hoffmann,L. Marnoch,S. D. Ryder*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文扩展了FRB宿主星系概率关联框架PATH，引入基于物理动机的宿主星系星等先验分布模型，并使用32个ASKAP/CRAFT调查的FRB数据拟合模型参数。结果表明，新的先验提高了宿主星系识别的置信度，证实了真实FRB宿主星系比恒星形成加权分布更暗（p值0.12%），但质量加权分布的拟合更差（p值10^{-9}）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PATH方法使用简化的宿主星系可观测性先验分布，缺乏基于物理的动机。本文旨在通过引入基于FRB宿主星系红移和色散测量物理关系的先验模型，提高宿主星系识别的准确性和置信度。

Method: 扩展PATH框架，构建三种基于FRB宿主星系红移的r波段星等分布模型P(m_r|z)，结合基于色散测量的红移先验P(z|DM)，利用32个ASKAP ICS模式的FRB宿主候选星系拟合模型参数。

Result: 新先验提高了所有ASKAP ICS FRB宿主星系识别的置信度；在低红移(z~0.1)处三种模型预测一致；真实FRB宿主星系分布显著暗于恒星形成加权分布(p=0.12%)，质量加权分布拟合更差(p=10^{-9})。

Conclusion: 需要更多z>0.5的高红移FRB样本以及考虑宿主金属丰度等扩展模型，以进一步区分不同先验模型并解决FRB宿主分布的不确定性。

Abstract: Over a hundred fast radio burst (FRB) host galaxies have now been identified, enabling both comparisons of host redshift with FRB dispersion measure to study the cosmological distribution of ionised gas, and analyses of host properties in order to identify FRB progenitors. The standard method for determining the most likely FRB host galaxy in an optical image is the Bayesian framework Probabilistic Association of Transients to their Hosts (PATH), which accounts for uncertainties in the radio localisation, and simplified prior distributions on the host being observable. In this work we extend PATH, incorporating physically-motivated priors that are based on expectations about FRB host galaxy magnitudes. We develop three different models for the apparent r-band magnitude distribution based on an FRB's expected host galaxy redshift, $P(m_r|z)$ and combine these with expectations for redshift based on an FRB's dispersion measure, $P(z|DM)$. We fit the parameters of these prior models using host galaxy candidates for 32 FRBs detected by the Australian SKA Pathfinder (ASKAP) in incoherent sum (ICS) mode by the Commensal Real-time ASKAP Fast Transients (CRAFT) survey.
  Employing PATH with the new priors on the host magnitudes, we find increased confidence in the most probable hosts of all ASKAP ICS FRB host galaxies. All three models predict similar distributions of FRB host magnitudes at low redshift $(z \sim 0.1)$, and we confirm previous results that the true FRB host galaxy distribution is fainter than expected for a star-formation-weighted distribution (p-value of 0.12%). However, a mass-weighted distribution provides an even worse fit (p-value of $10^{-9}$). Tests against more FRBs in the $z > 0.5$ range, where the models differ, and extensions of the models to account for e.g. host metallicity, may help to resolve these uncertainties in the FRB host distribution.

</details>


### [126] [Discovery of EP J175257.3-351923 as a Candidate Black Hole Low-Mass X-ray Binary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10566)
*G. L. Huang,Q. C. Zhao,L. Tao,A. Coleiro,A. Rau,S. Brennan,C . Y. Dai,R. Soria,F. Cangemi,F. Coti Zelati,A. Marino,L. Zhang,S. Guillot,H. Q. Cheng,H. Feng,D. Götz,Y. Huang,Y. F. Huang,D. Y. Li,Z. S. Li,P. Maggi,R. C. Ma,X. Ma,H. W. Pan,N. Rea,J. Wang,Q. Y. Wu,L. P. Xin,W. M. Yuan,Z. H. Yao,G. B. Zhang,W. D. Zhang,S. N. Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 爱因斯坦探针（EP）在银道面附近发现X射线暂现源EP J175257.3--351923（EP250916a），爆发持续≥40天，峰值流量~4×10⁻¹⁰ erg cm⁻² s⁻¹，呈现快速上升指数衰减（FRED）轮廓。该源始终处于硬态，光子指数~1.6–2.2，未发生谱态跃迁。宽带能谱建模表明存在截断吸积盘、弱反射成分和高能截止（~217 keV），符合黑洞系统硬态吸积特征。未探测到可靠光学对应体及脉动信号，X射线-光学流量比ξ≥21.75支持低质量伴星。该源被认定为黑洞低质量X射线双星候选体，展示了EP发现隐藏X射线双星群体的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用爱因斯坦探针（EP）在银道面附近寻找并发现新的X射线暂现源，以揭示此前未被探测到的隐藏X射线双星群体。

Method: 使用爱因斯坦探针（EP）进行X射线巡天观测，结合Swift/XRT、SVOM/VT、Swift/UVOT和GROND等多波段后随观测，通过时间分析（光变曲线轮廓）、能谱分析（幂律谱拟合、宽带建模）以及光学对应体搜寻来表征该暂现源的性质。

Result: 发现EP J175257.3--351923，爆发持续≥40天，FRED轮廓，硬态光谱（Γ≈1.6–2.2），截断盘+弱反射+高能截止~217 keV的宽带谱特征，未检测到脉动或显著非周期变，ξ≥21.75。

Conclusion: EP J175257.3--351923是一个黑洞低质量X射线双星候选体，该发现展示了EP在银道面附近发现此前隐藏的暗弱X射线双星群体的能力。

Abstract: We report the discovery of a new X-ray transient, EP~J175257.3--351923 (EP250916a), by the \textit{Einstein Probe} (EP) near the Galactic plane. The outburst lasted for at least $\gtrsim 40$~days, reached a peak 2--10 keV flux of $\sim 4 \times 10^{-10}$~erg~cm$^{-2}$~s$^{-1}$, and exhibited a fast-rise, exponential-decay (FRED) profile typical of X-ray binary outbursts. The source remained in the hard state throughout the outburst, with only modest variations in the photon index ($\sim 1.6$--$2.2$) and no evidence for a spectral state transition. Broadband spectral modeling suggests a truncated disk, a weak reflection component, and a high-energy cutoff at $\sim 217$~keV, consistent with hard-state accretion in black-hole systems. No reliable optical counterpart is detected within the Swift/XRT error circle in SVOM/VT, Swift/UVOT and GROND observations, and the inferred X-ray-to-optical flux ratio, $ξ\gtrsim 21.75$, is consistent with a low-mass companion. No pulsations or significant aperiodic variability are detected. Although the compact object cannot yet be firmly identified, the timing, spectral, and optical evidence favors EP~J175257.3--351923 as a black-hole low-mass X-ray binary candidate, highlighting EP's potential to uncover a faint, previously hidden population of X-ray binaries.

</details>


### [127] [Hubble as a Unique Discovery Engine of the Fate of Massive Stars and Black Hole Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10710)
*Avishai Gilkis,Eva Laplace,Charles D. Kilpatrick,Maria R. Drout,Anna J. G. O'Grady,Christopher A. Tout*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文指出恒星质量黑洞的形成机制尚未明确，提出许多黑洞可能由高温、紫外亮的大质量恒星（如沃尔夫-拉叶型前身星）形成，而非传统认为的低温超巨星。哈勃空间望远镜（HST）因其独特的紫外灵敏度、亚角秒成像和长周期连续性，是直接搜寻这类消失热大质量恒星的关键工具。作者规划了HST在2030年代的路线图，通过大规模巡天项目在近邻星系中识别候选消失恒星，预计每年可探测到约一次黑洞形成事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星质量黑洞的形成机制是天体物理学中一个悬而未决的问题，观测约束极为有限。目前不清楚哪些类型的恒星更可能产生黑洞，形成过程是否伴随强或弱的电磁暂现事件（甚至完全不产生），这成为大质量恒星演化谜题中缺失的关键环节。

Method: 提出利用HST独特的紫外灵敏度、亚角秒成像和长达十年的连续性，通过对近邻星系进行紫外波段重新成像的大规模巡天项目，结合互补巡天识别的低光度暂现源，搜寻候选消失恒星（即黑洞形成事件）。

Result: 理论事件率表明，HST可及的近邻星系样本预计每年可探测到约一次可观测的黑洞形成消失事件。

Conclusion: 将HST的运行延长至2030年代，将为解决黑洞形成这一未解难题提供关键洞察。虽然Rubin/LSST、Roman、JWST等将在时域天文学带来重大进展，但没有任何一项任务能同时具备紫外灵敏度、亚角秒成像和十年以上的连续性，HST在此领域具有不可替代性。

Abstract: How stellar-mass black holes are formed is an open question in astrophysics, with very limited observational constraints. It is not known which types of stars are more likely to produce black holes, and whether the formation process is accompanied by strong or weak electromagnetic transients - or none at all - and this issue remains a critical missing piece in the puzzle of the fate of massive stars. Recent theoretical work predicts that many stellar-mass black holes form from hot, UV-luminous massive stars, including Wolf-Rayet-like progenitors, and searches focused primarily on luminous cool supergiants may therefore miss a substantial fraction of black-hole formation events. While the coming decade will bring major advances in time-domain astronomy through Rubin/LSST, Roman, JWST, and wide-field transient surveys, none of these combines UV sensitivity, sub-arcsecond imaging, and decade-long continuity. HST uniquely enables direct searches for disappearing hot massive stars associated with black-hole formation. We outline a roadmap for extending HST's role in this area into the 2030s through a dedicated, large program to re-image nearby galaxies in the UV and identify candidate disappearing stars and unusual low-luminosity transients identified by complementary surveys. Theoretical event rates imply that the nearby galaxy population accessible to HST should yield of order one detectable black-hole-forming disappearance event per year. Extending HST operations into the 2030s would therefore provide crucial insights into the unsolved problem of black hole formation.

</details>


### [128] [Potential detection of ~ 4.2 keV emission line from GRS 1747-312](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10739)
*Amom Lanchenbi Chanu,Akash Garg,Ranjeev Misra,A. Senorita Devi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用AstroSat约40 ks数据，对中子星LMXB GRS 1747-312在2017年爆发衰变阶段进行宽带光谱分析，发现4.2 keV发射线可能源自中子星表面的反射铁线引力红移（z~0.6），为直接测量引力红移、约束质径比及致密物质物态方程提供了潜在手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过对中子星低质量X射线双星GRS 1747-312的宽带光谱分析，探测可能存在的引力红移信号，从而直接测量中子星表面引力红移，为约束中子星质量-半径比及致密物质物态方程提供关键观测证据。

Method: 使用AstroSat约40 ks观测数据，采用热康普顿化黑体发射与星际吸收模型拟合连续谱；用盘反射分量拟合6.4 keV处轻度展宽的铁线；用APEC热等离子体模型描述2 keV以下的窄发射线；并利用Swift短时标观测数据对4.2 keV发射线通量给出上限约束。

Result: 源在1.0–5.5 keV吸收通量为1.67^{+0.04}_{-0.07}×10^{-11} erg s^{-1} cm^{-2}，对应光度~(0.9–1.80)×10^{35} erg s^{-1}。探测到4.19^{+0.12}_{-0.10} keV发射线，宽度σ=0.2±0.2 keV，线通量13^{+10}_{-9}×10^{-5} erg s^{-1} cm^{-2}；Swift数据给出上限<30×10^{-5} erg s^{-1} cm^{-2}。该线被解释为中性Fe Kα线（6.4 keV）经中子星表面引力红移（z~0.6）所致，与1.4 M☉、10 km半径非自旋中子星预期一致。

Conclusion: 4.2 keV发射线如被后续观测确认，将提供中子星表面引力红移的直接测量，从而强有力地约束中子星质量-半径比，为致密物质物态方程研究提供重要观测限制。

Abstract: We present a broadband spectral analysis of the neutron star LMXB GRS 1747-312 using $\sim 40$ ks AstroSat data. The source was observed during the decay phase of the 2017 outburst, with an absorbed 1.0--5.5 keV flux of $1.67^{+0.04}*{-0.07} \times 10^{-11}$ erg s$^{-1}$ cm$^{-2}$, corresponding to a luminosity of $\sim (0.9-1.80) \times 10^{35}$ erg s$^{-1}$. The continuum is modeled with thermal Comptonization of blackbody emission and interstellar absorption. A mildly broad iron line at $\sim 6.4$ keV is fitted with a disc reflection component. Narrow lines below 2 keV are described by a hot plasma using the XSPEC model APEC. Additionally, there is a potential detection of an emission line at $4.19^{+0.12}*{-0.10}$ keV with width $σ= 0.2 \pm 0.2~\mathrm{keV}$ and line flux = $13^{+10}*{-9} \times 10^{-5}$ erg s$^{-1}$ cm$^{-2}$. Examination of several short duration ($\sim$ few kiloseconds) Swift observations at few times the AstroSat source flux, provided upper limits to the line flux $< 30 \times 10^{-5}$ erg s$^{-1}$ cm$^{-2}$. The 4.2 keV line likely originates from reflection off the neutron star surface. Shifting the neutral Fe $K*α$ line from its rest energy of 6.4 to 4.2 keV requires a redshift of $z \sim 0.6$, consistent with that expected from the surface of a non-spinning $1.4 M_\odot$, 10 km radius neutron star. If confirmed, this feature provides a potential direct measurement of gravitational redshift, allowing us to place strong constraints on the neutron star's mass-to-radius ratio and gain valuable insights into the equation of state (EOS) of dense matter.

</details>


### [129] [The Thousand-Pulsar-Array programme on MeerKAT XIX: Single-pulse data analysis, nulling and pulse energy distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10807)
*Michael J. Keith,Patrick Weltevrede,Lucy Oswald,Aris Karastergiou,Xiaoxi Song,Haoyue Wang,Jui-An Hsu,Simon Johnston,Geoff Wright,Matthew Bailes,Maciej Serylak*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了利用MeerKAT射电望远镜获取的千脉冲星阵列（TPA）单脉冲数据集，包含1192颗脉冲星的时序观测数据，每颗源约1000个连续脉冲。通过MeerTime单脉冲软件流水线进行校准和干扰抑制，并基于贝叶斯框架对脉冲能量分布和消零行为进行了群体级研究。结果显示约一半脉冲星需要多分量能量分布模型，消零现象与自转周期正相关，且单脉冲亮度可大幅超过长期平均亮度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对脉冲星单脉冲统计特性的研究多局限于小样本，缺乏对大样本脉冲星群体进行系统性的单脉冲能量分布和消零行为分析，难以揭示脉冲星发射机制的群体演化规律。

Method: 利用MeerKAT望远镜获取1192颗脉冲星的单脉冲时序数据，开发MeerTime Single Pulse软件流水线进行数据校准和射频干扰自动剔除。采用贝叶斯框架对相位平均的单脉冲能量分布建模，考虑多种本征能量分布函数并引入显式消零分数参数。

Result: 约半数脉冲星需要多分量本征能量分布；多数脉冲星检测到或限定了消零分数，消零分数随自转周期增大而增加，对周期导数依赖较弱；低自转能损率脉冲星的单脉冲亮度可大幅超过长期平均亮度。

Conclusion: 这些结果刻画了大样本脉冲星单脉冲发射的统计特性，揭示了能量分布和消零行为在P-$\dot{P}$图上的系统性演化趋势，同时指出相位平均能量分布模型在捕捉脉冲星发射变异的完整复杂性方面存在局限。

Abstract: We present the Thousand Pulsar Array (TPA) single-pulse data set, obtained with the MeerKAT radio telescope and comprising time-series observations of 1192 pulsars, typically containing ~1000 consecutive pulses per source. We describe the MeerTime Single Pulse software pipeline which calibrates the data and automatically excises interference signals to produce data products suitable for typical single-pulse studies. To demonstrate the capabilities of the dataset, we carry out a population-level study of phase-averaged single-pulse energy distributions and nulling behaviour. Pulse energy distributions are modelled within a Bayesian framework choosing from a range of intrinsic energy distributions, and including an explicit nulling fraction. We find that approximately half of the pulsars require multi-component intrinsic energy distributions, while the remainder are consistent with single-component models. Nulling is detected or constrained for most pulsars in the sample, and both the occurrence and inferred nulling fraction show systematic variation across the P-$\dot{P}$ diagram. In particular, nulling fractions increase with spin period and exhibit only a weak dependence on period derivative. We also examine trends in the preferred forms of pulse energy distributions as a function of spin-down luminosity, finding modest evidence for population-level evolution. Estimates of single-pulse luminosities indicate that individual pulses can exceed the long-term average luminosity by large factors, particularly for low-$\dot{E}$ pulsars. These results characterise the statistical properties of single-pulse emission across a large pulsar sample and highlight the limitations of phase-averaged energy distributions for capturing the full complexity of pulsar emission variability.

</details>


### [130] [A Multiwavelength Interpretation of HESS J1857+026 Emission Using the Fermi-LAT, VERITAS, and HAWC Observatories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10828)
*Jordan Eagle,Yu Chen,Ramiro Torres-Escobedo,Youyou Li,Ruo-Yu Shang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用Fermi-LAT、VERITAS和HAWC的数据对HESS J1857+026的MeV-TeV伽马射线起源进行了研究。通过空间和光谱分析以及辐射建模，确定了其主要伽马射线起源为脉冲星风云（PWN），由脉冲星PSR J1856+0245供能。演化模型得出系统年龄在16-21 kyr之间，磁场强度在0.4-1.6 μG之间，支持演化型PWN场景。研究发现低于10 GeV的成分可能由超新星遗迹的强子辐射主导，而高于10 GeV的PWN成分中粒子扩散受到抑制，且多仪器联合的空间信息能有效约束扩散性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定HESS J1857+026在MeV-TeV能段的伽马射线起源，区分PWN与SNR的贡献，并利用多波段观测约束其物理特性和粒子扩散机制。

Method: 综合利用Fermi-LAT、VERITAS和HAWC三个天文台的数据进行空间和光谱分析，结合基本演化辐射模型对MeV-TeV能谱进行建模，并通过径向表面亮度轮廓测量粒子扩散特性。

Result: 系统年龄为16-21 kyr，磁场强度为0.4-1.6 μG；低能段（<10 GeV）可能由SNR强子辐射主导；高能段PWN成分的局部扩散系数D(50 TeV)~10^28 cm²/s，低于ISM值。

Conclusion: HESS J1857+026的伽马射线主要起源于演化型PWN，逆康普顿散射主导高能辐射；联合多仪器空间信息可有效约束粒子扩散性质，低能段可能混杂SNR强子成分。

Abstract: We present a new study on the MeV-TeV gamma-ray origin of HESS J1857+026 using data collected from the Fermi-LAT, VERITAS, and HAWC observatories. A spatial and spectral study of HESS J1857+026 including radiative modeling of the MeV-TeV spectrum determines the likely dominant gamma-ray origin as a pulsar wind nebula (PWN) powered by the energetic pulsar PSR J1856+0245. The MeV-TeV spectrum is further characterized through basic evolutionary radiative modeling assuming a PWN origin to constrain the physical properties of the system such as the magnetic field strength and PWN age. The results of the PWN evolutionary model are consistent with the observational constraints of the system, finding an age of the system between t = [16,21]kyr and a magnetic field strength between B = [0.4,1.6]muG. These estimates support an evolved PWN scenario where the observed gamma-ray emission is generated by the relativistic electrons inverse Compton scattering (ICS) off local photon fields, however the low-energy (E < 10GeV) spectral component could be dominated by hadronic emission originating from a supernova remnant (SNR). For a PWN component above 10GeV, we measure the conditions for particle diffusion, finding that the local diffusion (D(50TeV) ~ $10^{28}cm^{-2}s^{-1}$) is suppressed compared to the interstellar medium (ISM) value, in agreement with similar TeV PWNe. By measuring the radial surface brightness profiles of the gamma-ray source across multiple instruments, we demonstrate that the combined MeV-TeV spatial information is a powerful tool to constrain particle diffusion properties.

</details>


### [131] [Fermi-LAT Gamma-ray Emission Discovered from the Composite Supernova Remnant B0453-685 in the Large Magellanic Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10840)
*Jordan Eagle*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用Fermi-LAT在MeV-GeV波段发现了第二个河外脉冲星风云（PWN），位于复合超新星遗迹B0453-685处。多波段建模表明该伽马射线辐射主要来自一个受反向激波影响的演化脉冲星风云（约14,000年），而非超新星遗迹本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找和确认河外星系中脉冲星风云的伽马射线辐射，以扩展对河外PWN的认知，并研究其在宇宙线产生中的作用。

Method: 使用Fermi-LAT数据进行MeV-GeV波段伽马射线数据分析，结合多波段观测进行详细研究，并采用半解析辐射演化模型来区分脉冲星、脉冲星风云和超新星遗迹的贡献。

Result: 在复合超新星遗迹B0453-685位置探测到300MeV-2TeV的微弱点状伽马射线源；多波段建模不支持超新星遗迹起源；半解析模型表明辐射主要来自一个约14,000年演化的脉冲星风云，且在5GeV以下可能存在显著的脉冲星成分。

Conclusion: 这是第二个被发现的河外脉冲星风云伽马射线源，其粒子加速机制对理解脉冲星风云在宇宙线产生中的作用具有重要意义，但需要进一步约束同步辐射截止频率来准确刻画粒子性质。

Abstract: A second extragalactic pulsar wind nebula (PWN) is discovered in the MeV-GeV band using the Fermi-LAT. Faint, point-like gamma-ray emission is detected at the location of the composite supernova remnant (SNR) B0453-685 from energies 300MeV-2TeV. The Fermi-LAT data analysis of the new gamma-ray source is presented together with a detailed multi-wavelength investigation to understand the nature of the observed emission. The observational evidence and physical implications from broadband modeling do not support an SNR gamma-ray origin. Semi-analytic radiative evolutionary models are explored to understand the potential for any pulsar or PWN component responsible for the observed gamma-ray emission. The modeling results favor an evolved PWN ($τ\sim 14,000$ years) that has been impacted by the return of the SNR reverse shock with a possible substantial pulsar component below $5$GeV. The particle acceleration mechanisms and their efficiency within B0453-685 have important implications for the role PWNe play in generating Cosmic Rays (CRs), but constraints on the synchrotron cut-off are required to accurately characterize the underlying particle properties.

</details>


### [132] [Pulsar Wind Nebulae (PWNe) -- A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10855)
*Jordan Eagle*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了脉冲星风星云（PWNe）的研究进展，包括其在射电至X射线的同步辐射和MeV至TeV γ射线的逆康普顿散射特征，讨论了粒子加速机制、理论模型对多波段观测的解释能力，以及未来γ射线天文学将如何推动对该领域的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星风星云中粒子加速过程（如终止激波处及其他区域）尚未被完全理解，亟需理论进展来解释其多波段观测特征。

Method: 综述近年理论研究的成果，分析其对多个PWNe宽带观测（包括能谱和空间特征）的解释能力，并指出未解决问题及未来γ射线天文学的机遇。

Result: 近年理论研究已能够解释多个PWNe的射电至γ射线多波段观测特征，包括同步辐射和逆康普顿散射的能谱与空间分布。

Conclusion: 未来γ射线天文学将发挥关键作用，推动对PWNe粒子加速机制和辐射过程的深入理解。

Abstract: Pulsar Wind Nebulae (PWNe) are relativistic, magnetic winds comprised of radiating electrons and positrons, powered by an energetic pulsar. The pulsar continuously injects particles into the PWN that are accelerated at the termination shock. As the relativistic particles enter the PWN, they radiate away the energy received at the shock as they interact with the PWN environment, generating synchrotron emission from interactions with the magnetic field of the PWN and Inverse Compton Scattering (ICS) from interactions with the local photon fields. Synchrotron emission is observed from the majority of known PWNe from radio to X-ray energies, and the ICS is observed in the $γ$-ray bands, from MeV to TeV energies. The particle acceleration processes at the termination shock and elsewhere within the PWN remain to be understood. Recent progress in theoretical studies have provided the capability to explain broadband observations of several PWNe including their spectral and spatial features. This work reviews some of the most compelling outcomes of recent literature, outlining the outstanding questions that remain to be answered, and how the future prospects of $γ$-ray astronomy will be instrumental in advancing the current understanding of PWNe.

</details>


### [133] [Time lags as proxy of spectral evolution in gamma-ray bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10926)
*C. Maraventano,F. Daigne,R. Mochkovitch,L. Nava,G. Ghirlanda,T. Di Salvo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用Fermi卫星GBM和LLE数据，对GRB 160625B和GRB 190114C两个明亮伽马射线暴进行时间分辨联合光谱分析（10 keV-100 MeV），通过互相关函数计算不同能段间的时间延迟。GRB 160625B呈现正延迟和硬到软的光谱演化，估计体洛伦兹因子Γ~120-250；GRB 190114C在30-100 MeV能段出现负延迟，对应延迟的高能幂律成分。时间延迟是区分GRB不同发射成分和光谱演化的有效诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽马射线暴中正延迟（硬光子先到）和负延迟（硬光子迟到）的物理起源尚不明确，区分产生两种延迟的效应对于识别GRB瞬时辐射和早期余辉阶段的物理机制至关重要。

Method: 使用Fermi GBM和LAT LLE数据，对GRB 160625B和GRB 190114C在10 keV-100 MeV范围内进行时间分辨联合光谱分析，通过互相关函数计算最低能段（10-100 keV）与更高能段（最高30-100 MeV）之间的时间延迟。

Result: GRB 160625B的光谱由单一成分描述，呈现清晰的硬到软演化，所有时间延迟均为正，高能指数截断分析给出体洛伦兹因子Γ~120-250。GRB 190114C在30-100 MeV能段呈现负延迟，对应约2.5秒后出现延迟的高能幂律成分主导LLE能段。

Conclusion: 时间延迟是GRB光谱演化的有效诊断工具：正延迟追踪瞬时辐射的软化过程，负延迟指示新的独立高能光谱成分的出现。

Abstract: Positive lags in gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), where hard photons anticipate softer ones, provide a unique window into the temporal evolution of their prompt emission. Negative lags, when hard photons are delayed, are instead more enigmatic to interpret. Disentangling the effects that produce both kinds of lags is critical for identifying the physical mechanisms at work in the prompt and early afterglow phases of GRBs. We investigate the potential of time lags for distinguishing different emission components at different energy bands. Using data from the Fermi Gamma-ray Burst Monitor (GBM) and the LAT Low Energy(LLE) technique, we perform a time-resolved joint spectral analysis in the range 10 keV-100 MeV for two exceptionally bright bursts, GRB 160625B and GRB 190114C. Time lags between the lowest-energy band (10-100 keV) and progressively higher-energy bands up to 30-100 MeV were computed across their distinct emission episodes via the cross-correlation function. For GRB 160625B, the spectra are described by a single component with clear hard-to-soft evolution, and the time lags are always positive. Analysis of the high-energy exponential cutoff, likely originating above the photosphere, yields bulk Lorentz factor estimates of $Γ\sim 120-250$. GRB 190114C exhibits negative lags in the 30-100 MeV band, coinciding with a delayed high-energy powerlaw component that dominates the LLE range after ~2.5 s. Comparison with multi-wavelength observations shows some compatibility with the early afterglow, though its origin remains open, leaving room for external shocks or internal dissipation. Time lags are effective diagnostic tools for the spectral evolution of GRBs: positive lags trace the softening of the prompt emission, whereas negative lags indicate the appearance of a new, independent high-energy spectral component.

</details>


### [134] [The link between obscured accretion and mildly relativistic precessing jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10964)
*Rob Fender,Sara Motta*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了黑洞X射线双星系统中，高速相对论喷流（洛伦兹因子>2）锁定在黑洞自转轴上，而低速进动喷流（约0.3c）与高遮蔽系统（超爱丁顿吸积）存在明确关联，推测是因喷流发射点附近的质量加载导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同速度等级的喷流（高速锁定vs低速进动）与吸积环境（遮蔽程度、吸积率）之间的物理关联机制。

Method: 基于已有观测证据，对比分析高速锁定喷流与低速进动喷流在黑洞/中子星X射线双星中的表现，建立其与系统遮蔽性质、吸积率（超爱丁顿）的关联。

Result: 发现高遮蔽系统与低速进动喷流存在清晰关联；高速喷流在低密度环境中发射并锁定方向，低速喷流在高密度环境中发射且易进动。

Conclusion: 喷流速度与方向稳定性受发射环境密度调控：低密度环境利于高速锁定喷流，高密度环境（超爱丁顿吸积）通过质量加载导致喷流减速和进动。

Abstract: We have recently shown evidence that the most relativistic jets (with Lorentz factor >2) from stellar-mass black holes in X-ray binary systems may be locked to a fixed axis, likely the spin axis of the black hole. Slower, mildly relativistic jets (with velocities typically ~ 0.3c) are often seen to precess and can be associated with both neutron stars and black holes. In this paper we demonstrate an additional clear link between highly obscured systems and these lower-velocity, precessing jets. We speculate that this link may be due to mass-loading of the jets close to their launch sites, since these obscured systems are likely to be examples of (sometimes persistent, other times transient) super-Eddington accretion. The fastest relativistic jets are now seen to be both locked to a fixed direction, likely the black hole spin axis, and to be launched in low-density environments, while jets launched in dense environments are generally slower and very likely to precess.

</details>


### [135] [Resolving SLX 1744-299 and SLX 1744-300 in the hard X-ray band: implications for their ultracompact nature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11133)
*Enzo A. Saavedra,Montserrat Armas Padilla,Teo Muñoz-Darias*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用NuSTAR档案观测数据，首次在10 keV以上空间分辨了银河系中心双源SLX 1744−299和SLX 1744−300。两者均处于硬态，但行为显著不同：SLX 1744−299表现为质量吸积率逐渐下降，而SLX 1744−300保持稳定并出现两次短复发I型X射线暴。结合距离上限，推导出低持久X射线光度，并根据盘不稳定性模型推断轨道周期，支持两者可能属于超致密X射线双星（UCXB）家族，其中SLX 1744−299的证据更为有力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续的低光度低质量X射线双星（LMXBs）为研究吸积这一尚不充分理解的能段提供了独特机会，同时有助于发现轨道周期短于约80分钟的超致密X射线双星（UCXB）新成员。

Method: 使用NuSTAR档案观测数据，在10 keV以上对SLX 1744−299和SLX 1744−300进行空间分辨分析；通过时序（均方根变异性）和能谱（热康普顿化模型）特性表征系统状态；结合距离上限推导X射线光度和质量吸积率，并将吸积率与盘不稳定性模型临界值比较以约束轨道周期。

Result: SLX 1744−300略亮，流量比约1.15（外推至0.5-10 keV增至约1.3）；两源均处于硬态；SLX 1744−299通量逐渐下降（质量吸积率降低），SLX 1744−300稳定但出现两次短复发I型X射线暴（混合H/He燃烧）；低持久X射线光度分别为≤1.1×10^36 erg s⁻¹和≤2.6×10^36 erg s⁻¹（3-78 keV）；轨道周期分别≤90分钟和≤105-155分钟。

Conclusion: 两源均符合UCXB参数范围，但SLX 1744−299的证据更为充分，因其周期上限更严格且此前报告过中等时长爆发。

Abstract: Persistent, low-luminosity low-mass X-ray binaries (LMXBs) offer a unique opportunity to study accretion in this poorly understood regime, as well as to unveil new members of the ultracompact X-ray binary (UCXB) family, characterised by orbital periods ($P_{\rm orb}$) shorter than $\sim 80$ min. We report on a NuSTAR archival observation that, for the first time above 10 keV, spatially resolves the Galactic Centre pair SLX 1744$-$299 and SLX 1744$-$300. We find SLX 1744$-$300 to be slightly brighter, with a flux ratio of $\sim 1.15$, increasing to $\sim 1.3$ when extrapolated to 0.5$-$10 keV. Both the timing (root-mean-square variability) and spectral properties (well described in both cases by a thermal Comptonisation model) indicate that the systems were in the hard state. The two sources, however, display markedly different behaviour throughout the observation. SLX 1744$-$299 shows a gradual flux decline consistent with a decrease in the mass-accretion rate, whereas SLX 1744$-$300 remains steady but exhibits two short-recurrence Type-I X-ray bursts indicative of mixed H/He burning. Combining our results with previously reported upper limits on the distance, we derive low persistent X-ray luminosities of $L_{\rm X}\lesssim 1.1\times10^{36}$ erg s$^{-1}$ and $L_{\rm X}\lesssim 2.6\times10^{36}$ erg s$^{-1}$ (3$-$78 keV) for SLX 1744$-$299 and SLX 1744$-$300, respectively. The corresponding mass-accretion rates, when compared with the critical values from the disc instability model, favour $P_{\rm orb}\lesssim 90$ min and $P_{\rm orb}\lesssim 105-155$ min. Although both limits are formally compatible with the UCXB regime, the case of SLX 1744$-$299 appears significantly more compelling, also considering the previously reported intermediate-duration burst.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [On partial T-adic exponential sums and partial exponential sums with p-power conductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10041)
*C. Douglas Haessig*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Liu和Wan的T-adic指数和理论推广到部分T-adic指数和，证明了相关L函数的T-adic亚纯性，从而给出了p幂导子特征的部分L函数有理性的p-adic证明，并给出了Newton-over-Hodge估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Liu和Wan建立的T-adic指数和理论，将其从完全指数和情形扩展到部分指数和情形，以处理更一般的具有p幂导子的特征和。

Method: 通过构造部分T-adic指数和，建立其对应的L函数，并利用T-adic分析方法证明这些L函数的亚纯性，进而推导有理性和Newton-over-Hodge估计。

Result: 证明了部分T-adic指数和的L函数是T-adic亚纯的；给出了p幂导子特征的部分L函数有理性的p-adic证明；得到了Newton-over-Hodge估计。

Conclusion: 成功将T-adic理论从完全指数和推广到部分指数和，为处理更广泛的特征和问题提供了新的p-adic工具。

Abstract: Liu and Wan introduced T-adic exponential sums as a way to interpolate all character sums with character having p-power conductor. In this paper, we generalize their T-adic theory to partial T-adic exponential sums. We prove that the associated L-functions are T-adic meromorphic, and as a consequence, give a p-adic proof of rationality for all partial L-functions of characters with p-power conductor. We also give Newton-over-Hodge estimates.

</details>


### [137] [A generic categorical local Langlands correspondence for quasi-split reductive groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10149)
*David Helm,Maarten Solleveld,Yujie Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类拟分裂p-adic群（包括所有拟分裂古典群及部分非古典群）的泛范畴（算术）局部Langlands猜想，构造了从自守侧generic Bernstein块的稳定∞-范畴到L-参数模栈上ind-coherent层的稳定∞-范畴的完全忠实函子；此外，结合Hansen-Mann的工作及Fargues-Scholze构造与谱Eisenstein级数的预期兼容性，去掉了genericity条件，得到了更广泛群的完整Fargues-Scholze范畴局部Langlands等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将[BZCHN24]中针对GL_n的范畴局部Langlands对应推广到更广泛的拟分裂p-adic约化群（包括所有拟分裂古典群及部分非古典群），并建立无需genericity条件的完整Fargues-Scholze范畴等价。

Method: 通过构造从自仿侧generic Bernstein块的稳定∞-范畴到L-参数模栈上ind-coherent层的稳定∞-范畴的完全忠实函子；对一般拟分裂群，建立经典局部Langlands框架以将经典对应提升至∞-范畴对应；结合Hansen-Mann[HM26]及Fargues-Scholze构造与谱Eisenstein级数的兼容性假设，去掉genericity条件。

Result: 对一大类拟分裂p-adic约化群G证明了泛范畴（算术）局部Langlands猜想，得到了完全忠实函子；在额外假设下，获得了无需genericity条件的完整Fargues-Scholze范畴局部Langlands等价。

Conclusion: 该工作将范畴局部Langlands对应从GL_n推广到了更广泛的拟分裂群类，并为完整Fargues-Scholze等价提供了关键步骤。

Abstract: We prove a generic categorical (arithmetic) local Langlands conjecture for a large class of quasi-split reductive $p$-adic groups $G$, including all quasi-split classical groups and some non-classical groups. More precisely, we construct a natural fully faithful functor from the stable $\infty$-category of generic Bernstein blocks on the automorphic side to the stable $\infty$-category of ind-coherent sheaves on the moduli stack of (arithmetic) $L$-parameters, generalizing earlier work of [BZCHN24] for $\mathrm{GL}_n$. Moreover, for an arbitrary quasi-split reductive $p$-adic group $G$, we formulate a classical local Langlands framework under which a classical correspondence can be lifted to an $\infty$-categorical correspondence.
  Furthermore, combined with the recent work of Hansen-Mann [HM26] and assuming the expected compatibility of Fargues-Scholze construction with spectral Eisenstein series, our results give the full Fargues--Scholze categorical local Langlands equivalence [FS24], without the genericity condition, for a large class of quasi-split reductive $p$-adic groups $G$.

</details>


### [138] [A Tsang-range high-moment bound for $\operatorname{Im}\log L(\tfrac12+it,χ)$ under GRH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10242)
*Scott D. Hughes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在广义黎曼假设下，证明了固定奇方根自由导子q≥3与原始非主特征χ的X_χ(t)的Selberg-Tsang高阶矩界，并由此推出高斯型大偏差上界(尾部概率exp(-c V²/L_T))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有对log|ζ(1/2+it)|的大偏差上界结果，但其虚部Im log L(1/2+it,χ)在高阶矩和偏差方面的已知结论尚不充分，本文致力于填补这一空白。

Method: 将Selberg点态近似公式移植到固定导子L(s,χ)上(在GRH下)，将X_χ(t)展开为三个素幂Dirichlet多项式之和，再利用Soundararajan均值引理计算各多项式的高阶矩，最终通过组合得到Selberg-Tsang型矩上界。

Result: 对任意K>0存在常数C_K、T_0，使得对所有T≥T_0和1≤k≤K L_T，有(1/T)∫_T^{2T} |X_χ(t)|^{2k} dt ≤ (C_K k L_T)^k；进而由Markov不等式推出对√L_T ≪ V ≪ L_T的高斯尺度尾界exp(-c V²/L_T)。

Conclusion: 本文给出了固定导子L-函数虚部在GRH下的高阶矩和大偏差上界，是已有实部结果在虚部情形的平行推广，对理解L-函数在临界线上的相位行为有理论价值。

Abstract: Conditional on the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis for $L(s,χ)$, we prove the Selberg--Tsang high-moment bound for $X_χ(t) = \operatorname{Im}\log L(\tfrac12+it,χ)$ at fixed squarefree odd conductor $q \ge 3$ and primitive non-principal character $χ$. Writing $L_T = \log\log(qT)$: for every $K > 0$ there exist constants $C_K$ and $T_0$ such that $\frac{1}{T}\int_T^{2T} |X_χ(t)|^{2k}\,dt \le (C_K\,k\,L_T)^k$ for all $T \ge T_0$ and every integer $1 \le k \le K L_T$. The proof ports Selberg's pointwise approximate formula for $S(t)$ to $L(s,χ)$ at fixed conductor under GRH, splits it into three prime-power Dirichlet polynomials, and evaluates their moments via Soundararajan's mean-value lemma. As a corollary, Markov's inequality yields a Gaussian-scale tail $\exp(-c V^2 / L_T)$ for $\sqrt{L_T} \ll V \ll L_T$ -- a GRH-conditional, fixed-conductor, imaginary-part analogue of the large-deviation upper bounds known for $\log|ζ(\tfrac12+it)|$.

</details>


### [139] [Prime Quadruplets and Jump Conditions on Arithmetic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10331)
*Himaghna Roy Choudhury,Shicheng Wei*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了同时满足φ(n+12)=φ(n)+12和σ(n+12)=σ(n)+12的合数n的特征，证明了当n和n+12均为无平方因子半素数时该特征完全成立，且单一素数幂不可能构成解。在10^12范围内未发现反例。若该猜想成立且存在无穷多解，则可证明存在无穷多素数四胞胎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 素数四胞胎(p,p+2,p+6,p+8)可生成满足跳变条件的解n=p(p+8)，但完整的特征刻画仍是一个未解决的猜想。本文旨在推进对此类合数n的完整刻画。

Method: 通过数论分析限制n和n+12的结构，分别考察无平方因子半素数情形和单一素数幂情形，并进行10^12以内的完全数值搜索。

Result: 证明当n和n+12均为无平方因子半素数时，所有解均由素数四胞胎生成；证明单一素数幂不可能构成解；10^12以内无反例。

Conclusion: 该猜想若被证明且存在无穷多解，则蕴含素数四胞胎的无穷性。

Abstract: We provide progress on the characterization of composite integers $n$ that satisfy the jump conditions $\varphi(n+12)=\varphi(n)+12$ and $σ(n+12)=σ(n)+12$ simultaneously. While it is known that prime quadruplets $(p,p+2,p+6,p+8)$ generate solutions $n=p(p+8)$, the complete characterization remains an open conjecture. We prove that this characterization is complete when $n$ and $n+12$ are both squarefree semiprimes, and that no solutions can be composed of a single prime power. Furthermore, a complete search up to $10^{12}$ resulted in no counterexamples to the conjecture. If this conjecture is proven true, and there are infinitely many such solutions, then it can be proved that there are infinitely many prime quadruplets.

</details>


### [140] [Unique decomposition of orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10521)
*Gaurav Digambar Patil*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了数域中序（order）的“基本定理”，证明任意序可唯一表示为不可约序的交集，且指数与导子在此分解下具有可乘性。并推广了Furtwangler准则，未来将应用于数域的加权枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了系统刻画数域中任意序的结构，解决序的导子结构及分解唯一性问题，并推广Furtwangler准则以回答更广泛的相关问题。

Method: 通过定义“不可约序”概念，证明任意序可唯一表示为不可约序的交集；利用该分解证明指数在整数环中具有乘法分配性，导子分解为两两互素的理想。

Result: 建立了序的不可约分解基本定理；推广了Furtwangler准则，得到关于序的导子结构的更一般结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为序的结构理论提供了基础性分解工具，未来可用于数域的加权枚举等应用。

Abstract: We establish a Fundamental Theorem of Orders (FTO), which allows us to express any order (in a number field) uniquely as an intersection of \textit{irreducible orders}. Along this decomposition, the index (in the ring of integers) distributes multiplicatively, and the conductor factors into pairwise co-prime ideals.
  We use it to show a more general version of Furtwangler criterion about the structure of conductors of orders over $\Z$, as this answers a wide variations of such questions. In a future work, we will also give applications to weighted enumeration of number fields.

</details>


### [141] [On the Ekedahl sieve for the singular locus of the discriminant polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10523)
*Gaurav Digambar Patil*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对Ekedahl筛法在处理高度偏斜盒子及大模量条件时的局限性，提出了一种专门针对判别多项式奇点轨迹的变体，通过利用判别式非退化性质绕过传统归纳框架，将筛法简化为高效的两步或一步过程，建立了鲁棒的尾端估计和平方自由幂节省界，从而优化了筛模的允许范围，改进了有界平方自由多项式值的误差项，并为按判别式加权的数域枚举提供了基础几何筛估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Ekedahl筛法在处理高度偏斜的盒子（highly skew boxes）时，由于依赖归纳步骤，往往产生次优的界。当引入与二元形式的尾端变量竞争的大模量条件时，这一局限性尤为突出。本文旨在克服这一限制。

Method: 开发了一种专门针对判别多项式奇点轨迹的Ekedahl筛法变体。利用判别式在最极端两个系数之外的非退化性质，绕过标准归纳框架，将筛法简化为两步或一步过程。建立了通用的尾端估计以及平方自由、幂节省界，并无缝整合了外部模量条件。

Result: 优化了筛模的允许范围（permissible range），改进了有界平方自由多项式值的枚举误差项，并为按判别式加权的数域枚举提供了所需的几何筛估计。

Conclusion: 该变体有效提升了Ekedahl筛法在偏斜盒子和外部模量条件下的性能，为相关算术枚举问题提供了更优的误差控制。

Abstract: The Ekedahl sieve is a powerful tool for enumerating arithmetic objects, but traditional formulations relying on inductive steps often yield suboptimal bounds when applied to highly skew boxes. This limitation is particularly restrictive when introducing large modular conditions that compete with the tail-end variables of a binary form. In this paper, we develop a specialized variant of the Ekedahl sieve tailored to the singular locus of the discriminant polynomial. By exploiting the specific non-degeneracy properties of the discriminant outside its two most extreme coefficients, we bypass the standard inductive framework, reducing the sieve to a highly efficient two-step process in some cases, and a one step process in others. We establish robust generic tail-end estimates as well as squarefree, power-saving bounds that seamlessly incorporate external modular conditions. This optimization maximizes the permissible range of the sieving modulus, yielding improved error terms for the enumeration of bounded squarefree values of certain polynomials and providing the foundational geometric sieve estimates required for the weighted enumeration of number fields by discriminant.

</details>


### [142] [Hilbert irreducibility for algebraic points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10584)
*Borys Kadets,Danny Neftin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究覆盖曲线φ: X→X₀上，次数为d的点p使得纤维φ⁻¹(p)在k(p)上可约的集合的有限性问题。当X本身有无穷多个d次点时，考虑去掉来自X的d次点像的修正问题。证明了一系列正性结果，并推论出对于固定曲线X和所有足够高次数的不可约有理函数φ: X→ℙ¹（有b个分支点），在次数d<b/7-2且不包含来自X的d次点的点上，可约纤维的集合是有限的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究覆盖曲线中，给定次数d的点使得纤维可约的集合何时有限。这是算术几何中关于有理点分布和纤维可约性的基本问题。

Method: 通过算术几何和代数曲面的理论，分析覆盖曲线的分支点与可约纤维的关系，利用次数下界条件（d<b/7-2）建立有限性定理。

Result: 证明了在适当条件下，可约纤维的集合是有限的；特别地，对于固定曲线X和足够高次数的不可约有理函数，次数d<b/7-2且不包含X的d次点的可约纤维集合是有限的。

Conclusion: 给出了覆盖曲线上可约纤维有限性的判定条件，建立了次数与分支点数量之间的显式不等式关系。

Abstract: We study the following problem: given a covering of curves $φ\colon X \to X_0$ over a number field $k$, and an integer $d$, when is the set \[\{p \in X_0(\overline{k})|\ \mathrm{deg}\ p = d, \text{ and the fiber } φ^{-1}(p) \text{ is reducible over } k(p)\}\] finite? In case $X$ itself admits infinitely many degree $d$ points, we consider the modified problem where the images of degree $d$ points on $X$ are removed from the set. We prove a number of theorems ensuring a positive answer. As a consequence we show that for a fixed curve $X$ and all sufficiently high-degree indecomposable rational functions $φ:X \to \mathbb{P}^1$ with $b$ branch points, the set of reducible fibers above degree $d<b/7-2$ points, not containing a degree $d$ point from $X$, is finite.

</details>


### [143] [Eisenstein-Kronecker classes on Hilbert moduli spaces and a new construction of Katz's $p$-adic measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10626)
*Guillermo Gamarra Segovia*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Kings-Sprang的Eisenstein-Kronecker类构造推广到Hilbert模空间上的万有阿贝尔概形（具有(Γ(l), Γ_{00}(p^∞))-水平），并将相关的p-adic Hilbert模形式与Katz构造的Eisenstein级数进行比较。进一步构造了一个插值Eisenstein-Kronecker类的p-adic测度，并证明该测度本质上恢复了Katz的p-adic Eisenstein测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Kings-Sprang的Eisenstein-Kronecker类构造从更一般的情形推广到Hilbert模空间上的万有阿贝尔概形，并建立与Katz经典工作的联系。

Method: 通过推广Kings-Sprang的构造到具有特定水平结构((Γ(l), Γ_{00}(p^∞)))的Hilbert模空间上的万有阿贝尔概形；构造p-adic测度来插值Eisenstein-Kronecker类；利用与Katz Eisenstein级数的比较结果证明测度的一致性。

Result: 成功构造了Hilbert模空间上的推广Eisenstein-Kronecker类，并证明了其对应的p-adic测度本质上等价于Katz的p-adic Eisenstein测度。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了Eisenstein-Kronecker类在Hilbert模空间上的构造，建立了与Katz经典Eisenstein级数理论的深刻联系。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the construction of the Eisenstein-Kronecker classes of Kings-Sprang to the universal abelian schemes lying over Hilbert moduli spaces parametrizing objects with $(Γ(l), Γ_{00}(p^\infty))$-level. We then compare the associated ($p$-adic) Hilbert modular forms to the Eisenstein series constructed by Katz. Similarly to the procedure of Kings-Sprang, we produce a $p$-adic measure which interpolates the Eisenstein-Kronecker classes, and use the mentioned comparison results to show that this recovers essentially the $p$-adic Eisenstein measure constructed by Katz.

</details>


### [144] [Linear relations of four conjugates of an algebraic number of degree eight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10672)
*Žygimantas Baronėnas,Paulius Drungilas,Jonas Jankauskas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了所有满足特定线性关系的8次代数数，并证明了当存在六个独立线性关系时，该代数数可分解为一个二次代数数与一个四次代数数之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数数共轭元之间线性关系的存在性条件，特别是8次代数数中四个共轭元满足线性关系的刻画问题。

Method: 通过分析代数数共轭元的线性关系，利用对称性及域论工具，分类讨论两种线性关系（α₁=α₂+α₃+α₄ 和 α₁+α₂+α₃+α₄=0），并特别处理最小多项式非偶次且存在六个独立线性关系的情形。

Result: 完整刻画了所有满足两种线性关系的8次代数数；进一步证明了当最小多项式非偶且存在六个形如αᵢ₁+αᵢ₂+αᵢ₃+αᵢ₄=0的独立线性关系时，α可表示为二次数与四次代数数之和。

Conclusion: 给出了8次代数数共轭元满足特定线性关系的充要条件，并揭示了此类代数数的域论结构分解性质。

Abstract: We characterize all algebraic numbers $α$ of degree $8$ for which there exist four distinct algebraic conjugates $α_1$, $α_2$, $α_3$, $α_4$ of $α$ satisfying the linear relation $α_{1}=α_{2}+α_{3}+α_{4}$. Analogous characterization is obtained for the linear relation $α_{1}+α_{2}+α_{3}+α_{4}=0$. In particular, when an algebraic number $α$ of degree $8$ has a non-even minimal polynomial and possesses exactly six distinct linear relations of the form $α_{i_1}+α_{i_2}+α_{i_3}+α_{i_4}=0$, we prove that $α$ is a sum of a quadratic and a quartic algebraic number.

</details>


### [145] [A Divisor Parametrization for the Erdős--Straus Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10922)
*M. Bello-Hernández,M. Benito,E. Fernández*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究将分数1/n表示为三个单位分数之和的问题，且要求这三个单位分数的分母都能被给定的整数m整除。通过缩放，这等价于将m/n表示为三个单位分数之和。主要关注Erdős–Straus情形m=4。文中引入基于除数的函数fab(n,a,b)，证明其可接受参数恰好能恢复所有三个分母都能被4整除的1/n分解，并将该参数化方法与已知的Type I/II描述进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单位分数分解问题中分母可被给定整数整除的约束条件，特别是Erdős–Straus猜想中m=4的特殊情形，旨在提供一种新的参数化方法。

Method: 引入一个基于除数的函数fab(n,a,b)，通过证明其可接受参数与所有三个分母都能被4整除的1/n分解一一对应，并与已有的Type I/II描述进行对比分析。

Result: 证明了fab(n,a,b)函数的可接受参数能够精确恢复所有满足分母被4整除条件的1/n的三单位分数分解。

Conclusion: 提供了一种新的参数化框架来理解Erdős–Straus猜想中分母可整除性的约束，与经典Type I/II描述互补。

Abstract: We study representations of \(1/n\) as a sum of three unit fractions whose denominators are all divisible by a prescribed integer \(m\). After scaling, this is equivalent to representing \(m/n\) as a sum of three unit fractions. Our main focus is the Erdős--Straus case \(m=4\). We introduce a divisor-based function \(\mathrm{fab}(n,a,b)\), prove that its admissible parameters recover exactly the decompositions of \(1/n\) with all three denominators divisible by \(4\), and compare this parametrization with well-known Type I/II descriptions.

</details>


### [146] [Weighted partitions with interval restrictions: exact formulas and a bivariate master identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11011)
*George E. Andrews,Mohamed El Bachraoui,Aritram Dhar,Ankush Goswami,Runqiao Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Andrews和El Bachraoui引入的两个带符号分拆函数a₂''(n)和b₂''(n)的猜想：给出了a₂''(n)的生成函数（由初等有理项和周期符号的假theta级数构成），证明了b₂''(n)仅取值-1,0,1,2，并通过引入辅助变量z建立了主恒等式，给出了闭式解和量子模解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Andrews和El Bachraoui关于区间限制分拆的两个带符号分拆函数的猜想，深化对这类特殊分拆函数生成函数性质及系数取值范围的理解。

Method: 引入辅助变量z记录大于1的非强制分拆部分个数，推导两个生成函数的闭式；利用解析与组合两种技巧证明主恒等式(1+q²)ℬ(z,q)-(1+q)𝒜(z,q)=-q⁴/(1-q³)；在z=-1处结合Rogers–Fine求值得到a₂''(n)的假theta公式和b₂''(n)的显式生成函数。

Result: 证明了a₂''(n)生成函数的假theta级数形式猜想；证明了b₂''(n)仅取值-1,0,1,2并给出精确系数描述；建立了主恒等式并给出其解析与组合证明。

Conclusion: 完整解决了两个带符号分拆函数的猜想，揭示了主恒等式的结构意义，并给出了量子模解释及多种推论（包括直接Heine–Rogers–Fine证明和固定细化结果）。

Abstract: Let $a_2''(n)$ and $b_2''(n)$ be the signed partition functions introduced by Andrews and El Bachraoui for interval-restricted partitions whose parts greater than $1$ are controlled by the smallest even part and by the number of ones. We prove two conjectures for these functions. The first gives the generating function for $a_2''(n)$ as an elementary rational term plus a false theta series with periodic signs; the second asserts that the companion coefficients $b_2''(n)$ take only the values $-1,0,1,2$. The central structural result introduces an auxiliary variable $z$ recording the number of non-compulsory parts greater than $1$. We obtain closed forms for the two resulting generating functions and prove the master identity $(1+q^2)\mathcal B(z,q)-(1+q)\mathcal A(z,q)=-q^4/(1-q^3)$ using both analytic and combinatorial techniques. At $z=-1$, this identity, together with a Rogers--Fine evaluation, gives the false theta formula for $a_2''(n)$ and an explicit generating function for $b_2''(n)$. The latter formula implies the asserted coefficient range and leads to an exact coefficient description of $b_2''(n)$. We also include a direct Heine--Rogers--Fine proof of the false theta formula, ordinary and fixed-refinement consequences of the master identity, and the resulting quantum modular interpretation.

</details>


### [147] [Adjacent comparison bounds and extremal sets for Ruzsa numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11069)
*Yuchen Ding,Huixi Li,Junfeng Li,Wei Niu,Xiamiao Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了环Z_m上的Ruzsa数R_m，证明了|R_{m+1}-R_m|≤144，给出了集合A大小的非平凡界限，计算了所有m≤100的R_m精确值，并提出了若干相关问题及部分结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Ding和Zhao于2024年提出的猜想的启发，旨在进一步理解Ruzsa数的性质及其变化规律。

Method: 通过组合数论和模m环上的加法结构分析，结合构造与反证法，推导相邻Ruzsa数之差的界；通过计算枚举得到小模数下的精确值。

Result: 证明了相邻Ruzsa数之差不超过144；给出了集合A大小的非平凡上下界；并列出了所有m≤100的R_m精确值，显著扩展了Sándor和Yang在2017年的结果。

Conclusion: 本文在Ruzsa数研究上取得了实质性进展，验证了猜想的部分内容，并为后续研究提供了新的问题和方向。

Abstract: Let $m$ be a positive integer and $\mathbb{Z}_m$ the residue class ring modulo $m$. The Ruzsa number $R_m$ is defined to be the least integer $r$ such that there is a subset $\mathcal{A}$ of $\mathbb{Z}_m$ satisfying $
1\le σ_{\mathcal{A}}(n)\le r $
for any $n\in \mathbb{Z}_m$, where $$ σ_{\mathcal{A}}(n) =\#\big\{(a,a')\in\mathcal A^2:
  a+a'\equiv n\pmod{m}\big\}. $$ Motivated by a 2024 conjecture of Ding and Zhao, we prove $
| R_{m+1}-R_m|\le 144. $ Let $\mathcal{A}$ be a subset of $\mathbb{Z}_m$ satisfying $1\le σ_{\mathcal{A}}(n)\le R_m$ for any $n\in \mathbb{Z}_m$. We also give nontrivial bounds for the size of $\mathcal{A}$. Additionally, we provide exact values of $R_m$ for all $m\le 100$, which substantially extends the table of values given by Sándor and Yang in 2017. Finally, we pose several related problems and prove some partial results.

</details>
