### [148] [Stochastic weather generators for high-frequency wind vector time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09941)
*Mingshi Cui,Kevin Eng,Justin T. Greene,Zern Ke,Abolfazl Sodagartojgi,Zhiqiu Xia,Gemma E. Moran,Michael L. Stein*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用超过30年的分钟级地表风观测数据，基于时间矢量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）开发了多种机器学习模型，用于生成俄克拉荷马州Lamont站点的真实地表风矢量时间序列，并评估了这些生成器捕捉复杂日变化结构和极端风速分布的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地表风速在分钟尺度上存在显著变化，现有研究对此精细时间尺度的风场模拟不足；同时风能、野火蔓延、航空等领域需要高保真的风时间序列生成器作为输入，而传统时间序列模型难以捕捉风速和风向复杂的日变化结构。

Method: 基于30余年分钟级高质量观测数据，以6月数据减少季节影响，采用时间矢量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）构建随机风生成器；设计了单日独立生成和基于前一日条件生成的两种模式，并引入离散天气状态变量；通过多种形式化和非形式化方法评估生成效果。

Result: 最优生成器能准确模拟风速波动性的日变化特征，但在匹配极端风速的观测分布方面存在困难；整体上能够捕捉观测数据中的大部分复杂特征，但未能完全复现所有特征。

Conclusion: 基于VQ-VAE的机器学习方法在分钟级地表风矢量时间序列生成上展现出潜力，能够捕捉复杂的日变化结构，但在极端风速分布的建模上仍需改进。

Abstract: Surface winds can vary substantially from one minute to the next, so there is scope for studying its variation on this fine time scale. Restricting to the month of June to minimize seasonality, this work develops a range of machine learning models for generating realistic time series of surface wind vectors at a site in Lamont, Oklahoma based on more than 30 years of high quality measurements at the minute time scale. Such a generator could be used as an input into models from a range of disciplines, notably for wind energy, but also wildfire spread and aviation, among others. The data show complex diurnal structures in both wind speed and direction that would be challenging to capture with standard time series models, so we consider a number of machine learning approaches to producing a stochastic wind generator based on time vector-quantized variational autoencoders. We consider generating a day's worth of data at a time and generating a day of wind vectors conditional on the previous day's winds. We also study methods for incorporating a discrete weather state variable in the generator. We evaluate the generators using a wide range of formal and informal methods. The best of these generators can capture many but not all of the complex features present in the observational data. In particular, the best of our approaches accurately mimic diurnal changes in wind volatility but struggle to match the observed distribution of extreme wind speeds.

</details>


### [149] [Predicting Hospitalization from a Whole-Person Health Score with Incomplete Electronic Health Records Data: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10093)
*Grayson E. Weavil,Joseph Rigdon,Sarah C. Lotspeich*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了如何在电子健康记录（EHR）中嵌入标准化全人健康指标——所有负荷指数（ALI），利用统计建模和机器学习方法，处理缺失数据问题，预测住院风险。结果表明，基于完整病例比例的综合指标表现尚可（AUC=0.64），而针对相同缺失模式的子集建模方法表现最佳（AUC=0.73），但交叉验证稳定性较差（AUC=0.63）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在EHR中嵌入标准化的全人健康测量指标（如ALI）有助于预防性医疗，但ALI的许多组分因患者未接受所有检测而缺失，因此需要开发有效方法填补信息缺口，以准确预测住院风险。

Method: 基于某大型学术医疗系统1000名患者的EHR数据，使用统计建模和机器学习方法（逻辑回归、随机森林等），将住院次数（计数或二值）作为预测目标，以ALI为主要预测变量并控制年龄和性别。采用多种缺失数据处理策略：含综合指标（如完整病例比例）和分离组分方法（如模式子模型法），通过ROC曲线和AUC评估性能。

Result: 计数模型未优于二值模型；逻辑回归优于随机森林。综合指标中，完整病例比例表现最佳（AUC=0.64），但各项指标差异不超过0.01。分离组分方法中，模式子模型法在样本内表现最优（AUC=0.73），但交叉验证结果较差（AUC=0.63）。

Conclusion: 对于缺失ALI数据的处理，综合指标整体表现相近；而针对相同缺失数据模式的子集进行定制建模是一种有前景的策略。后续计划将模型嵌入EHR系统，以支持大规模临床决策。

Abstract: Embedding a standardized whole-person health measure in electronic health records (EHR) could be instrumental to preventative care. The allostatic load index (ALI), calculated from ten component stressors across three body systems, offers a promising snapshot of holistic health. The ALI can be calculated from EHR data, but many components are missing, since not all patients undergo all tests. Using statistical modeling and machine learning, EHR data for $1000$ patients from a large academic health system were used to predict in-patient hospitalization (as a count or binary) from ALI, controlling for age and sex. Various methods were evaluated to fill in information gaps for patients' missing ALI components, including summary measures combining components or using them separately. Performance was measured using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curves and corresponding areas under the ROC curve (AUC). Count modeling of hospitalization did not improve upon binary, and logistic regression beat random forest. Overall, summary measures performed similarly, with the complete-case proportion (i.e., the proportion of non-missing components that were "unhealthy") performing best (AUC $= 0.64$) but by $\leq 0.01$. When using components separately, the pattern submodel approach most accurately predicted hospitalization (AUC $= 0.73$) in sample, but did not cross-validate as well (AUC $= 0.63$). All summary measures performed similarly. However, when including the ALI components separately, tailoring models to subsets of patients with the same missing data pattern performed best. Next steps include EHR implementation to enable prediction and support clinician decision-making at scale.

</details>


### [150] [Binomial Smoothing for Inventory and Information Control in Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10342)
*Rene Caldentey,Avi Giloni,Clifford Hurvich,Prem Talwai,Yichen Zhang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究双层供应链中零售商如何通过二项式平滑策略在订单可预测性与库存成本之间取得最优权衡，证明该策略能最小化制造商预测误差并提供常数近似保证，为缺乏信息共享的供应链协调提供了具体机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分散式供应链中，上游制造商无法直接观察市场需求，只能从零售商的订单流中推断下游状况。零售商的补货策略因此具有双重角色：既管理库存补货，又塑造上游可获取的信息。传统研究多关注降低订单方差（牛鞭效应），但忽略了订单中不可预测成分对上游预测的关键影响。

Method: 提出二项式平滑策略（Binomial Smoothing），将每单位需求在有限时间窗口内用二项式权重分散响应。在弱平稳高斯需求及温和正则性条件下，从理论上分析该策略的预测误差最小化性质和常数近似保证。

Result: 对于任意固定平滑窗口，二项式策略在所有相同平滑程度策略中最小化制造商的预测误差；策略保持可逆性，制造商可从订单中恢复需求历史；更一般地，二项式平滑实现相对于最优策略的常数因子近似保证。

Conclusion: 补货策略的设计应侧重于减少订单中不可预测的部分，而非单纯降低方差。精心设计的平滑策略能改善供应链绩效并部分替代信息共享机制，无需协作即可实现协调。

Abstract: In many decentralized supply chains, upstream firms do not observe market demand directly and instead infer downstream conditions from the order stream. A retailer's replenishment policy therefore plays a dual role: it governs inventory replenishment and shapes the information available for upstream forecasting. This creates a fundamental trade-off. Smoother orders improve upstream predictability, but delaying the response to demand can increase downstream inventory costs. We study how a retailer should optimally smooth demand in a two-tier supply chain with one retailer and one manufacturer when the manufacturer forecasts future orders from the retailer's order history. We propose Binomial Smoothing, a class of replenishment policies that implements delayed demand response by spreading each unit of demand over a finite horizon using binomial weights. The class is interpretable, easy to calibrate, and analytically tractable. Under weakly stationary Gaussian demand satisfying mild regularity conditions, we show that, for any fixed smoothing horizon, the Binomial policy minimizes the manufacturer's forecast error among all policies with the same degree of smoothing. It remains invertible, so the manufacturer can recover demand history from observed orders. More generally, Binomial Smoothing achieves a constant-factor approximation guarantee relative to an optimal policy. Our results yield a broader insight: replenishment policies should be designed not merely to reduce order variance, as in the traditional bullwhip measure, but to reduce the unpredictable component of orders. Carefully designed smoothing can improve supply-chain performance and partially substitute for information sharing, providing a concrete mechanism for coordination without collaboration.

</details>


### [151] [Two-stage imputation of longitudinal anthropometric data with cross-reference harmonisation: a simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10574)
*Flavia Alves*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段可重复的人体测量缺失数据插补方法：第一阶段使用受试者内线性插值填补就诊日期之间的内部空缺，第二阶段利用LMS方法基于年龄和性别特异性生长参考标准（如WHO或CDC）估计每个受试者的百分位数，前向/后向传递并默认第50百分位数，从而补全剩余缺失值。在合成数据上（60名受试者、288次就诊、30%缺失率）实现了100%完整填补，体重和身高的平均绝对百分比误差分别为3.5%和2.0%，偏差可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向数据集中体重和身高的测量值经常缺失，且合并不同数据源时可能使用不同的生长参考标准（如WHO标准和CDC图表），现有方法缺乏对参考标准差异的显式处理和审计能力。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段在受试者内部跨就诊日期进行线性插值（仅填补内部空缺，不进行外推）；第二阶段使用LMS方法，通过估计每个受试者的百分位数（前向和后向传递，未测量者默认第50百分位数），从年龄和性别特异性生长参考标准中读取期望值来填补剩余缺失值。不同数据源可使用不同参考标准，并可记录和审计。

Result: 在合成数据（n=60名受试者，288次就诊，30%缺失率）上，缺失填补率达到100%完整。掩码值恢复的体重平均绝对误差为1.78 kg（3.5% MAPE），身高为2.84 cm（2.0%），偏差可忽略。受试者内插值恢复的准确性高于从生长参考标准恢复的值。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种简单、无依赖且可审计的人体测量数据插补途径，显式处理不同参考标准差异并记录每个值的来源。下一步需应用于经验数据并将插补不确定性传播到下游模型，才能用于实质性分析。

Abstract: Objective. Longitudinal datasets frequently contain missing weight and height measurements, and studies that combine data sources may index measurements against different growth reference standards (e.g., the WHO reference and CDC charts). We describe and evaluate a reproducible two-stage method that imputes missing anthropometry while making the choice of reference standard an explicit parameter. Methods. Stage 1 applies within-subject linear interpolation across visit dates (interior gaps only, no extrapolation). Stage 2 imputes remaining values from an age- and sex-specific growth reference using the LMS method by estimating each subject's centile, carrying it forward and backwards within the subject, defaulting to the 50th centile when a subject is never measured, and reading the expected value off the reference at the visit age. Different references can be supplied per data source so that the standard applied is recorded and auditable. We assessed recovery accuracy by masking and re-imputing a random 20% of observed values. All evaluations used computer-generated synthetic data. Results. On synthetic data (n = 60 subjects, 288 visits, 30% missing), the method resolved missingness to 100% completeness. Masked-value recovery gave a mean absolute error of 1.78 kg for weight (3.5% mean absolute percentage error) and 2.84 cm for height (2.0%), with negligible bias. Values recovered by within-subject interpolation were more accurate than those recovered from the growth reference, as expected, supporting the two-stage ordering. Conclusion. The method offers a simple, dependency-free, and auditable approach to anthropometric imputation, with explicit handling of differing reference standards and per-value provenance. Application to empirical data and propagation of imputation uncertainty into downstream models are the necessary next steps before use in substantive analyses.

</details>


### [152] [Structural Under-Representation of Women in News: Nonparametric Bayesian Mixtures Capture Time-Dependent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10772)
*Isabella Habereder,Thomas Kneib,Isao Echizen,Timo Spinde*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用时间依赖贝叶斯混合模型分析2019-2024年加拿大新闻中女性被引比例，发现女性代表性不足普遍存在，新闻主题的驱动作用强于报道区域，超过85%的主题-区域时间序列未向性别平等改善，总体分布保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻媒体中女性作为引用来源的代表性不足是性别偏见的突出表现，理解性别偏见的集中位置及演变规律对于定向缓解至关重要。

Method: 采用非参数贝叶斯混合建模方法，结合时间依赖的贝叶斯混合模型技术与适用于女性引用份额（取值0-1之间）的Beta混合核，对2019-2024年加拿大新闻文章进行分析。

Result: 所有聚类中都存在结构性女性代表性不足；新闻主题对女性引用比例差异的驱动作用强于报道区域；超过85%的主题-区域时间序列在观测期内未向性别平等改善；2019-2024年间女性引用比例的总体分布保持稳定。

Conclusion: 先进的概率模型不仅能复现性别偏见研究中的已有发现，还能揭示简单方法难以捕捉的潜在依赖关系和结构模式，鼓励未来在媒体偏见研究中采用基于模型的框架。

Abstract: The under-representation of women as sources cited in news media is one prominent representation of gender bias. Understanding where gender bias concentrates and how it evolves is essential for targeted mitigation. Because gender representation varies across topics, time, and reported-on regions, creating complex dependencies that are difficult to capture parametrically, we employ a nonparametric model to uncover latent cluster structures and temporal dynamics. We combine time-dependent Bayesian mixture modeling techniques with a Beta mixture kernel tailored to female quote shares, bounded between 0 and 1. Fitted on Canadian news articles from 2019 to 2024, the model reveals structural under-representation of women across all clusters, with news topic driving differences in female quote shares more strongly than the reported-on region. More than 85% of topic-region time series show no improvement toward gender parity over the observation period. Dynamic density estimation confirms that the aggregate distribution of female quote shares remains stable between 2019 and 2024. Our application demonstrates that advanced probabilistic models not only reproduce findings in gender bias research but also reveal latent dependencies and structural patterns that simpler approaches miss, encouraging future adoption of model-based frameworks for studying media bias.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Tilting modules for the Cummings construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10204)
*Jan Trlifaj,Mykyta Dubov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限维代数$\La$的右小有限维数有限等价于存在一个（可能无限维的）倾斜右$\La$-模$T_f$，其倾斜类为$\{ T_f \}^{\perp_\infty} = (\mathcal P ^{< \infty})^{\perp}$。利用此等价性，解释了Cummings关于由任意基本有限维代数$A$构造的三角矩阵代数$\tilde{A}$左右有限维数不对称性的近期结果，并在$A$具有有限右整体维数时确定了倾斜右$\tilde{A}$-模$T_f$的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用小有限维数与倾斜模之间的已知等价关系，解释Cummings发现的三角矩阵代数左右有限维数不对称这一令人惊讶的现象。

Method: 将右小有限维数有限性等价于存在特定倾斜模的理论结果应用于三角矩阵代数，通过分析倾斜模的结构来解读左右有限维数的不对称性。

Result: 在基础代数$A$具有有限右整体维数的情况下，确定了三角矩阵代数$\tilde{A}$的倾斜右模$T_f$的具体结构。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解三角矩阵代数上左右有限维数的不对称性提供了倾斜模视角的结构性解释。

Abstract: Finiteness of the right little finitistic dimension of a finite dimensional algebra $\La$ is known to be equivalent to existence of a (possibly infinite dimensional) tilting right $\La$-module $T_f$ whose tilting class is $\{ T_f \}^{\perp_\infty} = (\mathcal P ^{< \infty})^{\perp}$, \cite{AT}. We use this equivalence to interpret the recent surprising results of Cummings \cite{C} concerning the asymmetry of left and right finitistic dimensions of the triangular matrix algebras $\tilde{A}$ built from arbitrary basic finite dimensional algebras $A$. In particular, we determine the structure of the tilting right $\tilde{A}$-module $T_f$ in the case when the algebra $A$ has finite right global dimension.

</details>


### [154] [Spin characters of the alternating group which are proportional to linear characters in characteristic 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10609)
*Eoghan McDowell*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文分类了交错群中自旋与非自旋不可约特征标在2-模约化下成比例的情况，等价于分类了这类特征标对在奇数阶元素上成比例的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究交错群表示论中特征标的模约化行为，特别是自旋与非自旋特征标之间的比例关系，这类问题在模表示论和有限群理论中具有重要理论意义。

Method: 通过分析交错群的自旋与非自旋不可约特征标，研究它们在2-模约化下的性质，并将问题转化为对奇数阶元素上特征标值的比较。

Result: 给出了自旋与非自旋不可约特征标在2-模约化下成比例时的完全分类。

Conclusion: 本文完成了交错群中自旋与非自旋特征标在2-模约化下成比例情况的分类，为理解交错群的模表示结构提供了重要结果。

Abstract: We classify when a spin and a non-spin irreducible character of the alternating group have proportional 2-modular reductions. Equivalently, we classify when such a pair of characters are proportional on elements of odd order.

</details>


### [155] [Spin characters of symmetric and alternating groups which are proportional in characteristic 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10622)
*Matthew Fayers,Eoghan McDowell*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限群G的p-模分解矩阵的行成比例问题，解决了当G是交错群或对称群的双覆盖且p=3时自旋特征标（非提升特征标）的缺失情形，从而完整解决了对称群双覆盖的该问题，并发现与p=2情形有惊人相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定有限群G的两个普通不可约表示何时具有相同的p-模约化（即分解矩阵对应行相等，或两个特征标在p-正则共轭类上取值一致），并进一步推广到更一般的分解矩阵行成比例问题。

Method: 针对交错群或对称群双覆盖中p=3这一此前未解决的遗留情形进行专门分析，聚焦于自旋特征标（即非从被覆盖群提升而来的特征标）。

Result: 解决了p=3时自旋特征标的成比例行问题，完全解决了对称群双覆盖的分解矩阵行成比例问题，并发现与p=2情形的解法有惊人平行之处。

Conclusion: 彻底解决了对称群双覆盖中分解矩阵行成比例问题（p=3情形），补全了此前仅剩的缺失部分。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group and $p$ a prime. It is interesting to determine when two ordinary irreducible representations of $G$ have the same $p$-modular reduction; this is the same as saying that the corresponding rows of the decomposition matrix are equal, or that the characters of the two representations agree on $p$-regular conjugacy classes. In fact we consider the more general problem of asking when two rows of the decomposition matrix are proportional. In the case where $G$ is a double cover of the alternating or symmetric group, this problem has been solved except when $p=3$. Here we resolve the missing case for spin characters (i.e. characters which are not lifted from the covered group), which completely solves the problem for the double cover of the symmetric group. There are surprising parallels to our solution to the corresponding problem for $p=2$.

</details>


### [156] [Spherical modules and the Auslander--Gorenstein condition for Auslander--Yoneda algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10901)
*Finn Bauwens,Norihiro Hanihara,René Marczinzik,Marvin Plogmann,Jan Thomm*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限维代数A的Auslander-Yoneda代数，证明了其是Auslander-Gorenstein代数当且仅当每个不可分解左右A-模是球状的。由此引入球状代数概念，刻画了其由一对子范畴构成分裂挠对的特征，证明了表示有限型球状代数是直向的，完全分类了球状Nakayama代数，并展示了遗传代数的复制代数是球状的。最后给出了Venjakob关于Auslander正则代数问题的否定回答，但在每个不可分解左A-模是球状的条件下给出了肯定回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限维代数的Auslander-Yoneda代数的性质，探究其成为Auslander-Gorenstein代数的条件，并由此引入球状代数这一新概念，以解决相关领域的开放问题。

Method: 通过代数结构分析，利用Auslander-Bridger的球状模理论、分裂挠对、表示论和复制代数等方法进行刻画和分类。

Result: 建立了Auslander-Yoneda代数是Auslander-Gorenstein代数与每个不可分解模是球状的等价关系；给出了球状代数的子范畴刻画；证明了表示有限型球状代数是直向的；完全分类了球状Nakayama代数；证明了遗传代数的复制代数是球状的；对Venjakob问题给出了否定和部分肯定的回答。

Conclusion: 球状代数是一类结构良好的代数，其等价刻画和分类结果丰富了Auslander-Gorenstein代数和Auslander正则代数的理论，并为相关开放问题提供了解答。

Abstract: For a finite dimensional algebra $A$ of finite global dimension we study the Auslander--Yoneda algebra defined as the Yoneda algebra of the direct sum of all indecomposable $A$-modules. We show that the Auslander--Yoneda algebra is an Auslander--Gorenstein algebra if and only if every indecomposable left and right $A$-module is spherical in the sense of Auslander and Bridger. This motivates the study of spherical algebras defined by the condition that every indecomposable module is spherical. We characterize spherical algebras by a certain natural pair of subcategories being a split torsion pair. Moreover, we prove that representation-finite algebras which are spherical are directed and give a full classification of spherical Nakayama algebras. Furthermore, we show that replicated algebras of hereditary algebras are spherical. As a final application of the new notion of spherical algebras, we give a negative answer to a question of Venjakob on Auslander regular algebras in general, but show that there is a positive answer when assuming that every indecomposable left $A$-module is spherical.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [157] [Integral Formulations for two-dimensional Multi-Arcs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10016)
*Jose Pinto,Ruben Aylwin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了多弧（即多个开弧在连接点交汇形成的集合）上的Laplace方程Dirichlet和Neumann边值问题，建立了基于开弧Sobolev空间的函数空间框架和迹算子理论，通过边界积分方程实现了问题的适定性重构，并数值揭示了解密度在分支点处的奇异性行为（与多边形角点奇异性类似），同时发现Neumann问题中超奇异算子在经典Sobolev空间上未必可逆，解可能在分支点出现跳跃间断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多弧结构（如网络、裂纹集合、多材料界面等）在工程和物理中广泛出现，但这类几何上的边值问题在经典Sobolev空间框架下缺乏系统的理论分析，特别是分支点处的奇异性行为和Neumann问题的适定性尚未得到充分理解。

Method: 1) 以开弧上的Sobolev空间为基本构建块，构造多弧上的Sobolev空间尺度；2) 扩展迹算子的定义；3) 将边值问题重新表述为边界积分方程；4) 对Dirichlet问题建立适定的积分公式，并可用标准数值方法离散；5) 通过数值实验研究分支点处解密度的奇异性行为；6) 对Neumann问题分析超奇异算子的可逆性，并数值验证解的分支点跳跃间断现象。

Result: 1) Dirichlet问题存在适定的边界积分公式，可进行标准数值离散；2) 分支点处的奇异性与多边形域中的角点奇异性类似；3) Neumann问题的超奇异算子在经典Sobolev空间上不一定可逆；4) 解的密度在分支点可能出现跳跃间断。

Conclusion: 多弧上的Laplace边值问题具有丰富的奇异性结构，Dirichlet问题可通过边界积分方法可靠求解，而Neumann问题需要更精细的函数空间框架来处理超奇异算子的不可逆性和解的不连续性。

Abstract: We study the Laplace equation with Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions posed on multi-arcs, i.e., collections of open arcs meeting at junction points.
  We begin by introducing a scale of Sobolev spaces constructed using the Sobolev spaces on open arcs as main building block and extend the definition of trace operators.
  We reformulate the boundary value problems using boundary integral formulations. We then establish a well-posed integral formulation for the Dirichlet problem, which can be discretized using standard numerical methods.
  We further investigate the singular behavior of the solution densities at branch points through numerical experiments and observe that these singularities are comparable to the corner singularities arising in polygonal domains.
  For the Neumann problem, we show that the associated hypersingular operator is not necessarily invertible on classical Sobolev spaces and provide numerical evidence that solutions may develop jump discontinuities at branch points.

</details>


### [158] [WING: A Simple Windowed Nonorthogonalized Initial Guess Procedure for Repeated Matrix Solves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10132)
*David Wells,Matthew G. Knepley,Boyce E. Griffith*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出WING算法，通过跳过正交化和使用伪逆求解奇异法方程，降低了生成初始猜测的成本，有效加速了相同矩阵、相似右端项序列的线性系统求解，在流固耦合、地幔对流和地震模型等数值基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在求解具有相同矩阵和相似右端项的线性系统序列时，Krylov子空间方法需要大量迭代。精心选择的初始猜测可减少迭代次数和计算成本，但现有方法（如Fischer第二算法）生成初始猜测的计算开销较大，需要降低这一成本。

Method: 对Fischer第二算法进行改进得到WING算法：跳过正交化步骤，并采用伪逆求解可能奇异的法方程，从而以更低计算代价生成可接受的初始猜测。

Result: 在流固耦合、地幔对流和地震模型等数值基准测试中，WING算法有效降低了计算成本，尤其在粗相对容差条件下效果显著。

Conclusion: WING算法是一种低成本的初始猜测生成方法，适用于相同矩阵、相似右端项的线性系统序列求解，特别对粗容差问题优势明显。

Abstract: Many numerical methods require solution of a sequence of linear systems with the same matrix and similar right-hand sides. Krylov subspace methods are a common tool for solving such linear systems, and a carefully chosen initial guess for the solution can reduce the total number of iterations, and thereby the total computational cost, required for convergence to a specified numerical tolerance. This paper introduces the WING algorithm, a modification of Fischer's second algorithm, which lowers the cost of forming an acceptably close initial guess by skipping orthogonalization and solving the possibly singular normal equations with a pseudoinverse. We demonstrate the efficacy of the new algorithm, particularly for solving linear systems with coarse relative tolerances, with numerical benchmarks based on fluid-structure interaction, mantle convection, and earthquake models.

</details>


### [159] [Bridging Scales: Asymptotic Analysis and AI-Assisted Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10138)
*Xu Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文回顾了渐近分析中跨尺度模型桥接的通用符号结构（ansatz、代入、逐阶匹配、提取有效方程），通过辐射传输与玻尔兹曼方程的动力学-流体极限以及Frozen Gaussian Approximation与Dirac扩展的量子-经典极限两个典型案例，论证了这类推导因其重复性符号模式而天然适合AI辅助形式化，并指出精心组织的综述论文可作为未来AI辅助环境的数学种子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示渐近分析中不同领域（动力学-流体、量子-经典）推导背后共通的符号结构，论证其作为AI辅助形式化候选的合理性，为构建可重用、可验证的符号推理环境奠定基础。

Method: 通过两个代表性案例（辐射传输界面层的动力学-流体极限及玻尔兹曼方程的神经网络近似、Frozen Gaussian Approximation及其Dirac扩展的量子-经典极限）剖析渐近分析中的通用符号结构（ansatz、代入、逐阶匹配、有效方程提取），并据此论证该结构适合AI形式化。

Result: 识别出渐近分析中跨尺度推导的共性符号骨架，说明其可被组织、验证和复用，从而成为AI辅助形式化的天然候选。

Conclusion: 这类具有重复性符号模式的推导非常适合AI辅助形式化，精心撰写的综述论文不仅是对已有知识的回顾，更可作为未来AI辅助环境的数学种子。

Abstract: Asymptotic analysis is one of the classical tools for bridging models across scales. Behind many such derivations lies a common symbolic structure: an ansatz, a substitution, an order-by-order matching procedure, and the extraction of effective equations or interface conditions. This article revisits that structure through two representative bridges: the kinetic-to-fluid limit, illustrated by radiative transfer with interface layers and by neural-network approximations of Boltzmann equations, and the quantum-to-classical limit, illustrated by the Frozen Gaussian Approximation and its Dirac extension. We then explain why such derivations are natural candidates for AI-assisted formalization: their recurring symbolic structures can be organized, verified, and reused. In this sense, a carefully structured expository paper may serve not only as a review, but also as a mathematical seed for future AI-assisted environments.

</details>


### [160] [A Virtual Element Method for elliptic problems on trimmed background meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10218)
*L. Beirão da Veiga,C. Canuto,R. H. Nochetto,G. Vacca,M. Verani*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对二维分段C^2域通过准均匀多边形背景网格的切割问题，提出了一种基于凸包的简单程序生成少量多边形边界单元（含小边和大纵横比）的裁剪背景网格，并系统研究了各类几何构型的稳定性和逼近性，发展了各向异性单元和小切割单元的鲁棒稳定机制与插值估计，证明了所得虚拟单元法在H^1中的一致稳定性以及H^1和L^2中的最优阶正则性误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分段C^2域切割多边形背景网格时产生的边界单元几何复杂性（小边、大纵横比等）给虚拟单元法带来的稳定性和逼近性挑战，为处理移动域中的ALE公式问题提供理论基础。

Method: 基于凸包的简单程序生成多边形边界单元；对几何构型进行分类；推导各向异性单元和小切割单元的鲁棒稳定机制与插值估计；建立增强型虚拟单元的弱最大值原理；在裁剪背景网格上构建虚拟单元法并进行误差分析。

Result: 证明了该VEM在H^1中一致稳定，且得到H^1和L^2中的最优阶正则性误差估计。

Conclusion: 所提方法适用于移动域中ALE公式问题的处理，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: We consider a two-dimensional piecewise $C^2$ domain that cuts through a quasi-uniform fixed polygonal background mesh, for instance made of quadrilaterals. A simple procedure based on convex hulls gives rise to a rather small number of polygonal boundary elements of various shapes, including elements with small edges and large aspect ratios; this is the computational mesh for a virtual element method (VEM), a trimmed background mesh. We classify all possible geometric configurations and study their stability and approximability properties. This entails deriving robust stabilization mechanisms and interpolation estimates for anisotropic elements and elements with small cuts, as well as a weak maximum principle for enhanced virtual elements; these contributions have intrinsic interest for VEM theory on geometric flexibility. We prove that the resulting VEM is uniformly stable in $H^1$, and also show optimal order-regularity error estimates in $H^1$ and $L^2$. Insightful numerical experiments corroborate and complement our theory. The proposed method is suitable for treating ALE formulations of problems in moving domains.

</details>


### [161] [Higher-order Diffusion Sampling via Chebyshev Interpolation and Gauss--Seidel Iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10353)
*Bingyuan Wei,Meng Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Chebyshev-Gauss-Seidel的高阶扩散模型采样器，并建立了非渐近收敛保证，允许近似阶数随外迭代次数对数增长。在精确评分设置下，该采样器仅需约d^{1+o_T(1)}ε^{-1/K_1}次评分函数调用即可在总变差距离ε内逼近目标分布，且仅需目标分布的多项式二阶矩有界性，无需有界支撑或高阶平滑假设。数值实验验证了该方法的准确性-成本权衡改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶ODE求解器在扩散模型加速方面表现出经验潜力，但缺乏严格的理论保证，且现有高阶理论通常要求有界支撑条件，限制了其适用范围。

Method: 提出Chebyshev-Gauss-Seidel高阶采样器，建立非渐近收敛分析，允许近似阶数随外迭代次数对数增长，放宽了对目标分布的有界支撑假设，并证明了其对评分和雅可比估计误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在精确评分设置下，采样器至多需d^{1+o_T(1)}ε^{-1/K_1}次评分函数调用即可达到总变差距离ε的精度；该保证仅需目标分布多项式二阶矩有界，且对估计误差鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该工作为高阶扩散模型采样器提供了首个严格非渐近加速保证，放宽了理论假设条件，并验证了实际性能提升。

Abstract: Higher-order ODE solvers have shown strong empirical promise for accelerating diffusion models through the probability flow ODE, but rigorous non-asymptotic guarantees for such acceleration remain limited. In this paper, we develop a Chebyshev--Gauss--Seidel higher-order sampler and establish a non-asymptotic convergence guarantee that allows the approximation order to grow logarithmically with the number of outer iterations. In the exact-score setting, up to logarithmic factors, the proposed sampler requires at most \[ d^{1+o_T(1)}\varepsilon^{-1/K_1} \] score functions to approximate the target distribution on \(\mathbb{R}^d\) within total variation distance \(\varepsilon\), where \(o_T(1)\to 0\) as \(T\to\infty\) and \(K_1>0\) is a sufficiently large constant. The analysis assumes only a polynomial second-moment bound on the target distribution, thereby relaxing the bounded-support condition imposed in existing higher-order theory. Moreover, the guarantee is robust to score and Jacobian estimation errors and does not require higher-order smoothness assumptions on the score estimates. Numerical experiments on anisotropic Gaussian mixture benchmarks support the predicted improvement in the accuracy--cost tradeoff under finite score-evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [162] [Spectral and computational aspects of a regularized fractional Laplacian for non-local diffusion on graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10480)
*Alessandro Filippo,Mariarosa Mazza*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了一种结合标准拉普拉斯算子和分数阶拉普拉斯算子的正则化算子的扩散特性，证明其在加权和非加权图上均具有超扩散行为，并给出了保持渐近计算效率的构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶拉普拉斯算子虽然能建模图上非局部扩散，但与原始网络拓扑存在结构不一致性，需要恢复这种兼容性。

Method: 对正则化算子进行扩散特性的理论分析，证明其超扩散性质（不依赖于图是否加权），并设计保持分数阶拉普拉斯算子渐近计算代价的高效构造方法。

Result: 正则化算子在加权和非加权图上均表现出超扩散行为，且构造方法在计算效率上与分数阶拉普拉斯算子相当。

Conclusion: 该正则化算子能有效且计算可行地建模真实网络上的非局部扩散，克服了分数阶拉普拉斯算子的拓扑兼容性问题。

Abstract: The fractional Laplacian has been widely used to model non-local diffusion on graphs, allowing interactions that extend beyond immediate neighbors. However, it suffers from a structural inconsistency as it breaks compatibility with the topology of the original network. To address this issue, a combination of the standard and fractional Laplacians aimed at restoring compatibility while retaining the spectral richness of the fractional operator was recently proposed.
  In this work, we provide a thorough analysis of the diffusion properties of the resulting regularized operator. We prove that it yields superdiffusive behavior independently of whether the underlying graph is weighted or unweighted -a property not generally satisfied by other nonlocal Laplacian variants. An efficient construction of the regularized operator which preserves the same asymptotic computational cost of the fractional Laplacian is also given. Our numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and computational practicality of the regularized operator for modeling non-local diffusion on real-life networks.

</details>


### [163] [Multiscale modeling for problems with high contrast heterogeneous coefficients by the CEM-GMsFEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10524)
*Eric T. Chung,Yalchin Efendiev,Xingguang Jin,Wing Tat Leung,Changqing Ye*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文综述了约束能量最小化广义多尺度有限元方法（CEM-GMsFEM），用于求解具有高度异质、高对比度系数的椭圆型偏微分方程。通过谱辅助空间构造多尺度基函数，结合过采样策略实现局部化计算并保证指数误差衰减，给出了严格的误差估计，并通过数值模拟验证了指数衰减性质，同时讨论了该方法的最新应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有高度异质性和高对比度系数的椭圆型偏微分方程数值求解中，传统方法计算成本过高的问题，需发展一种既能保持精度又能降低计算复杂度的多尺度方法。

Method: 采用谱辅助空间构造多尺度基函数，结合过采样策略实现局部化计算，通过约束能量最小化保证基函数的指数衰减性质。

Result: 数值模拟验证了多尺度基函数的指数衰减性质，理论上证明了该方法的最优收敛性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CEM-GMsFEM是一种高效、鲁棒的多尺度方法，在保持指数误差衰减的同时实现了局部化计算，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: This review paper provides a comprehensive overview of the Constrained Energy Minimizing Generalized Multiscale Finite Element Method (CEM-GMsFEM) for solving elliptic PDEs characterized by highly heterogeneous, high-contrast coefficients. We detail the construction of multiscale basis functions via spectral auxiliary spaces, combined with an oversampling strategy that enables localized computations and guarantees exponential error decay. Rigorous error estimates are outlined for reference to confirm the method's optimal convergence and robustness. Numerical simulations are provided to verify the exponential decay property of the multiscale basis functions. Additionally, we discuss and comment several up-to-date applications of CEM-GMsFEMs.

</details>


### [164] [Nonlocal modeling of opinion alignment and environmental feedback: Spatial aggregation and non-consensus patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10599)
*Rui Wang,Yunfeng Xiong,Zhengru Zhang,Xiaofei Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个耦合非局部对齐与注意力场的空间意见动力学模型，通过推导非局部平流-交叉扩散系统，揭示了注意力介导反馈如何扩大聚类参数区间，从而促进空间异质性和非共识模式的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代社会信息环境中，公众舆论的形成受到社会从众性与信息暴露之间相互作用的塑造。社会互动促进意见对齐，而异质性可见性和选择性暴露可能强化局部一致（回声室效应）。现有模型缺乏对注意力反馈如何影响空间异质性意见活动和非共识模式的宏观描述。

Method: 从基于有界信任交互和偏置随机游走的智能体跳跃机制出发，形式化推导出非局部平流-交叉扩散系统；通过均匀平衡态的线性稳定性分析刻画从空间均匀共识态到非共识聚类态的转变；采用保结构IMEX谱方法进行数值模拟。

Result: 注意力介导反馈对失稳阈值有显式修正，扩大了聚类发生的参数区间，从而促进持久空间异质性和非共识模式的形成。数值模拟支持理论预测并量化了聚合现象。

Conclusion: 非局部对齐与环境反馈共同塑造了非共识模式的空间特征，为理解回声室效应和舆论极化提供了宏观连续描述框架。

Abstract: The formation of public opinion in modern information environments is shaped by the interplay between social conformity and information exposure. While social interactions promote opinion alignment, heterogeneous visibility and selective exposure may reinforce local agreement, a mechanism commonly associated with the echo chamber effects. To describe how such reinforcement influences spatially heterogeneous opinion activity and non-consensus patterns, we propose a spatial opinion dynamics model with attention-mediated feedback. The model couples nonlocal alignment with an evolving attention field and captures a self-reinforcing mechanism in which regions of high opinion activity attract greater visibility. Starting from agent-based jump mechanism inspired by bounded confidence interactions and biased random walks induced by environments, we formally derive a nonlocal advection-cross-diffusion system, where opinion transport is driven by nonlocal conformity and modulated by attention-dependent redistribution. We characterize the transition from spatially homogeneous consensus states to non-consensus clustered regimes through linear stability analysis of the homogeneous equilibrium. The results show that attention-mediated feedback has an explicit correction on the instability threshold and enlarges the parameter regime in which clustering occurs, thereby promoting persistent spatial heterogeneity and non-consensus patterns. Numerical simulations based on a structure-preserving IMEX spectral method support the theoretical predictions and quantify the resulting aggregation phenomena. These findings provide a macroscopic description of how nonlocal alignment and environmental feedback jointly shape spatial signatures of non-consensus patterns.

</details>


### [165] [Dmsh: A Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for All-Quad Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10601)
*Anirudh Kalyan,Cosmin Anitescu,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk,Somdatta Goswami,Sundararajan Natarajan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出Dmsh，首个全自动强化学习管道，通过三个协调智能体（拓扑简化、几何正则化、网格生成）将几何分解与四边形网格生成统一在单一学习框架中，采用参数化SAC架构与课程学习策略，递归分解实现并行化全局一致全四边形网格生成，在多项基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任意几何体生成高质量网格仍是计算工程中的根本瓶颈，现有方法依赖启发式调参和半手动流程，缺乏自动化且鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 将网格生成问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，采用参数化Soft Actor-Critic架构配合解耦评论家网络处理混合离散-连续动作空间；设计三个协调智能体分别处理拓扑简化、几何正则化和网格生成；引入课程学习策略从简单域渐进扩展到复杂几何。

Result: 在广泛基准测试中，Dmsh在自动化程度、鲁棒性和网格质量方面一致优于现有方法，递归分解支持子区域并行网格化，无需后处理即可生成全局一致的全四边形网格。

Conclusion: Dmsh建立了学习驱动网格生成的新范式，首次实现了从几何分解到四边形网格生成的端到端全自动化流水线。

Abstract: Generating high-quality meshes for arbitrary geometries remains a fundamental bottleneck in computational engineering, often demanding heuristic tuning and semi-manual workflows. In this paper, we introduce Dmsh, a first fully automated reinforcement learning pipeline that unifies geometric decomposition and quadrilateral mesh generation within a single learning-based framework. Dmsh decomposes the problem through three coordinated agents handling topology simplification, geometric regularization, and mesh generation. The meshing process is formulated as a Markov Decision Process and solved using a parametric Soft Actor-Critic architecture with decoupled critics, enabling efficient exploration of a hybrid discrete-continuous action space. A curriculum learning strategy ensures scalability from simple domains to highly complex geometries, suppressing seed variance. By design, the recursive decomposition enables parallel meshing of subregions, yielding globally conforming all-quadrilateral meshes without post hoc correction. Across a wide range of benchmarks, Dmsh consistently outperforms existing methods in automation, robustness, and mesh quality, establishing a new paradigm for learning-based mesh generation.

</details>


### [166] [On the representation for stochastic graph delay propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11086)
*Shibo Zeng,Weiguo Gao,Yingzhou Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文利用Hermite展开逼近独立随机变量和与最大值的分布，采用三段式表示（左右尾部分别用Hermite函数组合，中间段用分段多项式），结合严格收敛性分析，并应用于图延迟传播问题，数值实验表明在分位数精度上显著优于经典高斯模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对独立随机变量和与最大值的分布逼近问题，传统高斯模型精度有限，需要更精确且具有理论保证的近似方法。

Method: 使用Hermite展开对分布进行三段式建模：左右尾部分别用Hermite函数组合逼近，中间段用分段多项式近似；基于经典结果建立了L²和逐点收敛性保证；并提出了将该模型应用于图延迟传播问题的算法框架。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提模型在捕捉分位数值方面精度显著高于经典高斯模型，与蒙特卡洛模拟结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 提出的Hermite展开三段式分布逼近方法具有理论收敛保证和实际高精度，尤其在图延迟传播等涉及和与最大操作的随机问题中优于传统方法。

Abstract: In this work, we utilize the Hermite expansion to approximate the distributions of the sum and maximum of independent random variables. We model distributions with a three-segment representation, where the left and right tails are respectively modeled as combinations of Hermite functions, and the intermediate segment is approximated by piecewise polynomials. This approximation admits rigorous $L^2$- and pointwise convergence properties supported by classical results. We develop an algorithmic framework for applying our model to the graph delay propagation problem, where sum and max operations are performed on the proposed model structure. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our model can capture the quantile values with high accuracy compared to Monte Carlo simulation results, significantly outperforming classical Gaussian-based models.

</details>

### [167] [Modeling pest dynamics in trap cropping to improve yield: the effects of attraction, retention, and land allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10605)
*Matthew H Holden*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过产量最大化模型，揭示了诱集作物防控效果关键取决于害虫对诱集植物的吸引强度及其离开频率，当诱集作物上的害虫扩散率降至主栽作物的四分之一时，最优诱集面积可从20-30%降至约5%，使诱集作物从不可行变为可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诱集作物能通过吸引害虫保护主栽作物，但害虫从诱集作物返回主栽作物会削弱其保护效果。现有研究缺乏对害虫回流机制及其对诱集作物经济可行性的定量理解。

Method: 建立简单的产量最大化模型，将害虫对诱集植物的吸引强度、离开频率以及诱集面积占比纳入统一框架，分析不同参数组合下的最优诱集覆盖比例。

Result: 当诱集作物与主栽作物上的害虫扩散率相同时，最优诱集面积需超过20-30%；当诱集作物上的扩散率降至主栽作物的1/4时，最优诱集面积降至约5%。

Conclusion: 诱集作物的可行性高度依赖害虫滞留能力，通过植物选择或靶向干预减少害虫从诱集植物上的扩散，可大幅降低所需诱集面积，使诱集作物成为可持续害虫管理的可靠手段。

Abstract: Trap crops reduce damage to a cash (main) crop by attracting pests away from it. Yet this protection is weakened when pests disperse back into the cash crop. In this paper, we focus on the importance of preventing this backflow, showing that effective trap cropping depends jointly on how strongly pests are attracted to trap plants and how rarely they leave them. Together with the proportion of the field devoted to trap plants, these processes determine both the efficacy and feasibility of trap cropping at commercial scales. We formalise this relationship using a simple yield-maximisation framework, in which growers weigh pest suppression benefits against the land sacrificed to trap plants. The model shows that when dispersal from trap plants equals that from the cash crop, optimal trap coverage can exceed 20 to 30 percent of the landscape, levels rarely acceptable to growers. However, reducing pest dispersal off trap plants to just one-quarter of cash crop dispersal lowers the optimal required trap area to approximately 5 percent, transforming trap cropping from impractical to feasible. Understanding these relationships can guide trap-cropping design, from plant choice to targeted interventions that reduce pest movement, to minimise damage, maximise yield, and make trap cropping a reliable component of sustainable pest management.

</details>


### [168] [Chaos and stability in the marine trophic network: the importance of interactions over complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10809)
*Ilaria Cunico,Guido Occhipinti,Gregor Fussmann,Paolo Lazzari*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究通过包含微生物环的海洋营养网络模型，发现较长的营养链和更多的消费者增加系统混沌性，而杂食性相互作用促进稳定性。研究表明相互作用和反馈而非复杂性是稳定性的关键驱动因素，且许多配置表现出高比例的混沌行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界复杂网络的动态特性对预测性、恢复力和生态系统管理至关重要，但生态网络中稳定性与复杂性的关系仍存在争议，因此需要探究复杂海洋营养网络是否表现出稳定、周期或混沌动态。

Method: 构建包含微生物环（细菌将碎屑循环为营养物）的营养网络模型，包含1-3个初级生产者、1-2个消费者和最多3个营养级的捕食者；通过关闭种间捕食链接和变化高维参数空间进行数值模拟。

Result: (i)较长营养链和(ii)更多消费者增加系统混沌性；(iii)杂食性相互作用促进稳定性；多数配置表现出高比例混沌行为；负反馈与正反馈的平衡对系统收敛到稳态起关键作用。

Conclusion: 相互作用和反馈而非复杂性是稳定性的关键驱动因素，不存在明确的稳定性-复杂性关系，而是存在稳定性-相互作用依赖性；混沌动态对预测性和生态系统管理具有潜在影响。

Abstract: Understanding the dynamics of real world complex networks is crucial for assessing their predictability, resilience, and improving ecosystem management, especially in the context of climate change. The relationship between stability and complexity in ecological networks is still debated in the literature. In this modeling study, we investigate whether a complex marine trophic network, characterized by multiple trophic interactions and environmental constraints, exhibits predominantly stable, periodic or chaotic dynamics. We incorporate the microbial loop into a trophic network model, which includes one to three primary producers, one or two consumers, and up to three trophic levels of predators. The microbial loop is a key process in which bacteria recycle detritus from higher trophic levels into nutrients available for the growth of primary producers, ensuring mass conservation within the system. We perform numerical simulations to investigate the dynamic behavior of the network, exploring several configurations by turning off predator prey links between species and varying the high dimensional parameter space. Our results show that (i) longer trophic chains and (ii) a higher number of consumers increase system chaoticity, whereas (iii) omnivorous interactions promote stability. Notably, many of the configurations exhibit high percentages of chaotic behavior. Feedback loop analysis suggests that the balance between negative and positive interactions plays a key role in the convergence of the system toward a steady state. This study shows that interactions and feedback, rather than complexity, are key drivers of stability, pointing to the absence of a clear stability complexity relationship and instead highlighting a stability interaction dependence. Chaotic dynamics may also play an important role, with potential implications for predictability and ecosystem management.

</details>
